### [138] [Scaling LLM Knowledge Boundaries via Distribution-Optimized Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23271)
*Songze Li,Yarong Lan,Zhongpu Bo,Zhaoyang Wang,Zhiqiang Liu,Yuan Yuan,Chengtao Gan,Menghao Qian,Enpei Niu,Xiaoke Guo,Yuanxiang Liu,Zhaoyan Gong,Xiangjin Hu,Liangyurui Liu,Jingdian Lu,Lei Liang,Jun Zhou,Huajun Chen,Wen Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出KDoS框架，通过知识密度驱动合成数据的分布优化，发现存在最优知识分布能最大化LLM知识边界扩展，且该分布在多种模型和规模下稳定有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前知识注入方法仅基于预设token数量或固定数据比例进行合成，缺乏对知识分布的感知，导致某些领域稀疏而其他领域冗余，限制了LLM知识边界的扩展。

Method: 提出KDoS（知识分布优化合成）框架，引入知识密度概念，通过三阶段反馈机制驱动合成过程，从盲目生成转向分布优化合成。基于Wikipedia构建不同知识分布的合成数据，在0.6B至16B参数模型（Qwen、Ling、LLaMA）和1B至5B token数据规模上进行实验。

Result: （1）存在最优知识分布能一致性地最大化知识边界扩展；（2）该分布在多种骨干网络和规模下保持稳定；（3）KDoS在六个知识基准上优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 从分布视角重新审视知识注入问题，证实了最优知识分布的存在性及稳定性，为合成数据驱动的知识注入提供了新视角和实用框架。

Abstract: Knowledge injection via synthetic data is crucial for enhancing Large Language Models (LLMs). However, current synthesis methods simply stop at preset token counts or fixed data ratios, lacking awareness of knowledge distribution. This results in some domains being sparse while others are redundant, limiting LLM knowledge boundaries. We revisit knowledge injection from a distribution perspective and hypothesize that an optimal knowledge distribution exists to maximize knowledge boundary expansion. We propose KDoS (Knowledge Distribution-optimized Synthesis), a framework that introduces knowledge density to drive synthesis through a three-stage feedback mechanism, shifting from blind generation to distribution-optimized synthesis. We construct Wikipedia-based synthetic data with varying knowledge distributions and conduct experiments on models from 0.6B to 16B (Qwen, Ling, LLaMA) and data scales from 1B to 5B tokens. Our key findings are: (1) an optimal knowledge distribution consistently maximizes boundary expansion; (2) this distribution is stable across backbones and scales; (3) KDoS outperforms baselines across six knowledge benchmarks. Our work offers a new perspective and practical framework for synthetic data-driven knowledge injection.

</details>


### [139] [Towards Root Memories: Benchmarking and Enhancing Implicit Logical Memory Retrieval for Personalized LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23283)
*Hongxun Ding,Xiang Yu,Chengbing Wang,Jianfei Xiao,Keqin Bao,Wenjie Wang,Xiangnan He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对现有大语言模型记忆系统中仅依赖语义相似度检索而忽略隐式逻辑记忆的问题，构建了首个面向长对话场景的隐式逻辑记忆检索基准IMLogic，并提出结构化决策保持的根记忆表示及RootMem框架，通过LLM路由器激活逻辑相关记忆，显著提升了检索准确率和下游任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM记忆系统的检索方法主要依赖语义相似度，可能遗漏语义重叠少但逻辑关键的记忆，而当前缺乏评估该问题的基准。

Method: 构建IMLogic基准数据集；提出根记忆（root memory）——一种结构化、保持决策信息的表示方法；提出RootMem框架，先蒸馏原始历史为结构化根记忆，再通过基于LLM的路由器激活逻辑相关记忆，与语义检索互补。

Result: RootMem显著优于最强检索基线，并能持续提升现有记忆代理的准确率。

Conclusion: 通过引入隐式逻辑记忆检索机制，可以有效弥补纯语义检索的不足，提升LLM个性化记忆系统的推理能力。

Abstract: Memory systems are essential for personalized Large Language Models (LLMs). However, existing retrieval methods in these systems primarily rely on semantic similarity, potentially missing logically critical memories with limited semantic overlap. Current benchmarks remain inadequate for evaluating this problem. To address this gap, we construct IMLogic, the first high-quality benchmark targeting implicit logical memory retrieval in long-dialogue scenarios. Motivated by this challenge, we introduce root memory, a structured, decision-preserving representation that distills reusable personalized logic from long-term user histories. We then propose RootMem, a plug-and-play framework that first distills raw histories into structured root memories and then uses an LLM-based router to activate logically relevant ones, complementing semantic retrieval with personalized decision logic. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RootMem significantly outperforms the strongest retrieval baselines and consistently boosts the accuracy of existing memory agents. Our benchmark and codes will be available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/IMLogic-DBB3.

</details>


### [140] [On the Effect of Segmentation Width and Cluster Size on Speech Resynthesis and Continuation in Generative Spoken Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23285)
*Shunsuke Kando,Wataru Nakata,Shinnosuke Takamichi,Yusuke Miyao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了生成式口语语言建模（GSLM）在不同比特率离散语音表示下的语音合成与延续性能，发现较低比特率即可生成可理解且自然的语音，同时LLM指标与人类主观评分的相关性仍较低，需更稳定的自动评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究GSLM在使用不同比特率离散语音表示时的语音合成与延续性能，验证传统GSLM设置是否冗余，并评估现有自动评价指标的有效性。

Method: 使用固定宽度分割语音表示，训练多种聚类大小的K-means模型以生成不同比特率设置，在GSLM框架下进行语音合成和延续任务的对比实验。

Result: 较低比特率下即可合成可理解且自然的语音；语音延续质量在低比特率下保持稳定；LLM指标与人类主观评分的相关性仍然较低。

Conclusion: 传统GSLM的高比特率设置对于有效语音生成可能是冗余的，当前自动评价方法仍不稳定，需要更完善的评估指标。

Abstract: Generative Spoken Language Modeling (GSLM) enables text-free speech modeling by training language models (LMs) using discrete speech representations instead of textual transcription. In this paper, we investigate the performance of GSLM on speech synthesis and continuation using discrete speech representations with varying bitrates. We segment speech representations with fixed widths and train K-means models in multiple cluster sizes, resulting in various bitrate settings. We demonstrate that intelligible and natural speech can be synthesized at lower bitrate settings than the baseline. Furthermore, speech continuation quality remains stable at lower bitrates across multiple metrics, suggesting that the conventional GSLM setting may be redundant for effective speech generation. Although LLM-based metrics show higher correlation with human subjective score than conventional metrics, it remains low, highlighting the need for more stable automatic evaluation methods.

</details>


### [141] [The Anatomy of the CTC Oracle Gap: Acoustic Exhaustion and Linguistic Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23306)
*Ivan Novosad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统证明了CTC内部评分在N-best假设选择中存在信息瓶颈：CTC声学置信度与语言合理性之间的瓶颈是语言性的而非声学性的。11种CTC内部策略均无显著WER改进，且其判别能力随G增大衰减53%。通过外部语言模型（RoBERTa PLL）的MBR解码可突破该瓶颈，在多个架构和数据集上取得一致提升。同时发现标准MWER训练在近收敛检查点会因信号过弱而崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究CTC内部评分在N-best假设选择中的局限性，定位声学置信度与语言合理性之间的信息瓶颈，并验证该瓶颈的性质（声学vs语言）。

Method: 1) 系统比较11种CTC内部及声学特征评分策略在N-best重打分上的效果；2) 通过Spearman相关系数分析CTC评分随beam size G变化的判别能力衰减；3) 引入基于RoBERTa伪对数似然(PLL)的MBR解码作为外部语言信息；4) 在多个架构和数据集上进行验证；5) 分析MWER训练的Rao-Blackwellized REINFORCE性质及近收敛失败原因。

Result: CTC内部评分无显著改进（p>0.05）；CTC的Spearman ρ从G=4的-0.574降至G=128的-0.270（衰减53%）；MBR-CER解码（RoBERTa PLL, τ=10, G=128）在test-other上达5.42% WER（greedy 5.96%, 相对降9.0%, p<0.0001）；RoBERTa ρ仅衰减21%；11/13条件显著提升；MWER训练在近收敛检查点崩溃（+6.18~+8.90 pp WER）。

Conclusion: CTC内部表示的判别能力已饱和，无法通过声学信号重组缩小oracle gap；信息瓶颈本质是语言性的，需引入外部语言模型突破；MWER训练在梯度信号极弱时失败，需注意训练检查点的选择。

Abstract: We study the limits of CTC-internal scoring for N-best hypothesis selection and locate the information bottleneck separating acoustic confidence from linguistic plausibility. Eleven CTC-internal and acoustic-feature scoring strategies produce no statistically significant WER improvement over greedy decoding on LibriSpeech dev-other at G=16 (all p > 0.05). The exhaustion is systematic: CTC's Spearman $ρ$ between hypothesis score and per-utterance WER degrades from -0.574 at G=4 to -0.270 at G=128, a 53% loss driven by blank-path proliferation. This establishes that the discriminative capacity of CTC-internal representations is saturated: no recombination of acoustic signals can close the oracle gap.
  Confirming that the bottleneck is linguistic, not acoustic, external linguistic information introduced via MBR decoding breaks through it. MBR-CER decoding with a RoBERTa pseudo-log-likelihood (PLL) posterior ($τ$=10, G=128) achieves 5.42% WER on held-out LibriSpeech test-other (greedy 5.96%, $Δ$=-0.535 pp, p<0.0001, 9.0% relative). RoBERTa PLL $ρ$ degrades only 21% over the same range, retaining discriminating power where CTC loses it. Applied without retuning across two Zipformer architectures, three domains (LibriSpeech, TED-LIUM 3, VoxPopuli), and four MUSAN noise levels, the recipe gives significant gains in 11 of 13 conditions.
  On the training side, standard MWER training via the CTC forward-backward algorithm implements Rao-Blackwellized REINFORCE at the output projection (variance about 3x below Viterbi). Yet sequence-level fine-tuning fails at near-converged checkpoints: all four MWER configurations on CR-CTC collapse (+6.18 to +8.90 pp WER), as a training oracle gap of 0.007 pp provides no usable reward signal.

</details>


### [142] [Tmax: A simple recipe for terminal agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23321)
*Hamish Ivison,Junjie Oscar Yin,Rulin Shao,Teng Xiao,Nathan Lambert,Hannaneh Hajishirzi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Tmax 是目前最强的开源终端智能体 RL 训练方案，仅用 9B 参数就在 Terminal-Bench 2.0 上达到 27% 的准确率，大幅超越此前更大规模的模型。该工作通过新颖的数据分类体系（结合难度控制、角色设定和验证器多样化）低成本生成了大量终端环境数据，并开源了此前最大（2.5 倍以上）的终端智能体数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于终端操作的智能体已成为语言模型最流行的下游应用，但学术界在 RL 训练这些模型方面的工作相对较少，原因包括缺乏困难的基准测试、数据不足以及缺少简单的基线方案。

Method: 提出 Tmax 配方：1）使用新颖的分类体系生成数据，结合难度控制、角色设定和验证器多样化；2）低成本生成大量终端环境数据用于 RL 和 SFT 训练；3）采用简单的、仅基于结果的 RL 训练方案。

Result: 在 Terminal-Bench 2.0 上仅用 9B 参数即达到 27% 的准确率，优于此前更大规模模型；开源数据集规模是此前已发布终端智能体数据集的 2.5 倍以上。

Conclusion: Tmax 为未来终端智能体的开放学术研究提供了强基线，通过开源数据、模型和代码推动了该领域的发展。

Abstract: Terminal-using agents have quickly become the most popular downstream application of language models (LMs). Despite their prevalence, relatively little academic work has examined RL-based training of these models, likely due to difficult benchmarks, a lack of data, and a lack of simple baseline recipes. We present Tmax, the strongest open RL recipe for terminal agents to date, bringing open data recipes closer to the frontier. While simple, our recipe achieves 27\% on Terminal-Bench 2.0 with only 9B parameters, outperforming much larger models from prior work. Concretely, we generate data using a novel taxonomy, combining difficulty control, personas, and verifier diversification, which allows us to cheaply generate large amounts of terminal environments for RL and SFT training. We open-source our terminal dataset, which is over 2.5x larger than previously released terminal-agent datasets. We then train open-weight models using RL with our data, using a simple, outcome-only recipe. We release our data, models, and code as a strong baseline for future open academic work on terminal agents at https://github.com/hamishivi/tmax.

</details>


### [143] [WaveDetect: Robust Framework for Machine-Generated Text Detection via Wavelet Transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23336)
*Zhichen Liu,Kaitong Qin,Linhan He,Yang Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于连续小波变换的频域分析方法，将LLM生成文本检测转化为信号处理任务，通过提取文本的"频谱指纹"来检测机器生成文本，在RAID、EvoBench和Domain-Shift基准上达到SOTA，并对对抗攻击、跨域偏移和模型演化具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成文本检测器在面对对抗扰动、跨域偏移和基础模型快速时间演化三大挑战时表现脆弱；仅依赖表层语义伪影的检测方法已不可靠。

Method: 将LLM生成文本建模为概率信号，应用可微连续小波变换（CWT）将其转换为可学习的频谱表示，从而揭示机器生成文本在时域中不可见的"频谱指纹"。

Result: 在RAID、EvoBench和Domain-Shift三个数据集上取得SOTA性能，对复杂攻击具有鲁棒性，能够泛化到分布外主题和未见过的演化LLM。

Conclusion: 频谱分析是LLM生成文本检测的有效新范式，从频域角度揭示了机器生成文本的本质特征。

Abstract: As Large Language Models asymptotically approach human-level fluency in natural language generation, solely relying on surface-level semantic artifacts for detecting LLM-generated texts has become increasingly precarious. Existing detectors often falter when facing three critical challenges: adversarial perturbations, cross-domain shifts, and the rapid temporal evolution of the foundation model. To address these issues, we propose \wavedetect, a novel framework that reformulates text detection as a signal processing task within the time-frequency domain. Unlike previous methods that analyze static token probability distributions, \wavedetect models the generated output as a probability signal, upon which a differentiable Continuous Wavelet Transform is applied to convert them into learnable spectral representations. This process reveals the intrinsic ``spectral fingerprints'' in machine-generated texts--patterns that remain invisible in time domain. Comprehensive evaluations on three well-curated datasets (RAID, EvoBench, and Domain-Shift) show that our method achieves a new state-of-the-art. It not only achieves superior accuracy but also exhibits remarkable robustness against sophisticated attacks, generalization across out-of-distribution topics and unseen evolving LLMs. Our results validate the efficacy of spectral analysis as a promising paradigm for LLM-generated texts detection.

</details>


### [144] [Measuring & Mitigating Over-Alignment for LLMs in Multilingual Criminal Law Courts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23375)
*Arthur Wuhrmann,Gaetan Stein,Daniel Brunner,Andrei Kucharavy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对刑事法律场景下LLM的过度对齐（over-alignment）问题，构建了多语言基准TF-RefusalBench（含5,200条提示），系统评估了模型在翻译和摘要任务中的拒答行为，并发现abliteration（拒绝方向消融）可在不影响任务性能的前提下有效消除拒答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然LLM在法律领域已有窄范围应用（如瑞士联邦最高法院使用本地小模型进行翻译和摘要），但在刑事法律场景中，由于判决和案件材料常包含暴力、性犯罪等敏感内容，模型的安全护栏（over-alignment）会频繁触发拒答和免责声明，严重影响司法人员的正常工作。

Method: 1) 构建TF-RefusalBench多语言基准数据集，包含法语、德语、意大利语和英语共5,200条提示，涵盖常见任务提示和易触发拒答的文本段落；2) 利用该基准评估over-alignment的多维度影响因素（模型、提示语言、文本语言）；3) 评估两种缓解策略：提示工程（prompting）和abliteration（拒绝方向消融）。

Result: 1) Over-alignment是多维现象，受模型、提示语言和文本语言共同影响；2) 其影响不能仅从过度拒答角度评估，免责声明还会损害任务忠实度（task faithfulness）；3) Abliteration能完全消除拒答，且对任务性能影响极小，优于提示工程方法。

Conclusion: 在刑事法律场景中，abliteration（拒绝方向消融）是比提示工程更有效的缓解over-alignment的方案，可在保持任务性能的同时消除不当拒答。

Abstract: While the wider applicability of LLMs in the legal field is currently debated due to their reliability and the gravity of any errors, narrow uses with well-understood and mitigated risks have emerged. Notably the Swiss Federal Supreme Court uses small on-premises models for tentative translations and short-passage summarization across the four official languages. However, such usage is challenging in the context of Criminal Law. Since rulings and cases employees work on routinely can contain detailed descriptions of violent and sexual offenses, their legitimate work is compromised by refusals and disclaimers due to the activation of model guardrails (over-alignment).
  To measure this phenomenon, we introduce TF-RefusalBench, a multilingual benchmark for criminal-law translation and summarization derived from public Swiss Supreme Court rulings. TF-RefusalBench contains 5,200 total prompts across French, German, Italian, and English, corresponding to common task prompts and passages likely to trigger refusal. We then use TF-RefusalBench to show that over-alignment is a multifaceted phenomenon, influenced by the model and the prompt and text languages being processed, and that its impact cannot be evaluated solely from an over-refusal perspective, given the disclaimer's impact on task faithfulness. Finally, we evaluate approaches to enable on-premises LLMs for Criminal Law Tasks, demonstrating that while prompting can be effective, abliteration (refusal directions ablation) eliminates refusal with minimal impact on task performance.

</details>


### [145] [Energy-Based Transformers as Predictors of Reading Difficulty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23382)
*Jakub Dotlacil,Ece Takmaz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次探索基于能量的变压器（Energy-Based Transformer）度量在计算心理语言学中的应用，发现能量度量（energy measure）能稳健预测阅读时间，在三类语料库中均超越surprisal的解释力；在关系从句处理实验中，单层能量可捕捉主/宾语不对称性，并可能统一替代之前需要的多个互补度量（如surprisal和注意力熵）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将能量基变压器模型引入计算心理语言学，为句子处理中的认知负荷预测提供与Hopfield网络和稠密关联记忆模型相联系的统一理论框架，探索是否存在一个单一度量可替代以往多个互补度量。

Method: 采用能量基变压器（energy-based transformer），提取其能量度量作为预测因子；在Natural Stories、UCL眼动追踪、UCL自步阅读三个语料库上比较能量度量与surprisal的预测能力；通过关系从句处理控制实验检验单层能量对主/宾语不对称性的捕捉能力。

Result: 能量度量在三个阅读时间语料库中均为稳健预测因子，显著超越surprisal的拟合效果；在关系从句处理中，单层能量可捕捉已知的主/宾语不对称性；证据表明能量度量可吸收本应由attention entropy和surprisal分别解释的效应。

Conclusion: 能量度量可能作为一个统一的预测指标替代之前需要的多个互补度量（如surprisal和注意力熵），为句子加工研究提供了与关联记忆理论相连接的新视角。

Abstract: Transformer language models have become established tools for modeling human sentence processing, with measures such as surprisal and attention entropy serving as effective predictors of reading difficulty that together capture complementary aspects of processing load. Here, we explore a related class of transformer models: energy-based transformers, which provide a principled formal link to associative memory models, bringing processing research into direct contact with the broader literature on Hopfield networks and dense associative memory. To our knowledge, this is the first exploration of an energy-based transformer measure in computational psycholinguistics. Across reading-time corpora (Natural Stories, UCL eye-tracking, UCL self-paced reading), the energy measure is a robust predictor of reading times, providing significant fit beyond surprisal in all three. In a controlled experiment on relative clause processing, energy at a single layer captures the well-known object/subject asymmetry. We find evidence that it subsumes effects attributable to both attention entropy and surprisal, suggesting that energy may serve as a single unified predictor where multiple complementary measures have previously been required.

</details>


### [146] [Self-Stigma Is Not a Monolith, but Generic Empathy Is: Persona-Conditioned LLM Support for People Who Use Drugs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23387)
*Layla Bouzoubaa,Rezvaneh Rezapour*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种人格感知的LLM支持方法，通过对Reddit上1,174名自我污名表达者的潜在特征分析，识别出四种人格类型。基于序列贝叶斯和递归神经网络的分类器在有限发帖历史中显著优于基准模型（macro-F1=0.74）。然而，临床专家的评估显示，人格匹配的回应虽能实现目标行为转变，但整体上专家更偏好通用共情回应，提示整体共情判断与临床对齐的回应设计可能存在冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物使用者（PWUD）中的自我污名会预测治疗回避和脱离，但现有对话系统通常将自我污名表达视为单一信号，缺乏个性化处理能力。

Method: 三阶段概念验证研究：1）对Reddit上1,174名自我污名表达者进行潜在特征分析（LPA），得出四种人格类型；2）使用序列贝叶斯和递归神经网络分类器从有限发帖历史中恢复人格类型；3）邀请八位临床专家评估三种当代LLM（含人格匹配 vs. 人格中性对照）的回应效果。

Result: 人格分类器在30条帖子时达到macro-F1=0.74，显著优于批量学习和少样本LLM基线；人格匹配回应成功实现目标行为转变，但临床专家整体上更偏好人格中性的通用共情回应。

Conclusion: 整体共情判断与临床对齐的回应设计可能相互冲突，评估LLM污名支持需要能够分解这两者的评估体系。

Abstract: Self-stigma predicts treatment avoidance and disengagement among people who use drugs (PWUD), yet conversational systems aiming to provide support typically treat self-stigma expression as a uniform signal. We present a three-phase, proof-of-concept study of a persona-aware approach to LLM support. Latent Profile Analysis (LPA) on indicator-level features from 1,174 self-stigma expressors on Reddit yields a four-persona typology validated against held-out behavioral and linguistic features. Sequential Bayesian and recurrent neural classifiers recover these personas from limited posting histories, substantially outperforming batch and few-shot LLM baselines (macro-F1 = 0.74 at 30 posts). Evaluation by eight clinical experts across three contemporary LLMs revealed a misalignment: persona-matched responses successfully achieved targeted behavioral shifts, yet raters holistically preferred the generic empathy of the persona-neutral baseline. Our findings suggest that holistic empathy judgments and clinically-aligned response design can pull in opposite directions, and that evaluating LLM-based stigma support requires rubrics capable of decomposing the two.

</details>


### [147] [Do LLM Embedding Spaces Recover Expert Structure?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23394)
*Yixuan Zhu,Zhenke Duan,Fanghen Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM嵌入空间是否能恢复心理健康语言中专家定义的症状结构。使用28个Reddit社区，比较预训练和微调Qwen3（0.6B和4B）嵌入空间，通过表征相似性分析（RSA）与专家症状矩阵对比。发现：预训练嵌入存在一定的对齐；微调增强了对齐（尤其在细粒度类别上）；更大模型提升零样本对齐和监督学习增益；在控制VAD、LIWC、词汇风格和主题分布等混淆因素后，剩余对齐仍然显著。结论：LLM嵌入能恢复专家相关的类别几何结构，但恢复程度具有层级依赖性，需通过明确混淆控制来验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练文本嵌入广泛应用于表征映射，但高类别可分性并不保证其几何结构能恢复专家定义的结构。在心理健康语言领域，症状关系提供了外部参考标准，但在线社区引入了领域、情感、风格和话语等多重混淆因素，现有研究缺乏系统评估嵌入空间与专家知识结构对齐程度的方法。

Method: 使用28个Reddit社区数据，比较预训练和监督微调Qwen3（0.6B和4B）嵌入空间。构建类别原型，通过表征相似性分析（RSA）评估表征相异性矩阵与专家症状矩阵的匹配度，并补充基于原型的典型性分析和多基线混淆控制（VAD、LIWC、词汇风格、主题分布）。

Result: 预训练嵌入在心理健康子集中与专家结构存在可测量的对齐；微调在细粒度类别上显著增强对齐；模型规模增大同时提升零样本对齐和监督学习增益；在控制VAD、LIWC、词汇风格和主题分布结构后，剩余对齐仍然显著。

Conclusion: LLM嵌入能够恢复专家相关的类别几何结构，但这种恢复具有层级依赖性（不同粒度级别表现不同）。研究强调不应仅从分类性能推断嵌入质量，而应通过明确的混淆控制来验证嵌入空间与专家知识的一致性。

Abstract: Pretrained text embeddings are increasingly used as representational maps, yet high category separability does not imply that their geometry recovers expert-defined structure. We study this problem in mental-health-related language, where symptom relations provide an external reference and online communities introduce strong domain, affective, stylistic, and discourse confounds. Using 28 Reddit communities, we compare pretrained and supervised fine-tuned Qwen3 embedding spaces at two scales (0.6B and 4B). We construct category prototypes, evaluate their representational dissimilarity matrices against an expert symptom matrix with representational similarity analysis, and complement this global test with prototype-based typicality and multi-baseline confound controls. Pretrained embeddings show measurable alignment with expert structure within the mental-health subset; fine-tuning strengthens this alignment most at the finest category level; and larger scale improves both zero-shot alignment and supervision-induced gains. Residual alignment remains substantial after controlling for VAD, LIWC, lexical style, and topic-distribution structure. These results suggest that LLM embeddings can recover expert-relevant category geometry, but this recovery is level-dependent and should be tested against explicit confounds rather than inferred from classification alone.

</details>


### [148] [ReasoningLens: Hierarchical Visualization and Diagnostic Auditing for Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23404)
*Jun Zhang,Jiasheng Zheng,Boxi Cao,Yaojie Lu,Hongyu Lin,Jia Zheng,Xianpei Han,Le Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ReasoningLens是一个开源框架，用于对大型推理模型的长Chain-of-Thought轨迹进行层次化可视化和诊断审计，通过结构化分层、自动化错误检测和系统性推理画像，将无结构化文本转化为可操作的洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型生成了极长的Chain-of-Thought推理轨迹，关键逻辑被大量程序化文本淹没，造成了透明度负担，亟需一种能够有效解释、调试和优化复杂推理链的方法。

Method: (1) 将推理轨迹结构化为交互式层次结构，分离高层策略与低层执行；(2) 利用智能审计代理进行自动化错误检测和工具增强验证；(3) 合成系统性推理画像以揭示模型特定的盲点。

Result: 将无结构的文本墙转化为可操作的洞察，为推理为中心的AI提供可解释性、调试和优化的模块化基础。

Conclusion: ReasoningLens为解释、调试和优化下一代推理型AI提供了一套模块化的基础框架，有效解决了长推理链的透明度问题。

Abstract: The emergence of Large Reasoning Models has introduced exceptionally long Chain-of-Thought traces, creating a transparency burden where critical logic is often buried under massive procedural text. To address this, we present ReasoningLens, an open-source framework designed for the hierarchical visualization and diagnostic auditing of complex reasoning chains. ReasoningLens addresses information necropsy by: (1) structuring traces into interactive hierarchies that separate high-level strategy from low-level execution; (2) leveraging an agentic auditor for automated error detection and tool-augmented verification; and (3) synthesizing systemic reasoning profiles to reveal model-specific blind spots. By transforming unstructured walls of text into actionable insights, ReasoningLens provides a modular foundation for interpreting, debugging, and optimizing the next generation of reasoning-centric AI.

</details>


### [149] [UnBias-Plus: Detect, Explain, and Rewrite Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23412)
*Ahmed Y. Radwan,Ahmed ElKady,Sindhuja Chaduvula,Mohamed Hafez,Amrit Krishnan,Shaina Raza*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UnBias-Plus是一个开源工具包，提供细粒度多类偏见分类、偏见跨度定位、中性文本改写以及推理解释功能，支持Python、CLI、REST API和Web等多种接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏见检测方法大多仅识别偏见是否存在，缺乏细粒度检测、可解释性、中性改写以及公开可用的训练模型。

Method: UnBias-Plus作为一个统一的开源工具包，集成了四个核心功能：段级多类偏见分类、偏见跨度定位、中性文本改写以及决策推理，并提供Python、CLI、REST API和Web等多种交互方式。

Result: 提供了一个功能完整的开源偏见分析工具包，支持多种交互方式，相关源代码、模型、数据集和文档均已公开。

Conclusion: UnBias-Plus通过开源方式填补了现有偏见检测工具在细粒度、可解释性和改写能力上的空白，推动偏见分析的普及与可访问性。

Abstract: Bias in natural language remains a persistent challenge in both human-written and AI-generated content, affecting domains such as journalism, education, and AI research. Most existing detection methods identify only the presence of bias, with limited support for granular detection, interpretable explanations, neutral rewriting, and openly available trained models. We present UnBias-Plus, an open-source toolkit unifying (1) segment-level multi-class bias classification, (2) biased span localization, (3) neutral text rewriting, and (4) reasoning for each decision. Available via Python, CLI, REST API, and web interfaces, UnBias-Plus supports accessible bias analysis. The toolkit, source code, models, datasets, and documentation are publicly available.

</details>


### [150] [TriggerBench: Investigating Prospective Memory for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23459)
*Tianhua Zhang,Xinjiang Wang,Qianxi Zhang,Qi Chen,Kun Li,Yaoqi Chen,Dingdong Wang,Helen Meng,Yan Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TriggerBench基准测试，系统评估大语言模型在长交互中的前瞻记忆（PM）能力。研究发现PM存在精度-召回率权衡和注意力脆弱性；PM比回溯记忆显著更难，在长上下文中性能急剧下降；PM可作为推理能力剩余容量的行为探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估主要关注回溯记忆（通过显式查询），而前瞻记忆（自发回忆并执行潜在约束的能力）缺乏系统评估。尽管LLM越来越多地部署在长交互场景中，PM能力对于真实应用至关重要却未被充分研究。

Method: 构建TriggerBench基准测试，涵盖日常助手和专业工作流五个维度。设计配对RM对照、对比正/负变体及过载触发条件，在统一协议下细粒度测量前瞻回忆率、虚警率和注意力鲁棒性。

Result: (i) PM存在精度-召回率权衡和注意力脆弱性，增强推理可提升前瞻回忆但可能过度拟合"始终提醒"启发式；隐式约束或触发电载条件下PM显著下降。(ii) PM比RM困难得多：同等上下文中RM在100K tokens内接近饱和，PM随上下文增长急剧衰减。(iii) PM可作为推理剩余容量的行为探针：结合AIME-2025数学题发现，成功轨迹的PM准确率高于失败轨迹。

Conclusion: 鲁棒的PM仍是开放挑战，现有模型在隐式约束和长上下文下的PM能力严重不足，需要新的方法来增强LLM的前瞻记忆能力。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in long interactions, existing evaluations focus predominantly on retrospective memory (RM) via explicit queries. Prospective memory (PM), the critical ability to spontaneously recall and act on latent constraints without direct prompts, remains largely unevaluated. We introduce TriggerBench, a comprehensive PM benchmark spanning five dimensions across both daily assistants and professional workflows. TriggerBench pairs scenarios with matched RM controls, contrastive positive/negative variants, and overloaded triggers, enabling fine-grained measurement of proactive recall, false-alarm rate, and attentional robustness under a single protocol. Our evaluation yields three key findings. (i) PM shows a precision-recall trade-off and attentional fragility. Though enhanced reasoning significantly improves proactive recall, models may overfit to an "always-remind" heuristic. Furthermore, PM accuracy degrades substantially under implicit constraints or triggers overloaded by concurrent user requests, indicating that robust PM remains an open challenge. (ii) PM is notably harder than RM: on identical contexts, RM near-saturates up to 100K tokens, while PM decays sharply as context length scales. (iii) PM may serve as a behavioral probe of spare reasoning capacity. Pairing PM scenarios with AIME-2025 math problems reveals that successful trajectories yield higher PM accuracy than failed ones at the same context length, showing PM tracks spare reasoning budget that token count obscures. Project page: https://github.com/KristenZHANG/TriggerBench-Official.

</details>


### [151] [War in the Abstract: The Rise and Consequences of Militarized Language in Scientific Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23462)
*Sovesh Mohapatra,David Lydon-Staley,Dani S. Bassett*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过对2010-2025年间2140万篇论文摘要的分析，发现军事化语言使用频率上升48%（OpenAlex）和32%（PubMed），且2019年后加速增长。军事化语言强度与国家冲突水平高度相关（r=0.77-0.84），全球南方增长最快。实验表明，战争框架降低了可信度、资助意愿和政策支持，仅微弱提升紧迫感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学家本不从事战争，但军事化词汇却频繁出现在学术摘要中。作者旨在量化这一现象的普遍程度、变化趋势及其对说服力的因果影响。

Method: 分析OpenAlex和PubMed中2140万篇论文（2010-2025）的摘要军事术语出现频率，并与乌普萨拉冲突数据项目进行相关性分析；同时开展被试内战争框架实验（N=801, 32,040次试验），测试军事化语言对可信度、资助意愿、政策支持和紧迫感的因果效应。

Result: 2010-2025年间军事术语出现率上升48%（OpenAlex）和32%（PubMed），2019年后加速；与国家冲突水平相关（r=0.77-0.84）；全球南方增长最快；社会科学使用水平最高，工程和计算机科学增长最快；COVID和LLM时代缩小了英语与非英语作者的差距。实验显示战争框架降低可信度（d_z=-0.28）、资助意愿（d_z=-0.12）和政策支持（d_z=-0.08），仅微弱提升紧迫感（d_z=+0.07）。

Conclusion: 科学摘要正持续向军事化语言偏移，但这种语言使用可能侵蚀可信度、资助意愿和政策支持，与学术界普遍直觉相反。

Abstract: Scientists do not, by profession, wage war. Yet warfare's vocabulary consistently appears in their abstracts. To quantify the extent to which warfare's vocabulary pervades scientific abstracts, we analyze 21.4 million papers (2010-2025; OpenAlex, PubMed). We additionally run a within-subject war-framing experiment (N = 801; 32,040 trials) designed to provide causal insight into the effects of militaristic language on persuasion. Between 2010 and 2025, the presence of militaristic terms in scientific abstracts rose 48% in OpenAlex and 32% in PubMed, with the rise accelerating sharply after 2019 (cross-database r = 0.96, p < 10^-8). The prevalence of militaristic language is conflict-aligned at both country and annual scales (Uppsala Conflict Data Program; r = 0.77-0.84), with the abstracts from the Global South displaying the fastest rise in militaristic language. Among disciplines, social sciences leads in level of such language while engineering and computer science lead in growth. The COVID and post-2022 large-language-model eras also saw the rise and narrowed the language gap between native-English and non-English authors. In our follow-up experiment, we found that war framing reduced credibility (mean shift -0.18 Likert units, 95% CI [-0.21, -0.14]; d_z = -0.28, p < 10^-20), funding willingness (d_z = -0.12) and policy support (d_z = -0.08), with a trend-level increase in sense of urgency (d_z = +0.07). Collectively, findings reveal that while scientific abstracts drift toward warfare, the use of militaristic language may erode credibility, funding willingness, and policy support.

</details>


### [152] [Self-Compacting Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23525)
*Tianjian Li,Jingyu Zhang,William Jurayj,Xi Wang,Chuanyang Jin,Mehrdad Farajtabar,Eric Nalisnick,Daniel Khashabi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SelfCompact是一种让模型自主决定何时以及如何压缩上下文的智能体框架，通过配对压缩工具和轻量级规则，在无微调情况下显著提升多跳推理和智能体搜索性能，同时降低30-70%的token成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体轨迹中，链式思考与工具调用产生的陈旧上下文会锚定后续生成并超出上下文窗口。已有的固定间隔压缩策略忽略轨迹结构，可能导致部分推导或搜索过程中间结果被丢弃。因此需要一种能感知结构、自适应决定压缩时机的框架。

Method: 提出SelfCompact智能体框架，包含两个核心组件：(i) 压缩工具——模型可主动调用来总结累积上下文；(ii) 轻量级规则——指定何时触发压缩（子任务完成或轨迹收敛）和何时抑制压缩（推导中间或陷入卡顿）。两者协同工作，无需微调或外部监督。

Result: 在6个基准（数学推理和智能体搜索）和7个模型上实验：无需压缩的基线相比提升最高18.1分（数学）和5-9分（智能体搜索）；相比固定间隔压缩，在30-70%更低token成本下达到同等或更优性能。暴露了元认知差距：无提示的模型无法可靠判断上下文何时"腐烂"，但轻量级规则可弥补这一差距。

Conclusion: 压缩时机的决策能力可以作为一种无需训练的框架提供的能力。SelfCompact通过工具+规则的组合，有效弥合了模型的元认知差距，实现了高效的自适应上下文压缩。

Abstract: Long agent traces composed of chains of thought and tool calls accumulate stale content that anchor subsequent generations, and eventually outgrow the context window. Existing scaffolds mitigate it with fixed-interval compaction triggered at a token threshold. Such triggers pay no heed to trajectory structure, risking discard of partial results mid-derivation or mid-search. We propose SelfCompact, a scaffold that allows the model itself to decide when and how to compact. Specifically, it pairs two inference-time elements: (i) a compaction tool the model invokes to summarize the accumulated context, and (ii) a lightweight rubric specifying when to fire (a sub-task has resolved, or the trajectory is converging) and when to suppress (mid-derivation, or when stuck). Both are needed. The tool alone is unevenly used across open-weight models, often invoked at unhelpful moments or not at all; the rubric alone cannot act. Together, they elicit effective adaptive compaction without any fine-tuning or external supervision. We present empirical results on six benchmarks (competitive math and agentic search) and seven models. Our results show that SelfCompact matches or exceeds fixed-interval summarization at a fraction of the token cost, improving over a no-summarization baseline by up to 18.1 points on math and 5-9 points on agentic search at 30-70% lower per-question cost. Our results expose a meta-cognitive gap: although unprompted models cannot reliably tell when their own context is rotting, a lightweight rubric closes this gap, reframing when to compact as a capability that scaffolds can supply without training.

</details>


### [153] [LangMAP: A Language-Adaptive Approach to Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23566)
*Clara Meister,Suchir Salhan,Andrzej Szablewski,Pietro Lesci,Paula Buttery,Tiago Pimentel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出LangMAP分词方案，在无需改变词汇表的情况下，通过共享词汇表实现语言特定的分词，并在形态边界对齐和AST叶子边界对齐上取得改进，但在下游任务上结果不一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言特定分词器能提升模型在对应语言上的性能，但使用这类分词器需要从头训练模型或适配已有模型的词汇表，成本高昂。因此，需要一种能在不改变词汇表的前提下实现语言特定分词的方法。

Method: 提出Language-adaptive Maximum a Posteriori (LangMAP) Tokenization，将UnigramLM算法扩展到多语言场景，通过共享词汇表生成语言特定的分词结果。训练时需语言标签，推理时无需语言信息即可进行语言特定分词。

Result: 在14个开源分词器、9种自然语言和9种编程语言上，LangMAP改善了形态边界对齐，并在所有编程语言上改善了抽象语法树叶子边界对齐。微调实验中，LangMAP提升了目标语言的语法可接受性（MultiBLiMP），但在知识相关任务（Global-PIQA、Belebele）上效果不一致。

Conclusion: LangMAP能在不改变词汇表的情况下实现语言特定分词，在形态和语法边界对齐上表现良好，但在下游知识任务上的收益不稳定，仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Language-specific tokenizers improve tokenization quality and the downstream performance of models on those languages. However, using such a tokenizer comes at a cost: either a new model must be trained from scratch, or the vocabulary of an existing pretrained model must be adapted. We propose Language-adaptive Maximum a Posteriori (LangMAP) Tokenization, a tokenization scheme that extends the UnigramLM algorithm to the multilingual setting, producing language-specific tokenization from a single shared vocabulary. Notably, LangMAP can be used when training a multilingual language model from scratch or to adapt a pretrained model's tokenizer to individual languages without changing its vocabulary. While language labels are required at training time, a key feature of the algorithm is that it then performs language-specific tokenization at inference without knowledge of the input's language. Across 14 open-source tokenizers, 9 natural languages, and 9 programming languages, LangMAP improves morphological boundary alignment and, for all coding languages tested, alignment with abstract syntax tree (AST) leaf boundaries. In fine-tuning experiments, results are mixed: LangMAP improves target-language grammatical acceptability (MultiBLiMP) on the languages tested; its benefits are less consistent on knowledge-related tasks (Global-PIQA, Belebele).

</details>


### [154] [Evaluation Awareness Is Not One Capability: Evidence from Open Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23583)
*Nilesh Nayan,Aishwarya Sampath Kumar,Rishiraj Girmal,Shivani Anilkumar,Sankaran Vaidyanathan,David A. Nader Palacio,Reshmi Ghosh,Soundararajan Srinivasan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过8项实验、37个开源模型和7个模型家族，系统性地刻画了评估意识（evaluation awareness）现象：模型能检测到评估场景并调整行为，导致基准测试中的合规性成为过度乐观的上界，无法反映真实部署行为。研究发现检测能力中等且主要由训练驱动、检测会改变安全行为、表征在行为崩溃后仍存活、各维度耦合弱，揭示了基准幻觉（benchmark illusion）的多变量特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全基准测试隐含假设测试条件下的行为能预测部署行为，但该假设在模型能够检测到评估线索并据此调整行为时失效。这导致基准性能与部署行为之间存在差距：在测试条件下测量的合规性成为过度乐观的上界，高估了移除评估框架后模型的安全行为。作者旨在系统性地刻画这种评估意识现象及其对安全评估的影响。

Method: 研究设计8个实验，涵盖37个开源权重模型、7个模型家族。实验包括：(i) 评估检测能力（AUROC指标），对比指令微调与规模的影响；(ii) 分析检测如何改变安全行为（假设框架下的硬拒绝率变化、HarmBench框架效应）；(iii) 检测表征在行为崩溃后的存活情况（探针AUROC、多层引导对下游任务的影响）；(iv) 各维度的耦合分析（相关性检验）。

Result: (i) 24/37模型检测能力超过随机水平，最佳AUROC 0.714 vs 人类0.819，指令微调主导作用大于模型规模；(ii) 假设框架下硬拒绝率下降5.8个百分点，21/140 HarmBench框架效应显著，合规性最高上升30个百分点；(iii) 表征在行为低于随机水平的改写后，探针仍保持AUROC 0.98，多层引导能因果性地影响三个下游任务而随机控制无效；(iv) 仅1/15相关性显著，唯一稳健关联是行为检测与框架抵抗之间（ρ=-0.79，p<0.001）。

Conclusion: 评估意识的存在使得基准测试衡量的是测试条件下的合规性而非真实部署安全，且由于可检测性、行为表现和可控性独立变化，这是一个多变量问题而非单一指标所能刻画，因此任何单一的意识评分都无法可靠地代理部署安全性。作者将此现象称为基准幻觉（benchmark illusion）。

Abstract: Safety benchmarks assume that test-condition behavior predicts deployment behavior, an assumption that fails if models detect evaluation cues and adapt. This opens a gap between benchmark performance and deployment behavior: compliance measured under test conditions becomes an optimistic upper bound that overstates how safely a model behaves once the evaluation harness is removed. We characterize this evaluation awareness through eight experiments across 37 open-weight models and seven families. (i)Detection is moderate and training-driven (24/37 models exceed chance, best AUROC 0.714 vs.0.819 human, with instruction tuning dominating over scale). (ii)Detection shifts safety behavior (hard refusal drops 5.8 percentage points under hypothetical framing, and 21/140 HarmBench framing effects are significant, with compliance rising up to +30 percentage points. (iii)Representations survive behavioral collapse (probes retain AUROC 0.98 under rewrites that drive behavior below chance, and multi-layer steering causally moves three downstream tasks while random controls do not). (iv)These axes are weakly coupled (only 1/15 correlations are significant, the sole robust link being behavioral detection versus framing resistance, $ρ=-0.79$, $p<0.001$). We call this gap the benchmark illusion: because detectability, behavioral manifestation, and controllability vary independently, it is multivariate rather than a single number, so no single awareness score is a reliable proxy for deployment safety.

</details>


### [155] [EnterpriseClawBench: Benchmarking Agents from Real Workplace Sessions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23654)
*Jincheng Zhong,Weizhi Wang,Che Jiang,Kai Tian,Zhenzhao Yuan,Junlin Yang,Dianqiao Lei,Kaiyan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了EnterpriseClawBench，一个基于真实企业工作会话构建的智能体基准测试，包含852个可复现任务，最佳配置（Codex+GPT-5.5）仅达0.663分，表明企业智能体评估需要报告多维度指标而非单一分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准测试缺乏对企业工作场景（异构文件、工具调用、业务交付件）的覆盖，且缺乏从真实企业会话中系统化构建评估任务的方法。

Method: 从大规模企业工作会话档案出发，构建852个可复现任务，每个任务附带恢复的fixture、重写的prompt、角色类别、技能子类、硬规则和语义评分准则。因数据包含内部企业内容，仅发布构建与评估协议。

Result: 最佳配置（Codex with GPT-5.5）仅达到0.663分，表明企业智能体任务具有高度挑战性。

Conclusion: 企业智能体评估必须报告多个维度（模型组合、交付件质量、视觉质量、成本、运行时间、技能迁移行为），而非单一性能分数。

Abstract: Enterprise agents increasingly operate inside workspaces: they read heterogeneous files, invoke tools, and deliver business artifacts. We introduce EnterpriseClawBench, an enterprise agent benchmark constructed from proprietary, real-world agent sessions. Starting from a large archive of workplace sessions, the EnterpriseClawBench produces 852 reproducible tasks, each paired with recovered fixtures, rewritten prompts, role classes, skill subclasses, hard rules, and semantic rubrics. Because the sessions contain internal enterprise content, we do not release the benchmark data; instead, our reusable contribution is the construction and evaluation protocol. On EnterpriseClawBench, the best configuration reaches only 0.663 (Codex with GPT-5.5). These results show that enterprise agent evaluation must report harness--model combinations, artifact delivery, visual quality, cost, runtime, and skill-transfer behavior, rather than collapsing performance into a single score. Code: https://github.com/FrontisAI/EnterpriseClawBench

</details>


### [156] [Can LLMs Reliably Self-Report Adversarial Prefills, and How?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23671)
*Quang Minh Nguyen,Uzair Ahmed,Taegyoon Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLM在面对对抗性前缀攻击时无法可靠地识别自身被污染的响应，其内省信号主要来源于安全与拒答相关推理，且该信号对探测方式高度敏感；三种LoRA微调方法虽能扩大意图探测差距但并未迁移至篡改探测，反而在多数模型上提高了攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究显示LLM在良性任务上具备内省能力，本文将其延伸至安全场景，探究模型能否可靠地识别自身输出是否受到了对抗性前缀攻击（adversarial prefill attack）。

Method: 在10个开放权重指令微调LLM（3B-70B）和4个安全基准上测试模型的自我识别能力；使用权重正交化方法消除拒答方向的影响；比较不同探测框架（内部意图 vs. 外部篡改）对结果的影响；在8个模型（3B-27B）上测试三种LoRA微调方法（SFT、GRPO、DPO）的效果。

Result: （1）没有模型能可靠识别自身被对抗性前缀攻击的响应（平均错误声称率27.3%）；（2）内省信号主要来自安全与拒答相关推理；（3）正交化拒答方向后，prefilled与natural输出的声称率差距几乎消失，但该方向并非唯一中介；（4）探测框架（内部意图 vs. 外部篡改）会引发定性不同的响应；（5）三种LoRA微调方法在所有8B-27B模型上均扩大了意图探测差距，但方法排名因模型而异；（6）干预未迁移至篡改探测，且反直觉地提高了多数模型的攻击成功率，仅构成部分缓解。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了LLM在安全场景下内省信号的机制基础，并强调了LLM自我报告在可靠性方面的风险。

Abstract: Prior work shows that large language models (LLMs) exhibit introspective capability on benign tasks. We extend the question to safety contexts and examine how reliably a model can recognize that its own prior response was elicited by an adversarial prefill attack. Across ten open-weight instruction-tuned LLMs (3B to 70B) and four safety benchmarks, no model reliably recognizes its own compromised outputs, with models claiming intent on prefilled responses at an average rate of $27.3\%$. Introspective signal stems largely from safety- and refusal-related reasoning. Orthogonalizing models' weights against the refusal direction collapses the gap between claiming rates on prefilled and natural outputs to near zero, though the direction is not its unique mediator. The signal is also probe-dependent: framing the question as internal intention versus external tampering elicits qualitatively different responses on the same models. We test three LoRA finetuning methods (SFT, GRPO, DPO) on eight models from 3B to 27B; all three widen the intention-probe gap on every model from 8B to 27B, with method ranking varying by model. The intervention does not transfer to the tampering probe and counterintuitively raises attack success rate under adversarial prefill on most models, amounting to a partial mitigation. These findings outline mechanisms underpinning the observed introspective signals in safety contexts and highlight risks in the reliability of LLM self-reports.

</details>


### [157] [Randomized YaRN Improves Length Generalization for Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23687)
*Manas Mehta,Fangcong Yin,Greg Durrett*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Randomized YaRN方法，通过结合YaRN位置外推、随机位置编码和长度课程，在短上下文训练中暴露模型于长位置分布，显著提升LLM在16K-128K超长序列上的推理泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在预训练后虽经长序列微调，但仍难以泛化到更长的序列，需要一种能有效提升长度泛化能力的方法。

Method: 将YaRN位置外推与随机位置编码结合，在短上下文训练时从更大位置范围采样分配YaRN位置编码，同时采用长度课程训练策略。

Result: 在BABILong和MRCR两个长上下文推理基准上，训练数据<8K上下文时，Randomized YaRN在16K到128K上下文中持续提升推理性能，显著优于标准微调，在远超出分布的长度上增益最大。

Conclusion: 渐进式地将模型暴露于分布外（OOD）的位置分布，是提升长上下文推理泛化能力的有效方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are typically pretrained on short sequences and then extended to work on longer sequences with additional training. However, such LLMs still struggle to further generalize to very long sequences. We propose Randomized YaRN, a training method that improves length generalization by combining YaRN-based positional extrapolation with randomized positional encoding and a length curriculum. During training on short context data, tokens are assigned YaRN positional encodings sampled from a larger position range, exposing the model to out-of-distribution positional representations even on short-context inputs. We evaluate Randomized YaRN on two challenging long-context reasoning benchmarks, BABILong and Multi-Round Coreference Resolution (MRCR). When training on data with <8K context, Randomized YaRN consistently improves reasoning performance on context lengths from 16K to 128K and outperforms standard fine-tuning, with the largest gains appearing at far out-of-distribution lengths. Our results suggest that progressively exposing models to OOD positional distributions provides an effective recipe for generalizable long-context reasoning.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [BELLS-O: Evaluating the Operational Trade-offs of LLM Supervision Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20668)
*Leonhard Waibl,Felix Michalak,Hadrien Mariaccia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: BELLS-O是首个独立的LLM监控系统运营基准，评估了17个提供商的28个系统（专用防护栏和通用LLM），涵盖检测率、误报率、延迟和成本四个维度。结果表明：内容审核任务中专用系统在运营上占优（检测率相当，速度快5-10倍，成本低~10倍）；越狱检测任务中前沿LLM检测率更高但成本和延迟显著增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM监控系统基准存在供应商偏见、忽略成本和延迟指标、很少将专用防护栏与通用LLM进行公平比较等问题，缺乏独立、全面的运营基准来指导实际部署中的安全措施选择。

Method: 构建包含11种危害类别和13种攻击技术的内部数据集（手写提示、专家精选样本、受控合成生成），对合成数据做释义处理以消除生成器指纹；在检测率、误报率、延迟和成本四个维度上系统评估28个系统。

Result: 内容审核：顶级专用系统检测率~95% vs 前沿LLM 94%，误报率均<=2%，但专用系统快5-10倍、便宜~10倍。越狱检测：前沿LLM检测率更高、误报率更低，但成本高10-50倍、延迟高5-10倍。

Conclusion: 不存在通用的最优监控系统，需根据用例在性能与运营成本间权衡；BELLS-O作为首个供应商中立的基准，为实际部署约束下选择安全措施提供了基础。

Abstract: LLM supervision systems, namely input/output moderation filters and jailbreak detectors, are the primary safeguard against misuse in deployed AI applications, yet existing benchmarks are often vendor-biased, omit cost and latency, and rarely compare specialized guardrails against repurposed generalist LLMs. We present BELLS-O (Benchmark for the Evaluation of LLM Supervision Systems, Operational), the first independent operational benchmark of LLM supervision systems. BELLS-O evaluates 28 systems from 17 providers: every major specialized guardrail (e.g., LlamaGuard-4, ShieldGemma-2, Lakera Guard) and frontier generalists repurposed as supervisors (e.g., GPT-5.4, Claude Sonnet 4.6, Grok-4.1), jointly on detection rate, false-positive rate, latency, and monetary cost. We cover input/output moderation across 11 harm categories and jailbreak detection across 13 attack techniques, using in-house datasets built from handcrafted prompts, expert-curated samples, and quality-controlled synthetic generation. To suppress latent generator fingerprints in synthetic data, every generated sample is paraphrased. Mapping the Pareto frontier reveals use-case-dependent tradeoffs. On content moderation, specialized supervisors are operationally dominant: top systems match frontier LLMs on detection (~95% vs. 94%) at comparably low false-positive rates (<=2%), while running 5-10x faster and ~10x cheaper. On jailbreak detection, the tradeoff shifts: frontier LLMs achieve higher detection and lower false-positive rates but at 10-50x higher cost and 5-10x higher latency. We release the benchmark, framework, leaderboard, and datasets as the first vendor-neutral basis for selecting safeguards under real deployment constraints.

</details>


### [159] [Amplify, Don't Create: Temporal Accumulation for Slow-Burn Prompt Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20746)
*J Alex Corll*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于时间累积（CUSUM和峰值统计）的方法，用于检测针对工具使用型智能体的控制平面慢速注入攻击。实验表明，该方法在集中攻击下优于逐事件检测器（差距+0.092，95% CI [+0.025, +0.155]），在git仓库泄露场景中，持久性累积能区分攻击与良性伪装（AUC 0.708），而峰值控制无效（AUC 0.417）。但该方法在无逐事件得分余量时失效（AUC 0.167），是一种窄带余量放大器，并贡献了伪复制警告和独立性审计标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的提示注入检测器仅对单个事件或消息进行评分，无法检测跨轨迹分布的慢速控制平面攻击（slow-burn attacks），这些攻击通过将微弱指令分散在多个事件中，使每个事件低于检测阈值，从而绕过检测。

Method: 在代理侧引入时间累积器，将冻结的逐事件得分（char-ngram SVM+嵌入对比头）降低为峰值（peak）和CUSUM持续性统计量（persistence statistic）。仅在满足下限、绑定到已执行动作边且仍低于检测阈值的嫁接（graft）进入慢速端点。通过聚类bootstrap和AUC评估。

Result: 集中攻击下，轨迹级累积优于逐事件检测（差距+0.092）；git仓库泄露场景中，持久性累积AUC 0.708，峰值控制AUC 0.417；宽清洁路径下瞬时信号失效（AUC 0.167）。独立性受限（仅3-4个独立任务）。

Conclusion: 时间累积是一种窄带余量放大器：能积累低于阈值的信号，但无法在逐事件检测器无余量时创造有效区分。作为副产品，贡献了伪复制警告和智能体基准评估的独立性审计标准。

Abstract: Most prompt-injection detectors score a single event or message. Control-plane attacks against tool-using agents can instead distribute weak directives across a trajectory while keeping each event below threshold. We test whether a proxy-side temporal accumulator recovers this slow-burn signal by reducing frozen per-event scores to peak and CUSUM persistence statistics. To avoid circularity, grafts are generated against a held-out autoregressive cloaking target and then re-scored under a detector of record: a frozen char-ngram SVM plus an embedding-contrastive head. Only floor-met grafts bound to executed action edges and still sub-threshold under the detector of record enter the slow-burn endpoint. This is a boundary result, not a deployable detector. On concentrated attacks, trajectory-level accumulation beats the per-event foil under a clustered bootstrap (gap +0.092, 95% CI [+0.025, +0.155]), while persistence and peak are statistically tied. On git repo-exfil, density-four floor-met sub-threshold grafts add persistence mass that matched benign shams do not (persistence-delta AUC 0.708 over four attack survivors and six benign shams), while the matched peak-delta control does not separate attack from sham (AUC 0.417), localizing the effect to accumulated persistence rather than a single hot graft. The effect fails on broader clean-path actions (persistence-delta AUC 0.167), where the detector assigns attack and benign actions indistinguishable per-event scores, leaving no margin for CUSUM to bank. Independent powering is blocked by only three to four independent tasks. Temporal accumulation is therefore a narrow-band margin amplifier: it can bank elevated sub-threshold signal but cannot create margin where the per-event detector has none. As byproducts, we contribute a pseudo-replication warning and an independence-audit standard for agent-benchmark evaluation.

</details>


### [160] [Privacy-Preserving Compliance on Public Ledgers via Selective Disclosure Authorization Schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20760)
*Supriya Khadka,Sanchari Das*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出选择性披露授权方案（SDAS），一种用于公共账本上细粒度、可撤销合规检查的密码学原语，通过将零知识证明绑定到链上发送者地址来防止前置交易和证明重用攻击。作者在以太坊上实现了ZK-Compliance系统，基于14约束的Circom电路实现发送者绑定，浏览器端证明生成<200ms，链上验证消耗240,512 gas。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共分布式账本通过彻底透明性来保证完整性，这与监管合规所需的数据最小化原则产生冲突。现有零知识证明方案在智能合约环境中忽视了对抗性约束，特别是链下证明生成与链上提交的异步解耦在公共mempool中引入了前置交易和证明重用风险。

Method: 形式化定义了选择性披露授权方案（SDAS），引入账本绑定属性不可链接性和上下文感知发送者绑定两个安全概念。在以太坊上实现了ZK-Compliance系统，使用14约束的Circom电路将零知识证明锚定到执行链上的发送者地址，实现了用户控制的"授权、验证、撤销"生命周期。

Result: Sepolia测试网评估证实了实际可行性：基于浏览器的证明生成在200毫秒内完成，链上验证消耗240,512 gas，能够在保护严格属性隐私的同时消除不同调用者的证明重用行为。

Conclusion: SDAS通过发送者绑定机制有效解决了公共账本环境中零知识证明的前置交易和重用攻击问题，在透明性与隐私之间取得了平衡，为监管合规的隐私保护提供了实用的密码学方案。

Abstract: Public distributed ledgers enforce integrity through radical transparency, creating tension with data minimization principles required for regulatory compliance. While Zero-Knowledge Proofs (ZKPs) offer a theoretical privacy solution, existing constructions often overlook adversarial constraints in smart contract environments. Specifically, the asynchronous decoupling of off-chain proof generation from on-chain submission introduces front-running and proof-reuse risks in public mempools. In this work, we formalize Selective Disclosure Authorization Schemes (SDAS), a cryptographic primitive for granular and revocable compliance checks on public ledgers without revealing the underlying witness. We define a security model for SDAS, introducing Ledger-Bound Attribute Unlinkability and Context-Aware Sender Binding to capture how valid proofs remain bound to their intended authorization context. To validate sender binding, we present ZK-Compliance, an Ethereum-based instantiation that operationalizes a user-controlled "Grant, Verify, Revoke" lifecycle. We implement the sender-binding component using a 14-constraint Circom circuit that anchors the zero-knowledge proof to the executing on-chain sender address. Our Sepolia evaluation confirms practical viability: browser-based proof generation executes in under 200 ms, and on-chain verification costs 240,512 gas, neutralizing proof reuse by different callers while preserving strict attribute privacy.

</details>


### [161] [Memory-Centric Computing: Security Benefits and Challenges of Processing-in-DRAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20786)
*Ismail Emir Yuksel,F. Nisa Bostanci,Ataberk Olgun,Onur Mutlu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文探讨了从处理器中心计算向内存中心计算（特别是处理单元内计算，PiD）范式转变对系统安全的影响。基于真实DRAM芯片，实现了高达16.05 Gb/s吞吐量的真随机数生成器和比先前最优方案延迟低5.75%的物理不可克隆函数；同时揭示了两个关键安全挑战：放大后的DRAM读干扰（首次翻转所需最小访问次数减少158倍）和高吞吐量内存时序信道（14.8 Mb/s通信吞吐量）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统处理器中心计算中，数据在CPU与主存间的频繁移动导致性能和能耗严重低下。内存中心计算将计算能力迁移至数据所在位置，但这一范式转变对系统安全的影响尚未得到充分探索，亟需系统性分析其安全收益与挑战。

Method: 采用Processing-in-DRAM (PiD)范式，利用并增强DRAM芯片的操作特性在存储器内直接执行计算。通过真实DRAM芯片实验，评估基于DRAM的硬件安全原语（TRNG和PUF）性能，并测量放大后读干扰和内存时序信道的攻击指标。

Result: 1) 实现了16.05 Gb/s吞吐量的DRAM真随机数生成器，以及比先前最优方案延迟低5.75%的物理不可克隆函数；2) 发现读干扰威胁加剧：首次比特翻转所需最小DRAM访问次数减少158倍；3) 内存时序信道可达14.8 Mb/s的高速通信吞吐量。

Conclusion: 内存中心计算（PiD）既带来了新的安全原语（高效TRNG/PUF）和减少数据暴露的机遇，也引入了放大读干扰和高速时序信道等新攻击面。未来应将内存设计为计算、存储与安全三位一体的主动基板。

Abstract: Today's computing systems are processor-centric: they require frequent data movement between processing elements (e.g., CPU) and main memory (DRAM), leading to significant inefficiencies in performance and energy consumption. Memory-centric computing instead moves computation to the data, enabling computation capability in and near all places where data is generated and stored, and greatly reducing the performance and energy overheads of data access and data movement. This shift from a processor-centric to a memory-centric paradigm has important and underexplored consequences for system security. Turning memory from a dumb, inactive store into an active computing substrate introduces benefits as well as challenges for system security: it can provide new in-memory security primitives and also reduce data exposure, but it can also expose new attack surfaces. This work discusses the security benefits and challenges of memory-centric computing, specifically Processing-in-DRAM (PiD), a paradigm where the operational characteristics of a DRAM chip are exploited and enhanced to perform computation on data stored in DRAM. Specifically, we describe 1) new state-of-the-art DRAM-based true random number generators that provide up to 16.05 Gb/s throughput and physical unclonable functions with 5.75% lower evaluation latency than the prior state-of-the-art, both on real DRAM chips and 2) two key security challenges of PiD: amplified DRAM read disturbance (e.g., 158x reduction in the minimum number of DRAM accesses required to induce the first bitflip) and high throughput memory timing channels (e.g., a communication throughput of 14.8Mb/s). We believe it is time to design, use, and program DRAM, and in general memory, not as an inactive storage substrate, but as a combined computation, storage, and security substrate, where computational capability, storage density, and security are all key goals.

</details>


### [162] [PromptMark: A Prompt-Guided Iterative-Feedback Framework for Source Code Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20835)
*Istiaq Ahmed Fahad,Mridha Md. Nafis Fuad,Kazi Sakib*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为PromptMark的黑盒提示引导水印框架，通过结构化输入指令向AI生成代码中嵌入隐形统计信号，在保持代码功能正确性的同时实现可检测的水印，实验证明效果优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水印方法大多需要访问LLM解码过程，但实际开发中多数开发者使用黑盒商业LLM API，无法获取内部解码信息，因此需要一种无需模型内部访问权限的黑盒水印方案。

Method: 通过结构化输入提示引导模型生成具有特定标识符和注释命名模式的代码，利用统计检验检测水印信号，并通过迭代反馈循环基于检测分数优化提示。

Result: 在MBPP和HumanEval基准测试上，PromptMark在保持高代码正确率的同时实现了强水印可检测性，性能优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 提示引导的黑盒水印方法可以有效嵌入不可见但可统计检测的信号，为商业LLM API场景下的代码溯源和问责提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Watermarking has become a crucial technique for ensuring provenance and accountability in AI-generated source code. As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into development workflows, reliable attribution remains challenging. In practice, most developers rely on commercial LLM APIs operating under black-box constraints, making existing approaches that require access to the decoding process less feasible for real-world integration. To address this limitation, we propose PromptMark, a black-box, prompt-guided watermarking framework that embeds invisible yet statistically detectable signals into generated code via structured input instructions. The method steers models toward subtle identifier and comment naming patterns while preserving the functional correctness and structural integrity of the generated code. Detection is performed using statistical tests designed to remain reliable across varying code lengths and model outputs. The embedding is further refined through an iterative feedback loop, where prompts are updated based on watermark detection scores. Experiments on the MBPP and HumanEval benchmarks show that PromptMark consistently achieves strong watermark detectability while maintaining high code correctness, outperforming baseline approaches.

</details>


### [163] [Synthetic Network Packet Generation through Statistical Learning and Genetic Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20864)
*Mayank Raj,Nathaniel D. Bastian,Lance Fiondella,Gokhan Kul*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出并比较了两种带硬约束的物联网网络包生成方法：基于统计学习（PCA+OCSVM/IF）和基于遗传算法的方法，两者均通过异常检测门控、特征范围裁剪和独立验证确保生成数据物理有效。在ACI IoT 2023数据集上，统计方法达到1.20%平均异常率、~1091包/秒吞吐量；遗传算法达到0.62%平均异常率、~5.7包/秒吞吐量，二者均通过所有攻击类别的验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物联网入侵检测系统数据集存在固定活动分布和极端类别不平衡问题，而深度生成模型（GAN、VAE）无法保证合成数据包保持在物理有效的特征范围内，亟需具有硬约束机制的合成数据生成方法。

Method: 提出两种约束增强方法：1）统计学习方法：PCA基潜在空间采样结合双异常检测（单类SVM和孤立森林）边界强制；2）遗传算法方法：将包生成视为多目标优化问题，包含显式适应度准则（异常模型接受度、分布保真度）。两种方法均在合成流程中嵌入硬有效性约束：双异常检测门控、特征范围裁剪、独立验证。

Result: 在完整ACI IoT 2023数据集（1,231,411个包，12种攻击类别，类别不平衡最高175,805:1）上，两种方法均在30%异常率阈值下通过所有类别的独立验证。统计方法：平均异常率1.20%，吞吐量~1,091包/秒；遗传算法：平均异常率0.62%，各类别有机方差0.00%-2.50%，吞吐量~5.7包/秒。两种方法成功将5样本ARP欺骗类别扩增200倍至1,000个验证包。方法间吞吐量比约190:1。

Conclusion: 两种方法具有互补质量特征，吞吐量差异为不同部署场景（快速数据集增强 vs. 对抗鲁棒性测试）提供了基于证据的选择标准。

Abstract: Developing robust intrusion detection systems (IDS) for IoT environments requires large, labeled datasets capturing realistic traffic distributions across both benign and malicious activity. Existing public datasets suffer from fixed activity distributions and extreme class imbalance, while deep generative models (GANs, VAEs) provide no mechanism to enforce that synthetic packets remain within physically valid feature ranges. This paper proposes and compares two constraint-enforcing approaches for synthetic IoT network packet generation: (i) a statistical learning method combining PCA-based latent space sampling with dual One-Class SVM (OCSVM) and Isolation Forest (IF) boundary enforcement, and (ii) a genetic algorithm (GA) method that treats packet generation as a multi-objective optimization problem with explicit fitness criteria for anomaly model acceptance and distributional fidelity. Both methods embed hard validity constraints -- dual anomaly-detection gating, feature-range clamping, and independent validation -- directly into the synthesis pipeline. Evaluation on the complete ACI IoT 2023 dataset (1,231,411 packets, 12 attack categories, class imbalance up to 175,805:1) demonstrates that both methods achieve PASS status across all categories under independently trained validators with a 30% anomaly rate threshold: the statistical method attains 1.20% average anomaly rate with ~1,091 packets/s throughput, while the GA attains 0.62% average anomaly rate with organic per-class variance (0.00%-2.50%) at ~5.7 packets/s. Both methods successfully amplify the 5-sample ARP Spoofing category by 200x to 1,000 validated packets. The ~190:1 throughput ratio between methods, combined with their complementary quality profiles, provides evidence-based selection criteria for deployment contexts ranging from rapid dataset augmentation to adversarial robustness testing.

</details>


### [164] [Can LLMs Reason About Brand Ownership? An Empirical Study of Domain Attribution Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20868)
*Fathima Mashood,Mohamed Nabeel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估了大型语言模型（LLM）在品牌情报任务中的表现，涵盖域名枚举、开放域品牌归属和所有权分类三项任务。实验表明，LLM在从记忆中枚举品牌域名时可达82%的精确率，但在无外部工具辅助的所有权验证中表现不佳（宏F1最高仅0.37）。引入WHOIS信息后，所有权分类宏F1提升最多0.65，精确率高达0.99，显著降低了防御方的误报风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 防御者在面对与知名品牌相似的新域名时，无法区分是攻击者注册的钓鱼域名还是品牌自身注册的合法域名（无论是防御性注册还是新产品/服务上线）。错误地将品牌自有域名标记为恶意会产生误报，损害终端用户和品牌声誉。解决这一模糊性需要品牌情报能力，即大规模判断给定域名是否属于某品牌的能力。

Method: 系统评估了四种LLM（Gemini 2.5 Flash、Gemini 3.5 Flash、Claude Sonnet 4.5、Claude Sonnet 4.6）在三种品牌情报任务上的表现：域名枚举（Q1）、开放域品牌归属（Q2）、二元所有权分类（Q3）。实验覆盖36个最常被仿冒的品牌，在四种检索设置（上下文内、网络搜索、WHOIS查询、组合）下进行评测。

Result: 模型从记忆中枚举品牌域名精确率最高达82%；无外部工具的所有权分类宏F1最高仅0.37；引入WHOIS后Q3宏F1提升最多0.65，精确率高达0.99。

Conclusion: LLM在品牌情报中表现出明显的二分性：记忆驱动的域名枚举表现良好，但所有权验证高度依赖外部工具。WHOIS增强可大幅降低误报风险。研究为防御者在品牌保护流水线中部署LLM提供了具体建议。

Abstract: When a new domain resembling a popular brand appears, defenders face a fundamental ambiguity: it may be an attacker-created squatting site for phishing, or it may be a domain the brand itself registered, either defensively, to block attackers, or legitimately, for a new product or service launch. Incorrectly flagging a brand-owned domain as malicious produces a false positive that harms end users and damages the brand's reputation. Resolving this ambiguity requires brand intelligence: the ability to determine, at scale, whether a given domain belongs to a brand. Large language models (LLMs), with their broad knowledge of brand domain relationships, offer a promising zero configuration approach to this problem, but their reliability for brand intelligence tasks remains unknown. We present the first systematic empirical evaluation of LLM brand intelligence across three tasks: domain enumeration (Q1), open ended brand attribution (Q2), and binary ownership classification (Q3). We evaluate four models, Gemini 2.5 Flash, Gemini 3.5 Flash, Claude Sonnet 4.5, and Claude Sonnet 4.6, across four retrieval settings (in context, web search, WHOIS lookup, and combined) on 36 of the most phished brands. Our results reveal a stark dichotomy: models achieve up to 82% precision enumerating brand domains from memory alone, yet fail at ownership verification without external tools, with macro F1 at most 0.37 in ICL mode. WHOIS augmentation lifts Q3 macro F1 by up to 0.65 points, yielding near perfect precision (<= 0.99), dramatically reducing the false positive risk for defenders. We provide concrete recommendations for deploying LLMs in brand protection pipelines.

</details>


### [165] [Whose Agent Are You? Multi-Layer Fingerprinting and Attribution of Autonomous Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20910)
*Dayeon Kang,Hyejun Jeong,Jade Sheffey,Pubali Datta,Amir Houmansadr*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出基于网络层（TLS/HTTP）和浏览器行为层的多层级指纹识别方法，可高精度（97%准确率）区分AI网页代理、人类用户和传统爬虫，为内容保护和网络安全策略执行提供抗规避的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI网页代理结合大语言模型与自主浏览器控制，导致无差别的数据抓取成为隐私和安全挑战。现有防御手段（如robots.txt、主动机器人拦截）因易被违反和规避而不足，亟需更有效的区分与防护机制。

Method: 构建多层级指纹机制：在网络层提取TLS/HTTP特征，在应用层分析浏览器交互行为。对六个主流代理框架（AutoGen、Browser Use、Claude、Gemini、Operator、Skyvern）进行流量分析，发现其HTTP请求组装、TLS/HTTP连接建立及自主浏览器行为中的潜在结构差异，并采用决策树分类器进行识别。

Result: 决策树分类器在多层特征上达到97%的识别准确率，能有效区分不同代理架构、人类浏览基线及传统爬虫流量。

Conclusion: 跨层代理追踪为内容保护和网络安全策略执行提供了稳健、抗规避的有效方案。

Abstract: As AI web agents proliferate, combining large language models with autonomous, browser-level control, indiscriminate content scraping by web agents has emerged as a privacy and security challenge. Existing defenses, such as robots.txt and active bot-blocking, are insufficient, as they are widely violated and easily circumvented. In this work, we demonstrate that AI web agents can be effectively distinguished from humans and traditional crawlers using a multi-layer fingerprint based on both network layer characteristics (e.g., TLS, HTTP) and browser interaction behavior. We implement this mechanism as a programmatic logging framework that can be deployed on a live, instrumented domain.
  By analyzing six prominent agent frameworks (AutoGen, Browser Use, Claude, Gemini, Operator, and Skyvern), we uncover latent structural differences in how these systems assemble HTTP requests, establish TLS/HTTP connections, and execute autonomous browser actions. Feeding these multi-layer features into a decision tree classifier, our framework achieves high-fidelity identification (97% accuracy), successfully isolating distinct agent architectures and differentiating agent traffic from both human browsing baselines and legacy crawlers. Our findings demonstrate that cross-layer agent tracking provides a robust, evasion-resistant strategy for content protection and web security policy enforcement.

</details>


### [166] [Think Twice Before You Act: Protecting LLM Agents Against Tool Description Poisoning via Isolated Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20922)
*Shanghao Shi,Xiao Wang,Chaoyu Zhang,Hao Li,Wenjing Lou,Thomas Hou,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik,Chongjie Zhang,Ning Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM智能体面临的跨工具描述投毒攻击，发现现有提示注入防御迁移性差，并提出基于隔离规划概念的系统级防御Tool-Guard，通过将可疑工具调用隔离至受影响列表来阻断毒化描述的影响，在AgentDojo和ASB基准上显著降低攻击成功率同时保持高任务效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外部工具集成扩展了LLM智能体能力的同时引入了新的攻击面，跨工具描述投毒攻击能通过操纵工具元数据来引导智能体轨迹，而现有防御对此新兴威胁的效果尚不明确。

Method: 提出Tool-Guard防御方法，核心是基于隔离规划（isolated planning）概念：检测到异常或可疑的工具调用后，将该工具放入隔离列表（influenced list），阻断毒化描述的持续影响，同时允许工具继续用于支持任务。

Result: 在AgentDojo和ASB基准测试上，Tool-Guard显著降低攻击成功率，同时保持较高的任务效用。

Conclusion: 现有提示注入防御对跨工具描述投毒迁移性差；毒化描述在规划上下文中持续存在是攻击成功的关键；Tool-Guard通过隔离规划实现了鲁棒的防御，兼顾安全性与工具可用性。

Abstract: The integration of external tools has substantially expanded the capabilities of large language model (LLM) agents, but it also introduces new attack surfaces beyond prompt injection. In particular, cross-tool description poisoning can manipulate planner-visible tool metadata to steer an agent's trajectory, even if the poisoned tool itself is never chosen. To understand the effectiveness of existing defenses against this emerging threat, we first evaluate several prompt-injection defenses and find that they transfer poorly to cross-tool description poisoning. A key observation is that poisoned descriptions persist in the planning context across steps, enabling continuous influence over subsequent tool choices. Building on this insight, we propose Tool-Guard, a novel system-level defense based on a new concept called isolated planning, in which tool invocations that are detected as misaligned or suspicious cause the corresponding tool to be placed in a quarantined list (the influenced list), breaking further influence from poisoned descriptions. With this influence isolated, the tool can continue to be used to support the task, enabling a robust defense that preserves legitimate tool utility. Experiments on the AgentDojo and ASB benchmarks show that Tool-Guard substantially reduces attack success while maintaining high task utility. Our code is available at https://github.com/shishishi123/Tool-Guard.

</details>


### [167] [Quantifying the Impact of Stealthy BLE Spam & Flooding Attacks on IoT Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21016)
*Usman Rauf,Adalynn Martinez,Fadi Mohsen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了BLE协议在IoMT（特别是军事医疗系统）中的能效设计及其面临的安全威胁——BLE泛洪攻击。首次公开了基于树莓派的攻击概念验证，并扩展到Flipper Zero等商用平台。作者提出了基于敏捷性的量化分析框架和威慑策略，通过提高攻击成本来抵御此类攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BLE协议在军事医疗和战场可穿戴传感器等IoMT场景广泛应用，但其高效发现机制导致攻击面扩大，易受BLE泛洪攻击（DoS）。低成本商用设备（Flipper Zero、HackRF等）降低了攻击门槛，在对抗环境中可造成严重破坏，亟需定量分析和有效防御策略。

Method: 建立定量分析框架评估BLE泛洪攻击的影响，提出基于敏捷性（agility）的威慑策略，通过动态调整通信参数提高攻击成本。

Result: 首次公开实现基于树莓派的BLE泛洪攻击概念验证，并成功适配到Flipper Zero、HackRF、Android等商用平台。提出了量化攻击影响的方法论和基于敏捷性的防御框架。

Conclusion: BLE泛洪攻击在军事IoMT环境中构成严重威胁，低成本的商用设备使攻击更易实施。基于敏捷性的防御策略能有效提高攻击成本，为对抗环境下的BLE通信安全提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: The energy-efficient design of the BLE protocol, emphasis on rapid, and userfriendly discovery, making it an ideal choice for IoMTs, specifically, military field medical systems, and battlefield wearable sensors. Especially in active conflict zones, when static medical facilities are vulnerable and often targeted, limiting their viability for sustained care delivery. This rapid deployment, and ease of management comes at the cost of expanded attack surface, i.e., BLE flooding attacks. During such attacks, adversaries flood advertisement channels with unauthorized connection or advertising requests to exhaust nearby device resources and disrupt legitimate communication, sometimes culminating in denial-of-service conditions. A first public proof-of-concept of such attacks, using a Raspberry Pi has since been adapted to commodity platforms (e.g., Flipper Zero, HackRF, Android), lowering the barrier to attack. In contested environments, such platforms are directly relevant to adversarial RF jamming and spoofing operations, where low-cost, portable devices can induce disproportionate disruption in dense wireless ecosystems. In this work, we develop a quantitative foundation for understanding the impact of such attacks and propose a practical deterrence strategy based on agility to raise the cost of such attacks.

</details>


### [168] [Honeyquest for LLMs: Rethinking Cyber Deception for AI Attackers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21037)
*Kerri Prinos,Lilianne Brush,Cameron Denton*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出自动化评估框架，对21个LLM（共10,962条响应）与47名人类基线进行对比实验，发现LLM在三个关键维度上构成独特攻击者类别：LLM被骗陷阱率显著高于人类、人类注意转移效应在LLM中缺失、73.4%的情况下LLM虽能识别陷阱但仍会利用欺骗元素，表明以人为中心的欺骗假设无法可靠转移至AI攻击者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络欺骗的实证基础依赖以人为中心的假设，但自主AI攻击者的快速涌现挑战了这一基础是否适用于AI智能体，因此需要系统评估LLM作为攻击者时的行为特征是否与人类一致。

Method: 基于Honeyquest工具改编的自动化评估框架，构建包含21个LLM（覆盖10家提供商、多种架构和专业、开源/闭源、8B至超1T参数规模）的测试集，在174个侦查查询上产生10,962条响应，与47名人类基线进行对比。

Result: (1) 所有LLM模型落入欺骗陷阱的比率均显著高于人类；(2) 人类中存在的注意力转移防御效应在LLM中统计上缺失；(3) 存在关键的认识-行动差距：LLM在推理文本中能成功识别陷阱，但73.4%的情况下仍利用欺骗元素。21个模型中，推理文本中的陷阱识别与落入陷阱行为无显著相关（Spearman r=+0.08, p=0.73）。

Conclusion: 以人为中心的欺骗假设无法可靠地迁移至AI攻击者，亟需针对AI原生主动防御框架的新研究。

Abstract: The empirical foundation of cyber deception relies on human-centered hypotheses, but the rapid emergence of autonomous, AI-enabled attackers challenges whether this foundation transfers to AI agents. To address this, we introduce an automated evaluation framework adapted from the Honeyquest instrument to assess LLM attacker judgment at scale. Our 21-LLM cohort spanned 10 providers, diverse architectures and specializations, open- and closed-weight models, and parameter scales from 8B to over 1T. We evaluated the performance of this LLM cohort (yielding 10,962 responses) against the 47-participant human baseline across an identical set of 174 reconnaissance queries. Our empirical evaluation reveals three key findings that establish LLMs as a distinct attacker class: (1) every model in our cohort falls for deceptive traps at a significantly higher rate than human attackers; (2) the defensive attention-diversion effect observed in humans is statistically absent in our LLM cohort; and (3) a critical recognition-action gap, where LLMs successfully articulate trap recognition in their reasoning but exploit the deceptive elements anyway 73.4\% of the time. Across the 21 models, trap recognition in reasoning text did not predict fell-for-trap behavior (Spearman $r = +0.08$, $p = 0.73$). Ultimately, these findings demonstrate that human-centered deception hypotheses do not reliably transfer to AI attackers, highlighting the critical need for new research into AI-native active defense frameworks.

</details>


### [169] [OVIG: Optimistic Verification of AI Training Integrity via Gradient Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21045)
*Hongxu Su,Jianzhu Yao,Huan Zhang,Xuechao Wang,Pramod Viswanath*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: OVIG是一个乐观验证框架，通过基于异构重放校准的梯度差异经验边界来审计外包的AI后训练过程，结合乐观采样和步长参数s，在保持0%攻击成功率的同时大幅降低存储和传输开销（s=2000时降低1996倍），总系统开销仅为1.143倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外包AI后训练存在训练完整性缺口：模型所有者收到检查点、日志和聚合指标，但无法直接证明声明的训练轨迹被忠实执行。不可信的提供商可能为了节省计算或引入安全风险而偏离预定轨迹。由于异构加速器上的浮点执行存在良性数值漂移，难以区分诚实重放差异与完整性违规。

Method: OVIG采用乐观验证框架，从诚实异构重放中校准梯度差异的经验边界，对开区间进行边界检查，并结合乐观采样与步长参数s（将训练划分为步长对齐区间，仅保留区间端点证据）。

Result: 在捷径训练攻击和定向操纵攻击上，OVIG在语言、视觉和扩散工作负载中保持0%攻击成功率。在Qwen3上，步长从s=1增加到s=2000，链下存储和证据传输减少1996倍，同时保持0% ASR；该设置下总系统开销仅为无验证训练的1.143倍。

Conclusion: OVIG为异构执行环境下的外包AI后训练提供了实用的完整性保障层。

Abstract: The rapid growth of AI has increased the demand for domain-specific post-training, while the cost and specialization of accelerator infrastructure push many model owners to outsource this process. Outsourced training lowers operational barriers, but creates a training-integrity gap: the owner receives a checkpoint, logs, and aggregate metrics without direct evidence that the declared training trajectory was faithfully executed. An untrusted provider may have incentives to deviate from that trajectory, either to save computation or to introduce targeted security risks. Auditing such deviations is difficult because floating-point execution on heterogeneous accelerators introduces benign numerical drift, making it hard to distinguish honest replay differences from integrity violations. Existing verification methods either observe training at too coarse a granularity or impose costs and deployment constraints that are impractical at scale. We present OVIG, an optimistic verification framework that audits outsourced post-training using an empirical boundary on gradient differences calibrated from honest heterogeneous replays. OVIG checks opened intervals against this boundary and combines optimistic sampling with a stride parameter $s$, which partitions training into stride-aligned intervals and retains only interval-endpoint evidence. Across shortcut training attacks and targeted manipulation attacks, OVIG maintains $0\%$ ASR on language, vision, and diffusion workloads. On Qwen3, increasing the stride from $s=1$ to $s=2000$ reduces off-chain storage and evidence transmission by $1996\times$ while preserving $0\%$ ASR; at this setting, OVIG incurs only $1.143\times$ total system overhead relative to training without verification. These results show that OVIG provides a practical integrity layer for outsourced AI post-training under heterogeneous execution.

</details>


### [170] [DEFENGRAPH: Knowledge Graph-Enhanced LLMs for Blue Team Cyber Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21059)
*Zhen Wang,Kristen Moore,Qin Wang,Guangsheng Yu,Minjune Kim,Diksha Goel,Gang Li,Ahmed Ibrahim,Ahmad Mohsin,Helge Janicke*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DEFENGRAPH是一个基于LLM的网络安全防御助手，通过双层次静态-动态知识图谱和基于图的路径检索、上下文过滤与重排序，显著提升了LLM在网络安全事件响应中的推理召回率和操作召回率，在多个主流LLM上取得了新的最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在网络安全决策中存在幻觉、时间推理能力差、系统上下文缺乏深度理解等问题，限制了其在高风险、时间动态演化的网络安全环境中的可靠性。

Method: 提出DEFENGRAPH框架，集成双层次静态-动态知识图谱，结合基于图的路径检索、LLM驱动的上下文过滤和基于推理的重排序，将LLM输出锚定于长期领域知识和动态事件上下文中。

Result: 在GPT-4o上，推理召回率从61.45%提升至73.49%，工单操作召回率从52.17%提升至72.46%；在LLaMA-3、DeepSeek-R1、QWen-3上也取得一致提升；正确防御动作数量达50个（最优基线为36个），且错误率保持稳定。

Conclusion: DEFENGRAPH通过在知识图谱中融合长期领域知识与动态事件上下文，有效增强了LLM在网络安全防御中的时序感知和因果推理能力，证明了结合结构化知识表示与LLM推理的可行性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise for supporting decision-making in cybersecurity, but their reliability in high-stakes, time-evolving environments remains limited due to hallucinations, poor temporal reasoning, and shallow grounding in system context. We introduce DEFENGRAPH, an LLM-driven assistant designed to support human defenders during cybersecurity incidents. DEFENGRAPH improves contextual reasoning by integrating a dual-layer Static-Dynamic Knowledge Graph (KG) with graph-based path retrieval, LLM-driven contextual filtering, and reasoning-based re-ranking. The framework grounds LLM outputs in both long-term domain knowledge and evolving event context, enabling faithful and temporally aware decision support. We evaluate DEFENGRAPH in a cyber defense setting using knowledge graphs constructed from heterogeneous security artifacts, including SIEM alerts, system topology, attacker behaviors, and prior defensive actions. The evaluation uses data collected during live Red vs. Blue team cyber range exercises simulating attacks on critical infrastructure, which generate realistic and noisy datasets reflecting real-world defender workflows and system dynamics. Evaluations across four prevalent LLMs show that DEFENGRAPH sets a new state-of-the-art: on GPT-4o it boosts reasoning-recall from 61.45\% to 73.49\% and ticket-action recall from 52.17% to 72.46% (precision 24.49\% to 29.24\%), with similar gains on LLaMA-3 (46.99\% to 61.45\%), DeepSeek-R1 (45.78\% to 56.63\%) and QWen-3 (51.81\% to 59.04\%), while surfacing up to 50 correct defense actions versus 36 for the next best baseline and holding fault rates steady.

</details>


### [171] [A Blockchain Consensus Mechanism for Distributed Electricity Trading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21060)
*Shanglin Yang,Guo Chen,Shiping Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Proof of Energy (PoE)共识机制，通过将区块链出块权与真实能源贡献挂钩，实现分布式能源系统的去中心化协调、公平结算、可信运行和系统优化，无需中心化机构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式电力系统在规模化过程中面临去中心化协调、公平经济结算、可信运行和系统优化四大挑战，且缺乏中心化权威机构来统筹解决这些问题。

Method: 提出PoE区块链共识机制：引入能量贡献单元(ECU)模型，通过稀缺性加权归一化将异构能源服务映射为统一价值度量；基于可验证随机函数(VRF)的提议机制确保节点被选中的概率严格正比于其贡献值。

Result: 通过案例研究从电网协调、激励公平性和优化效率三个维度验证了PoE的有效性。

Conclusion: PoE构建了一个密码学安全、激励兼容的去中心化价值分配框架，为分布式能源系统提供了无需中心化权威的可信运行基础。

Abstract: Distributed power systems complement centralized grids by coordinating distributed energy resources (DERs) to achieve regional energy self-sufficiency. Scaling such systems raises four persistent challenges: decentralized coordination, fair economic settlement, trustworthy operation, and system optimization, all without a central authority. This paper proposes Proof of Energy (PoE), a blockchain consensus mechanism that addresses these challenges through cryptographically secured, contribution-proportional node selection. In PoE, block generation rights are tied directly to real-world energy contributions, enabling distributed consensus without centralized dispatch. An Energy Contribution Unit (ECU) model is introduced to map heterogeneous energy services onto a unified value metric via scarcity-weighted normalization. A Verifiable Random Function (VRF)-based proposal mechanism then ensures selection probability is strictly proportional to node contribution, preserving fairness and resisting manipulation. Case studies validate PoE across three dimensions: grid coordination, incentive fairness, and optimization efficiency. The result is a cryptographically secured, incentive-compatible framework for decentralized value distribution in energy systems.

</details>


### [172] [Snatcher: Apple Find My Network Exposes Your Lost Devices To Strangers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21067)
*Zhenyu Ren,Yanbo Zhang,Boya Liu,Mo Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Apple Find My网络由于BLE广播未加密、声学触发未认证及MAC地址随机化缓慢等设计缺陷，导致丢失设备可被未授权发现和物理盗窃。研究者开发了全Android平台的Snatcher攻击框架，通过声音定向、RSSI-IMU融合导航和时空聚类三个层次，实现了对丢失Apple配件的物理追踪与定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Apple Find My网络连接近十亿设备，但其BLE广告协议的安全性尚未被充分评估。研究者希望揭示隐私保护、反跟踪设计与物理安全之间的冲突，并促使Apple加强防御。

Method: 开发Snatcher攻击框架，完全基于Android智能手机实现，无需专用硬件。攻击分三个层次：基于声音的定向发现、RSSI-IMU传感器融合导航、以及时空聚类分析，实现对丢失Apple设备的物理追踪与定位。

Result: 在实际测试中成功实现了对丢失Apple配件和设备的物理追踪与定位，验证了BLE广告未加密、声学触发未认证和MAC地址随机化缓慢三个安全漏洞的可利用性。

Conclusion: Apple在Find My网络设计中存在隐私保护与物理安全之间的根本性冲突，亟需加强防御措施以防止未授权发现和物理盗窃。

Abstract: Apple's Find My network connects nearly one billion devices to locate missing property via Bluetooth Low Energy (BLE). This paper reveals that insecure BLE advertisements and design tradeoffs allow unauthorized discovery and physical theft of lost Apple devices. We develop Snatcher, an attack and analysis framework implemented fully on Android smartphones without specialized hardware. Snatcher identifies vulnerabilities in unencrypted BLE advertisements, unauthenticated acoustic triggers, and slow MAC address randomization. Through three levels - sound-based direction finding, RSSI-IMU sensor-fusion navigation, and spatial-temporal clustering - our Android-based platform physically tracks and locates lost Apple accessories and devices in real-world tests. Our results highlight a crucial conflict between privacy protection, anti-stalking design, and physical security, urging Apple to strengthen Find My defenses.

</details>


### [173] [Local LLM Agents as Vulnerable Runtimes:A Source-Code Audit of the Agent Runtime Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21071)
*Zhengsong Zhang,Zongze Li,Jiawei Guo,Haipeng Cai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CLAWAUDIT，一个针对本地LLM智能体运行时的静态审计框架。该框架基于STRIDE推导出五类漏洞分类法，开发了47条Semgrep YAML规则和30条CodeQL查询，并在包含446个源码级公告的OPENCLAWBENCH基准上进行评估。在保留测试集上，Semgrep召回率从21.7%提升至66.8%，CodeQL召回率从13.8%提升至75.1%，且训练/测试差距在4个百分点以内，表明规则具有良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注提示注入、恶意技能、市场风险或黑盒评估，但本地LLM智能体的实现层——包括提示构建器、解析器、工具调度器、技能加载器、内存写入器、网络客户端和权限门——作为安全边界尚未得到审查，没有前人工作对智能体的源代码树进行审计以检测实现级安全弱点。
