Method: CLAWAUDIT从STRIDE威胁模型推导出五类漏洞分类法，针对智能体特有模式开发自定义静态分析规则（47条Semgrep YAML规则和30条CodeQL查询），构建了包含446个OpenClaw仓库源码级公告的OPENCLAWBENCH基准，并按时间顺序划分为229条规则推导（训练集）和217条保留测试集。

Result: 在保留测试集上，Semgrep召回率从21.7%（Pro基线）提升至66.8%，CodeQL召回率从13.8%（security-extended）提升至75.1%。所有四种配置的训练/测试差距均在4个百分点以内。初步的实时代码审计表明，这些面向召回率的规则需要手动分类，提示需要语义过滤才能投入生产部署。

Conclusion: CLAWAUDIT证明了针对本地LLM智能体运行时的实现级安全审计是可行且有效的，规则具有良好的泛化能力，但需结合语义过滤以降低误报率才能用于生产环境。

Abstract: Local LLM agents such as OpenClaw and Nanobot run on end-user machines and act on host resources - the shell, filesystem, browser, stored credentials, and messaging applications - through natural-language goals. These agents have become privileged software runtimes that mediate between user intent, model outputs, and host-level actions. Existing research characterizes the landscape through prompt injection, malicious skills, marketplace risks, or black-box evaluation of agents. But the implementation layer that performs this mediation, the prompt builder, parser, tool dispatcher, skill loader, memory writer, network client, and permission gate, has remained an unexamined safety boundary. To our knowledge, no prior work has examined the agent's source tree to audit these components for implementation-level security weaknesses. We present CLAWAUDIT, a static auditing framework for measuring vulnerability exposure in local LLM agent runtimes. CLAWAUDIT derives a five-category vulnerability taxonomy from STRIDE and develops custom static-analysis rules that target agent-specific patterns absent from established rule sets for vulnerability analysis. We instantiate the taxonomy in two backends, 47 Semgrep YAML rules and 30 CodeQL queries, and evaluate on OPENCLAWBENCH, a benchmark of 446 source-code-level advisories from the OpenClaw repository and split temporally into 229 rule-derivation (train) and 217 held-out (test) advisories. On the held-out test, CLAWAUDIT raises Semgrep recall from 21.7% (Pro baseline) to 66.8%, and CodeQL recall from 13.8% (security-extended) to 75.1%. Train/test gaps remain within 4 percentage points for all four configurations, indicating that the rules generalize to vulnerabilities unseen during rule writing. A preliminary live-code audit shows that these recall-oriented rules require manual triage, motivating semantic filtering before production deployment.

</details>


### [174] [OTTER: A Red-Teaming System for Toxicity-Evading Jailbreak Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21077)
*Jerry Wang,Hsin-Ling Hsu,Yi-Cheng Lai,Nai-Chia Chen,Fang Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出 OTTER 框架，仅需替换最少5个token即可解耦表面毒性与对抗意图，将GPT模型的攻击成功率从7.0%提升至84.0%，并首次定量分析毒性-绕过关系，为生产部署中的分类器加固提供建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生产级LLM依赖基于毒性的内容过滤器作为主要防线，但该方法的隐含假设——有害意图与毒性表面措辞相关——是脆弱的。需要证明这种假设可以被轻易打破，并推动更鲁棒的防御机制。

Method: 提出OTTER（Obfuscated Toxicity-Evading Token Evolution for Rewriting）框架：一种黑盒红队攻击方法，仅需标准API访问，通过进化算法替换最少5个token，解耦表面毒性与对抗意图，绕过毒性检测。

Result: 在457个AdvBench提示、4个GPT模型上，平均攻击成功率（ASR）从7.0%提升至84.0%；首次定量分析了毒性-绕过关系并给出按类别细分的表现。

Conclusion: 毒性过滤器的假设是脆弱的，攻击者可通过极小改动绕过；研究结果可转化为生产部署中分类器加固的可操作建议。

Abstract: Production LLMs increasingly rely on toxicity-based moderation filters as a primary defense, assuming that harmful intent correlates with toxic surface wording. We show this assumption is fundamentally brittle: surface toxicity and adversarial intent can be decoupled by replacing as few as five tokens. We present OTTER (Obfuscated Toxicity-Evading Token Evolution for Rewriting), a black-box red-teaming framework requiring only standard API access, directly targeting the practical constraints of industry security audits. Evaluated on 457 AdvBench prompts across four GPT models, OTTER raises average ASR from 7.0% to 84.0%. We further provide the first quantitative analysis of the toxicity--bypass relationship and a per-category breakdown, translating our findings into actionable recommendations for classifier hardening in production deployments.

</details>


### [175] [AgenticOS: An Intent-Oriented Secure Operating System Architecture for Autonomous AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21129)
*Zhen Zhao,Yu Zhang,Yanpeng Zhu,Jia Wang,Songqiao Tao,Xin Cheng,Jiexin Gao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对LLM驱动自主代理面临的提示注入等安全威胁，提出AgenticOS——一种面向意图的操作系统安全架构，将OS从资源管理者重构为意图过滤器，通过四层架构和意图ABI实现最小权限、强制审计和信息流约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于"资源暴露+权限检查"的OS安全模型无法应对LLM代理的规划、工具使用、网络访问和代码执行等能力组合带来的安全挑战，一旦运行时被提示注入或恶意工具输出攻陷，攻击者可组合POSIX资源原语超越用户授权执行恶意行为。

Method: 提出四层架构：Ghost Kernel（幽灵内核）、Logic Shutter（逻辑快门）、Agent Capsule（代理胶囊）和Semantic Boundary Gateway（语义边界网关），配合Intent ABI（意图ABI）、Manifest-Only Runtime（仅清单运行时）、基于Weaver的能力生成，以及面向AgenticOS原生技能（Skills）的准入模型。核心思路是将OS从资源管理者重构为意图过滤器，代理提交结构化意图声明，系统合成最小特权环境并施加强制中介、审计和信息流约束。

Result: 提出了一套完整的面向意图的安全OS架构设计，将可委托、可审计的软件能力整合为OS原生能力，而非替换所有应用。

Conclusion: AgenticOS通过意图导向的安全架构，从根本上解决了LLM驱动代理的安全问题，为未来自主代理系统提供了更安全的操作系统级保护方案。

Abstract: Traditional OS security models based on "resource exposure plus permission checks" face structural challenges as LLM-driven autonomous agents acquire capabilities for planning, tool use, network access, and code execution. Once an agent runtime is compromised through prompt injection or malicious tool outputs, an attacker can compose POSIX-style resource primitives into behaviors far beyond the user's task authorization. To address this, we propose AgenticOS, an intent-oriented secure OS architecture that consolidates delegable, auditable software capabilities into OS-native ones rather than replacing all applications. The core insight is to reframe the OS from a "resource manager" into an "intent filter": instead of requesting low-level resources directly, agents submit structured intent declarations, from which the system synthesizes a least-privilege environment with mandatory mediation, auditing, and information-flow constraints. At the implementation level, we introduce a four-layer architecture -- Ghost Kernel, Logic Shutter, Agent Capsule, and Semantic Boundary Gateway -- together with the Intent ABI, Manifest-Only Runtime, Weaver-based capability generation, and an admission model for AgenticOS-native Skills.

</details>


### [176] [Impact Analysis of Speech Representation Learning Models for Acoustic Side-Channel Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21210)
*Nitin Choudhury,Vikrant Vikram Pratap Maurya,Arun Balaji Budhuru,Orchid Chetia Phukan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出KEYAC数据集，研究语音表征学习模型在键盘声学侧信道攻击（ASCA）中的泛化能力，发现标准微调架构在VoIP编解码器场景下表现不佳，并提出使用KAN进行微调，取得了新的最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音表征学习模型在键盘声学侧信道攻击（ASCA）中的应用潜力尚未被探索，特别是在不同编解码器条件下的泛化能力问题亟待解决。

Method: 构建KEYAC数据集，涵盖标准与VoIP编解码器场景；评估六种表征学习模型在零样本与部分微调设置下的表现；提出使用Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KAN)替代传统全连接和卷积网络进行微调，以更好地建模非线性特征交互。

Result: 部分微调能提升性能，但模型在VoIP编解码器间泛化能力差；KAN-based微调在所有基线方法中表现最优，在KEYAC上达到新的最优结果。

Conclusion: KAN能够更有效地建模非线性特征交互，从而提升ASCA模型在跨编解码器场景下的泛化性能。

Abstract: Acoustic side-channel attacks (ASCA) on keyboards have gained increasing attention, yet impact of speech representation learning models in ASCA remains unexplored. Addressing this, we introduce KEYAC, a dataset designed to analyze representation generalization for ASCA under both standard and VoIP codec settings. On KEYAC, we evaluate six representation learning models under zero-shot and partial fine-tuning settings using fully connected and convolutional networks. Results show that while partial fine-tuning improves performance, models struggle to generalize across VoIP codecs. We hypothesize this limitation stems from inadequate modeling of nonlinear feature interactions in conventional fine-tuning architectures. To address this, we employ Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KAN) for fine-tuning. Empirical results show that KAN-based fine-tuning consistently outperforms the baselines and establishes a new state-of-the-art on KEYAC.

</details>


### [177] [DIPBox: A Multi-scale Testing Framework for Tracking Dataset Regeneration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21240)
*Tian Dong,Yan Meng,Shaofeng Li,Guoxing Chen,Yuling Chen,Zhen Liu,Haojin Zhu,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出DIPBox，首个通过多尺度特征相似性测试来追踪数据集再生（未经授权复制）的检测框架，在16个基础数据集和320个再生数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御主要关注单个数据点追踪，忽视了数据集再生（整体或部分复制）的威胁。通过真实肿瘤数据集的测量研究，发现存在大量部分数据集复制现象，引发许可证合规性问题。

Method: 关键洞察：保持模型效用的再生必然保留跨多特征尺度的可测量信号。将数据集特征分为样本级、集合级和分布级三个层次，设计四种相似度指标。开发DIPBox框架，支持从有限到完全信息的多种防御者访问设置下的多尺度相似性测试。并提供学习理论分析证明多尺度度量的合理性及效用-散度权衡的内在限制。

Result: 在16个视觉和文本基础数据集、320个再生数据集和590个衍生模型上的实验表明，DIPBox优于现有方案，并在三种自适应攻击下验证了其鲁棒性和局限性。

Conclusion: DIPBox是首个追踪数据集再生的测试框架，通过多尺度特征相似性有效检测未知的对抗性再生，同时揭示了效用与散度之间的基本权衡，为数据集版权保护提供了新思路。

Abstract: Training datasets have tremendous proprietary value and are vulnerable to unauthorized copying. Existing defenses mainly focus on tracking individual data points, but pay little attention to the threat of dataset regeneration. Through a measurement study of public tumor datasets, we identify substantial real-world partial-dataset replication, raising concerns about potential license noncompliance. To counter the challenge of tracking previously unknown adversarial regeneration, our key insight is that regeneration that preserves model utility inevitably preserves measurable signals across multiple feature scales. We categorize these dataset features into sample-, set-, and distribution-level features and design four similarity metrics to accurately identify regeneration. Based on these metrics, we develop DIPBox, which to our knowledge is the first testing framework that tracks regeneration suspects via multi-scale similarity testing across a spectrum of defender access settings, from limited to full information. We further provide a learning-theoretic analysis that justifies these multi-scale metrics and formalizes an inherent utility--divergence trade-off, implying fundamental limits on evasive regeneration. Extensive experiments on 16 vision and text base datasets, 320 regenerated datasets, and 590 derived models validate that DIPBox outperforms previous solutions while characterizing its robustness and limits under three adaptive attacks.

</details>


### [178] [Differential Zonotopes for Verifying Global Robustness of DNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21282)
*Anagha Athavale,Samuel Teuber,Matteo Maffei,Ezio Bartocci,Dejan Nickovic,Georg Weissenbacher*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于差分光环区间的静态分析技术，用于验证深度神经网络的全局鲁棒性，并通过新工具TwoSafe在精度和可扩展性上显著超越现有方法，能验证比先前技术大一个数量级的网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局鲁棒性比局部鲁棒性更强但更难验证，现有方法在处理大规模网络时面临精度和可扩展性瓶颈，且缺乏对低置信度预测情景的合理鲁棒性定义。

Method: 提出差分光环区间（differential halo zonotopes）这一新型抽象域，将zonotope扩展至联合传播成对的扰动输入，同时紧密约束其分歧；引入对称置信度全局鲁棒性松弛，忽略导致低置信度差异预测的扰动。

Result: 在标准DNN验证基准测试上，TwoSafe在精度和可扩展性上均显著优于现有技术，能够验证比先前方法大一个数量级的网络。

Conclusion: 差分光环区间为全局鲁棒性验证提供了一种有效的静态分析方法，对称置信度鲁棒性为实际应用提供了更有意义的松弛定义。

Abstract: The robustness of deep neural networks (DNNs) is critical in security-sensitive applications, where small input perturbations should not alter model predictions. This property is commonly formalized as local or global robustness: the former considers perturbations around a single input, while the latter -- strictly stronger -- quantifies over all input pairs. While local robustness can be expressed as a safety property, global robustness is a 2-safety property, making it substantially more challenging to verify. We present a novel static analysis technique for verifying the global robustness of DNNs. Our approach is based on differential halo zonotopes, a new abstract domain that extends zonotopes to jointly propagate pairs of perturbed inputs in lock-step while tightly bounding their divergence. In addition, we introduce a symmetric variant of confidence-based global robustness that disregards perturbations leading to differing but low-confidence predictions. This relaxation yields a practically meaningful notion of robustness that applies to a broader class of networks. We implement our approach in a new tool, called TwoSafe, and evaluate it on standard DNN verification benchmarks, including widely deployed models. Our results show that TwoSafe significantly outperforms the state of the art in both precision and scalability, enabling the verification of networks an order of magnitude larger than those handled by prior techniques.

</details>


### [179] ["What Happens Locally, Leaks Globally": Detecting Privacy Leakage Risks in MCP Servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21338)
*Biwei Yan,Minghui Xu,Yijun Yang,Boyang Ma,Xuelong Dai,Jingku Li,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 MCPPrivacyDetector，一个针对 MCP 协议服务器的跨语言静态分析框架，用于检测由协议本身引发的隐私泄露（如 API 密钥、PII 等）。在 10,655 个真实 MCP 服务器上发现泄漏率超过 10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP 协议已成为 LLM 连接外部资源的事实标准，但其协议特性导致凭证、API 密钥、PII 等敏感数据在工具处理函数中因返回、日志记录或异常抛出而跨越本地/LLM 边界泄露，现有工具难以检测此类协议诱导的隐私风险。

Method: 提出 MCPPrivacyDetector，一个上下文感知的跨语言静态分析框架。首先将多语言（如 Python）实现的异构代码提升为统一程序表示；然后应用上下文感知的语义过滤来隔离真正敏感的值和协议特定的隐式汇点（如 @mcp.tool 处理函数）；最后执行污点分析以枚举可行数据流。

Result: 对 10,655 个真实世界的 MCP 服务器进行检测，发现泄漏率超过 10%。案例研究确认了具体的暴露情况，包括泄露的 Bearer 令牌、传播的 API 密钥和明文认证凭据。

Conclusion: MCP 协议存在系统性的隐私风险，需要在新兴的 LLM 代理工具链中引入协议感知的防护机制。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) has rapidly become the de facto standard for connecting large language models (LLMs) to external resources, but it also introduces a class of privacy risks that existing tools are ill-equipped to detect. Unlike conventional exfiltration bugs, leakage in MCP servers is largely protocol-induced: credentials, API keys, and Personally Identifiable Information (PII) cross the local/LLM boundary simply by being returned, logged, or raised inside a tool handler, with no explicit outbound request in the source code. We present MCPPrivacyDetector, a context-aware cross-language static analysis framework that detects such leakage in multilingual MCP servers. MCPPrivacyDetector lifts heterogeneous code implemented across different programming language (e.g., Python) into a unified program representation, applies context-aware semantic filtering to isolate genuinely sensitive values and protocol-specific implicit sinks (e.g., @mcp.tool handlers), and performs taint analysis to enumerate feasible flows. Applied to 10,655 real-world MCP servers, MCPPrivacyDetector finds leakage rates above 10%. Case studies confirm concrete exposures including leaked Bearer tokens, propagated API keys, and plaintext authentication credentials, arguing for systematic, protocol-aware safeguards in the emerging LLM agent toolchain.

</details>


### [180] [LLM-assisted Generation of Pseudo-C2 Servers for IoT Malware Dynamic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21349)
*K. Hasui,S. Matsugaya,M. Shimamura,M. Hashimoto*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合Ghidra与大语言模型(LLM)的系统，从IoT恶意软件二进制文件中提取通信规范并自动生成伪C2服务器，在Mirai实验中以100%准确率提取全部20个核心协议元素，成功复现10种DDoS攻击向量中的7种，并端到端验证了自定义变种的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数IoT恶意软件作为依赖于C2服务器的僵尸网络运行，但攻击基础设施的生命周期短，导致样本在无C2通信时处于休眠状态，阻碍了动态分析。

Method: 结合Ghidra逆向工程框架与LLM，从恶意软件二进制中提取通信规范，并自动生成伪C2服务器。

Result: Mirai实验显示100%的规范提取准确率（全部20个核心协议元素与真实情况一致）；生成的伪C2服务器成功复现了10种DDoS攻击向量中的7种，攻击行为与原始C2一致；在修改后的Mirai变种上实现端到端成功。

Conclusion: 该方法将LLM的应用从分析辅助扩展到动态分析环境的自动构建，LLM能够从二进制结构中推断规范而无需依赖预训练知识。

Abstract: Most IoT malware operates as botnets dependent on Command and Control (C2) servers, but the short-lived nature of attack infrastructure often leaves samples dormant without C2 communication, hindering dynamic analysis. This paper proposes a system that combines Ghidra with a Large Language Model (LLM) to extract communication specifications from a malware binary and automatically generate a pseudo-C2 server. Experiments using Mirai demonstrate that the proposed system semantically interprets binary control structures and extracts all 20 core protocol elements in agreement with the ground truth (100\% specification extraction accuracy). The generated pseudo-C2 server fully reproduces seven of ten DDoS attack vectors with attack behavior consistent with the original C2. When applied to a customized variant created by modifying the publicly available Mirai source code, the method succeeds end-to-end -- from specification extraction through pseudo-C2 generation to attack reproduction -- demonstrating that the LLM infers specifications from binary structures without relying on pre-trained knowledge. This approach extends the applicability of LLMs from analysis assistance to the automated construction of dynamic analysis environments.

</details>


### [181] [Beyond Classification Accuracy: An Exploration-Range Evaluation of Adaptive Crawling for Fake Shopping Sites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21353)
*K. Karasawa,K. Takeshige,S. Matsugaya,M. Shimamura,M. Hashimoto*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对搜索引擎投毒导致虚假购物网站泛滥的问题，提出一种闭环爬虫系统，利用假网站分类器的页面级输出生成下一轮搜索查询，采用种子复合策略（正例特征词+语境种子词）构建查询，持续发现新虚假主机。实验表明，固定关键词基线从第2轮起零新主机发现，而所提方法在第3轮累计唯一主机数约为基线的7.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过SEO投毒针对日本用户的虚假购物网站日益增多，传统基于固定关键词的收集方法无法应对演变的攻击活动，导致新网站发现滞后。

Method: 提出闭环爬虫系统，将假网站分类器（fastText+LightGBM）的页面级输出反馈到下一轮搜索查询生成中；采用种子复合策略，从正例页面提取特征词并结合语境种子词（如"深折扣""官方"）构建查询；引入每轮新主机数和累计唯一主机数作为探索范围指标。

Result: 所提方法在每轮持续发现新主机，第3轮累计唯一主机数约为固定关键词基线的7.6倍；基线方法从第2轮起新主机获取量为零，完全停滞。

Conclusion: 基于分类反馈的闭环爬虫能有效突破固定关键词的局限，持续发现新出现的虚假购物网站，显著提升搜索引擎投毒攻击的检测覆盖面。

Abstract: In recent years, fake shopping sites targeting Japanese users have appeared in the top results of search engines through SEO poisoning, causing increasing damage. Conventional collection methods rely on fixed keywords and cannot keep up with evolving attack campaigns, delaying the discovery of new sites. We propose a closed-loop crawler that incorporates the page-level outputs of a fake-site classifier (fastText+LightGBM) into the search queries of the next cycle. Search queries are generated by a seed-compound strategy that combines characteristic words extracted from positive pages with seed words from the fake-shopping context (e.g., ``deep discount,'' ``official''). To complement evaluations that tend to focus on classifier accuracy, we also introduce per-cycle new-host counts and cumulative unique-host counts as exploration-range metrics. In a comparative experiment ($n=3$ for the proposed method, $n=2$ for the baseline), the fixed-keyword baseline yielded zero new-host acquisition from cycle 2 onward, indicating complete stagnation, whereas the proposed method continued to discover new hosts and, at cycle 3, achieved a cumulative unique-host count approximately 7.6 times that of the baseline on average.

</details>


### [182] [ARENA: An Architecture for Measuring the Transferability of Autonomous Cyber Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21377)
*Sidnei Barbieri,Ágney Lopes Roth Ferraz,Wagner Comin Sonaglio,Gioliano de Oliveira Braga,Henrique Curi de Miranda,Lourenço Alves Pereira Júnior*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从生产环境金融SOC中提取、匿名化、结构化并验证SIEM数据的方法，在保护隐私的前提下保留任务相关的调查结构。实验表明：作为训练数据（37个MITRE ATT&CK映射的HIKARI挑战）在保持时序和实体一致性时有效；作为测量基准（200个SOCpilot事件），确定性验证器能检测出人类基线中不存在的违规LLM行为。最终建立了可测量的隐私-效用边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境的SOC遥测数据是最真实的，但由于原始日志无法公开，研究只能依赖合成或过时数据集。需要一种方法桥接私有生产遥测与可重用研究工件之间的鸿沟。

Method: 将私有生产遥测与可重用研究工件之间的边界作为设计对象，提出了一套方法论：从生产金融SOC中提取、匿名化、结构化并验证SIEM数据，在声明的隐私边界内保留任务相关的调查结构。

Result: 作为训练材料（37个MITRE ATT&CK映射的HIKARI挑战），匿名化保留时序顺序和实体一致性时有效；作为测量基底（200个SOCpilot事件），确定性验证器能检测出人类基线中不存在的违规LLM行为。

Conclusion: 建立了一个可测量的隐私-效用边界，而非形式化的匿名性声明。

Abstract: Operational evidence is not automatically scientific evidence. The most realistic Security Operations Center (SOC) data is production telemetry, yet it remains scientifically inaccessible because raw logs cannot be released; as a result, research relies on synthetic or dated datasets. We treat the boundary between private production telemetry and reusable research artifacts as the design object: a methodology that extracts, anonymizes, structures, and validates Security Information and Event Management (SIEM) data from a production financial SOC while preserving task-relevant investigative structure within a declared privacy boundary. Two consumers stress the same artifact. As training material, it fails loudly: 37 MITRE ATT&CK-mapped HIKARI challenges work only when anonymization preserves temporal order and entity consistency. As a measurement substrate, it fails quietly: across 200 SOCpilot incidents, a deterministic verifier detects non-compliant Large Language Model (LLM) actions that are absent from the human baseline. The result is a measurable privacy-utility boundary rather than a formal anonymity claim.

</details>


### [183] [From Production SIEM to Reusable Cybersecurity Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21389)
*Sidnei Barbieri,Leonardo Vaz de Meneses,Ágney Lopes Roth Ferraz,Wagner Comin Sonaglio,Lourenço Alves Pereira Júnior*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从生产金融SOC中提取、匿名化、结构化并验证SIEM数据的方法论，在保护隐私的同时保留任务相关的调查结构。通过HIKARI挑战（37个MITRE ATT&CK映射）和SOCpilot事件（200个）两个用例验证，发现该方法存在"大声失败"（训练材料）和"安静失败"（测量基准）两种模式，最终形成一个可测量的隐私-效用边界而非正式匿名性声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的SOC遥测数据是最真实的，但因隐私原因原始日志无法公开，导致研究只能依赖合成或过时数据集。本文旨在解决这一矛盾，在隐私保护与科研可用性之间建立可行的方法论。

Method: 设计一种方法，从生产金融SOC中提取、匿名化、结构化并验证SIEM数据，在声明的隐私边界内保留任务相关的调查结构。通过两种消费场景（HIKARI训练挑战和SOCpilot测量基准）进行验证。

Result: HIKARI挑战中，37个MITRE ATT&CK映射的攻击案例仅在匿名化保留时间顺序和实体一致性时才能工作（大声失败）；SOCpilot的200个事件中，确定性验证器检测到LLM的非合规行为而人类基线中不存在（安静失败）。

Conclusion: 该方法建立的是一个可测量的隐私-效用边界，而非正式的匿名性声称。边界两侧分别对应不同的失败模式——训练场景下失败明显可察觉，测量场景下失败隐蔽不易发现。

Abstract: Operational evidence is not automatically scientific evidence. The most realistic Security Operations Center (SOC) data is production telemetry, yet it remains scientifically inaccessible because raw logs cannot be released; as a result, research relies on synthetic or dated datasets. We treat the boundary between private production telemetry and reusable research artifacts as the design object: a methodology that extracts, anonymizes, structures, and validates Security Information and Event Management (SIEM) data from a production financial SOC while preserving task-relevant investigative structure within a declared privacy boundary. Two consumers stress the same artifact. As training material, it fails loudly: 37 MITRE ATT&CK-mapped HIKARI challenges work only when anonymization preserves temporal order and entity consistency. As a measurement substrate, it fails quietly: across 200 SOCpilot incidents, a deterministic verifier detects non-compliant Large Language Model (LLM) actions that are absent from the human baseline. The result is a measurable privacy-utility boundary rather than a formal anonymity claim.

</details>


### [184] [Evaluating LLMs for Real-World Web Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21397)
*Sebastian Neef,Luca Jungnickel,Antonio Benjamin Buchholz,Valene Spence,Vicente Birke Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对六种前沿和开源LLM（Claude Opus 4.6、Codex GPT-5.4、Gemini 3.1-pro-preview、Qwen 3.5、Qwen 3 Coder Next、MiniMax M2.5）在WordPress插件真实Web漏洞（SQL注入、XSS、路径遍历、RCE）检测中的表现进行了基准测试。结果显示Claude Opus 4.6检出率最高（63%），开源模型MiniMax M2.5表现持平（48%），但所有模型均存在报告一致性低（低至50%）和漏检问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在自动化漏洞检测方面展现出潜力，但其在Web特定漏洞上的有效性尚未被充分探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统评估LLM在真实Web漏洞检测中的能力与局限。

Method: 选取六种前沿和开源LLM，使用五种不同结构、范围和复杂度的提示设计，在WordPress插件上对SQL注入、存储型XSS、路径遍历和RCE四类真实Web漏洞进行三组实验迭代的静态分析基准测试。

Result: Claude Opus 4.6达到最高检出率（63%），MiniMax M2.5与前沿模型持平（48%），Qwen 3.5仅35%；范围限定提示优于开放式提示，提示复杂度影响不大；所有模型报告一致性均不理想（低至50%），无模型能完全识别基线漏洞。

Conclusion: LLM在Web漏洞检测中既有机会也有局限，模型和提示设计均影响检测效果，但报告一致性差和漏检问题仍需解决。研究为安全从业者提供了实践教训，并公开了全部代码和数据以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have emerged as a promising tool for automated vulnerability detection, yet their effectiveness on web-specific vulnerabilities remains to be explored.
  This work benchmarks six frontier (Claude Opus 4.6, Codex GPT-5.4, Gemini 3.1-pro-preview) and open-weight models (Qwen 3.5, Qwen 3 Coder Next, MiniMax M2.5) on their ability to detect real-world web vulnerabilities using static analysis in WordPress plugins, including SQL injection, stored cross-site scripting, path traversal, and remote code execution. Using five prompt designs of varying structure, scope, and complexity across three experiment iterations, we aim to answer how model and prompt choice affects vulnerability detection.
  Our results show that all models are capable of detecting valid security issues, but the detection rate varies depending on the model and prompt. For example, Claude Opus 4.6 achieved the highest web vulnerability detection rate (63%), while open-weight MiniMax M2.5 performs on par with other frontier models (48%), and self-hosted Qwen 3.5 only achieved 35%. We show that scoped prompts that narrow the vulnerability scope outperform open-ended ones, whereas the prompt complexity has little impact. Surprisingly, no model achieved full reporting consistency across three experiment iterations, with some as low as 50%. Our experiments demonstrate the opportunities and limits of LLM-based vulnerability detection, as no model correctly identified one baseline vulnerability in one of the plugins.
  Additionally, we derive practical lessons learned for security practitioners and publish all code and data to support future research.

</details>


### [185] [A Longitudinal Study of Android Apps Signing Key Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21487)
*Mark Huasong Meng,Qing Zhang,Weirao Lu,Chunyang Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究通过对GitHub等公开仓库的大规模挖掘，发现了5,673个泄露的Android签名密钥库，这些密钥关联到278个真实应用（包括26个第三方应用和252个预装应用），影响了超过100亿用户，并展示了实际的漏洞利用风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Android应用签名依赖于开发者管理的凭据，近期两大OEM厂商的平台密钥泄露事件表明，即使签名机制设计稳健，也可能因开发者疏忽而被攻破，因此需要系统性地评估这一威胁。

Method: 通过纵向生态研究，挖掘公开仓库中的Android签名凭据，利用泄露密码恢复受损密钥，并将密钥与从主流商店和OEM系统镜像收集的4,000多个应用的签名进行匹配分析。

Result: 发现5,673个受损密钥库（GitHub）、26个唯一证书关联到278个真实应用，涉及26个第三方应用和252个预装应用（来自7家制造商），影响超100亿用户；并通过概念验证的应用替换攻击证明了实际可利用性，还发现非智能手机平台（如覆盖1,100多个车型的车载系统）存在溢出风险。

Conclusion: 签名密钥管理不善是系统性风险，Android发布工程和分发基础设施亟需更严格的密钥管理支持。

Abstract: Android app signing relies on developer-managed credentials, making secure key protection essential for the integrity of the software supply chain. A recent platform key leakage incident involving two major OEM manufacturers demonstrates that even robustly designed signing mechanisms can be compromised due to developers' oversight. In this work, we conduct a longitudinal ecosystem study to characterize this threat by mining public repositories for Android signing credentials, recovering compromised keys via exposed passwords, and matching them against signatures from over 4,000 apps collected from major stores and OEM system images. Our analysis identifies 5,673 compromised keystores on GitHub and 26 unique certificates linked to 278 real-world apps. These include 26 third-party apps in public app stores and 252 preinstalled apps from seven manufacturers, collectively affecting over 10 billion users. We demonstrate the practical exploitability of these leaks through a proof-of-concept app replacement attack and identify spillover risks in non-smartphone platforms, including a popular automotive head-unit platform installed in over 1,100 vehicle models. Our results reveal that signing-key mismanagement is a systemic risk, underscoring the need for a more rigorous key-management support in Android release engineering and distribution infrastructures.

</details>


### [186] [Enhancing Stateful Detection of Adversarial Attacks with Soft-labels' Temporality and Robust Similarity Approximations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21592)
*De Zhang Lee,Han Fang,Ee-Chien Chang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合查询相似性和软标签时间相关性的两阶段状态检测方法，能有效检测黑盒对抗攻击，同时解决了现有近似相似度函数易被规避的漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有状态检测（SD）方法（如Blacklight、PIHA）利用查询相似性检测对抗攻击，但存在两个问题：一是忽略了时间信息（尤其是软标签的时间相关性）这一对抗攻击的显著特征，导致假阳性率较高；二是许多SD系统使用计算成本低的近似相似度函数替代精确度量，这种不一致性可被攻击者利用，通过构造在近似下不相似但在预期度量下相近的查询来逃避检测。

Method: 提出两阶段方法：第一阶段识别具有高相似性的查询子序列，并引入随机性以防止针对近似相似度函数的对抗攻击；第二阶段分析软标签的时间相关性，进一步验证是否存在对抗查询。

Result: 在Boundary Attack、HSJA、SimBA、Square Attack等攻击下，真实阳性率（TPR）达到1.00，假阳性率（FPR）不超过0.06；且对OARS自适应攻击具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 结合时间相关性信息能有效降低假阳性率，引入随机性可防御针对近似相似度函数的规避攻击，所提框架在检测性能和鲁棒性上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Stateful Detection (SD) mitigates adversarial attacks by determining whether a sequence of queries contains queries from a black-box adversary. Recent works, such as Blacklight and PIHA utilize query similarity to detect such queries. In this paper, we observe that temporal information, in particular, the temporal correlation of the classification soft labels, is a prominent characteristic of adversarial attacks and can be leveraged to reduce false positive rates. Moreover, we point out a potential vulnerability in SD implementation. Many SD systems identify similar queries according to some implicit, computationally expensive metric. To improve efficiency, these systems often adopt an approximate similarity function as substitute. This discrepancy could be exploited by crafting queries that appear dissimilar under the approximation but are close in the intended metric, thereby evading detection. We refer to this as an ``adversarial attack'' on the approximation function, and demonstrate it through a lightweight attack on Blacklight's similarity function.
  Based on the above observations, we propose a two-phase approach. The first phase identifies subsequences of queries with high similarity, incorporating randomness to prevent the aforementioned ``adversarial attacks''. The second phase analyzes temporal correlation of the soft-labels to further validate the presence of the adversary's queries. Experimental results show that the framework detects adversarial queries generated by Boundary Attack, HSJA, SimBA, Square Attack with true positive rate (TPR) reaching 1.00, while maintaining a false positive rate (FPR) of at most 0.06. Additionally, the method is robust against OARS which is an adaptive attack.

</details>


### [187] [Toward Open Weight Models Without Risks: Separating Public and Private Capabilities in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21638)
*Charbel El Feghali,Arkil Patel,Nicholas Meade,Spandana Gella,Verna Dankers,Siva Reddy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出分层语言模型（TLMs），通过在开源权重中嵌入秘密密钥控制的参数排列，实现同一组权重支持多个能力层级，默认公共配置表现为常规LLM，密钥配置可解锁额外能力（如新语言、指令遵循、事实记忆），且该机制抵抗微调提取和部分密钥泄露，为开源模型的能力选择性控制提供了新方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前开源大语言模型在能力控制上存在两难：要么在发布前压制危险能力（牺牲所有用户能力且易被越狱攻击），要么通过封闭服务控制访问（与开源本质矛盾）。需要一种既能保持开源权重发布、又能选择性控制敏感能力的方法。

Method: 提出Tiered Language Models（TLMs）：1）在单一开源权重中嵌入一个由秘密密钥指定的参数子集排列，改变计算图以暴露额外能力；2）联合预训练两种配置（公共和密钥）；3）在私有数据上微调密钥配置，同时使用正则化保持公共模型行为不变；4）支持多层次层级扩展。

Result: 在180M和650M参数的TLM上验证：密钥配置能习得新语言、获得指令跟随能力、记忆私有事实知识，而公共配置完全不展现这些能力。机制能抵抗基于微调的提取攻击和部分密钥泄露。

Conclusion: TLMs向调和开源权重发布与选择性能力控制迈出了一步，通过在模型权重结构层面而非输入空间进行权限控制，提供了更鲁棒的能力隔离方案。

Abstract: Open-weight Large Language Models (LLMs) enable scientific progress and broad deployment. However, they make it difficult to control access to sensitive capabilities. Current practice either suppresses dangerous capabilities before release or mediates access through closed services that use specialized model variants, input/output monitors, and API permissions. The former is susceptible to jailbreaks while sacrificing capability for all users to mitigate the risks posed by a few, and the latter is fundamentally incompatible with open-weight release. In this paper, we propose Tiered Language Models (TLMs), where a single set of released weights supports multiple capability levels. In its default public configuration, a TLM behaves as a conventional LLM. A compact secret key specifies a permutation over a small parameter subset, inducing an alternative computation graph over the same weights that exposes additional capabilities. We develop a training protocol that jointly pretrains both configurations from scratch, then fine-tunes the keyed configuration on private data with regularization to preserve the public model's behavior. We pretrain 180M- and 650M-parameter TLMs and demonstrate that the keyed configuration can acquire a new language, gain instruction-following ability, and memorize private factual knowledge, whereas the public configuration exhibits none of these capabilities. Moreover, we show that our approach extends naturally to multiple hierarchical tiers. Because authorization operates on the model's weight structure rather than in the input space, the mechanism resists fine-tuning-based extraction and partial key compromise. In general, TLMs take a step toward reconciling open-weight release with selective capability control.

</details>


### [188] [A Hybrid, Multi-Layered Pipeline for Phishing and Threat Classification: Independently Validated URL and NLP Engines with a Calibrated Multi-Channel Fusion Stage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21690)
*Saifelden M. Ismail,Aser O. Ibrahim,Omar A. Mahmoud*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对钓鱼威胁的多模态混合检测流水线，包含URL引擎（四阶段堆栈）、NLP分类器（DistilBERT）和威胁情报同步器三个独立引擎，并通过决策级融合（概率OR）将三者结果整合。在10,677封邮件的系统基准测试中，F1达到0.914，召回率从0.8%提升至87.3%，假阳性率降至3.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钓鱼攻击是多模态威胁，单一检测方法（仅URL或仅文本）泛化能力不足，现有模型的同分布准确率高但分布外泛化能力弱（召回率仅0.8%），需要构建多模态融合的混合流水线来提升真实钓鱼邮件的检测泛化能力。

Method: 构建三个独立引擎：(1) URL引擎：四阶段堆栈（Domain Guard、词汇模型、威胁情报、非对称L2融合侧车）；(2) NLP分类器：泛化强化的DistilBERT；(3) 威胁情报同步器：端到端OpenTelemetry检测确保1:1消息守恒。最后通过决策级融合（校准的概率OR）整合URL、头部和钓鱼概率三个通道的输出。

Result: NLP分类器在留出真实钓鱼邮件上的召回率从0.8%提升至87.3%；在10,677封邮件系统级基准测试中，F1=0.914，真实垃圾邮件的假阳性率降至3.6%；威胁情报同步器达到1:1消息守恒。

Conclusion: 部署可用的检测系统其关键约束是泛化能力（分布外性能）而非同分布准确率。当前结果仍是初步集成结果，因基准测试使用了代理URL和头部通道且操作点仍需重新校准。

Abstract: Phishing is a multi-modal threat. We present a hybrid pipeline that scores each modality with its own engine and fuses the results. Three engines are built, deployed, and independently benchmarked: a four-stage URL stack (Domain Guard, lexical model, threat intelligence, and an asymmetric L2 fusion sidecar); a generalization-hardened DistilBERT NLP classifier whose held-out real-phishing recall rises from 0.8% to 87.3%; and a threat-intelligence synchronizer with end-to-end OpenTelemetry instrumentation confirming 1:1 message conservation. A decision-level fusion stage, characterized on a 10,677-email whole-system benchmark, reaches F1 = 0.914 with a calibrated probabilistic-OR over URL, header, and phishing-probability channels while cutting held-out real-spam false positives to 3.6%. Because that benchmark uses proxy URL and header channels and an operating point still needing recalibration, we present it as a preliminary integrated result. The binding constraint for deployable detection is generalization rather than same-distribution accuracy.

</details>


### [189] [Safe to Check, Unsafe to Use: Relinking at the Compression Boundary of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21732)
*Zesen Liu,Zihan Zhang,Dongdong She*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现了一种新型安全漏洞"重链接(relinking)"：当大语言模型使用基于摘要的提示压缩时，攻击者可将恶意指令分割成多个表面上无害的片段，压缩模型作为混乱代理会将这些片段重新链接成完整恶意指令。作者提出了自动化工具Relink，在四个长上下文基准上实现86.9%的重链接成功率，并提出KBRA防御方法将后端执行率降至0.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于摘要的提示压缩正被广泛用于缩短长上下文，但这改变了安全边界：过滤器检查压缩前的提示，而后端模型实际执行的是压缩后的上下文。这种分离产生了新的攻击面，攻击者可以利用压缩模型作为混乱代理，将分布式、局部良性的片段重新链接成完整的恶意指令。

Method: 1) 形式化定义了"重链接"漏洞及"对抗性重链接"攻击；2) 提出了Relink工具，使用领域特定语言(DSL)将恶意载荷自动分割为多个良性片段，使完整恶意指令在压缩前不存在；3) 提出了KBRA(Knowledge-Based Relinking Attack)防御方法。

Result: 在四个长上下文智能体基准测试中，Relink实现了86.9%的重链接成功率和后端执行率，而干净分割的对照方法仅为17.0%。现有防御无法可靠捕获对抗性重链接，KBRA防御将残余后端执行率降至0.0%。

Conclusion: 提示压缩引入了新的安全漏洞，需要重新审视安全边界设计。重链接攻击利用了摘要机制本身的性质（注意力机制、预训练、后训练），现有防御不足，但KBRA可有效缓解。

Abstract: Summarization-based prompt compression is increasingly used by LLM agents to shorten long, distributed contexts, but it shifts the security boundary: filters inspect the pre-compression prompt while the backend acts on a newly generated compressed context. We identify relinking, a compression-boundary vulnerability where the compressor behaves as a confused deputy, summarizing distributed, locally benign fragments into a complete malicious instruction. Unlike prompt injection, relinking need not place an explicitly malicious payload in the source context. We show that relinking arises from summarization itself: attention makes separated fragments jointly available, pre-training makes compatible fragments plausible to connect, and post-training favors compact backend-actionable summaries. We formalize the attacker-induced form as adversarial relinking and present Relink, an automated DSL-based tool that splits malicious payloads into benign fragments while keeping the complete payload absent before compression. Across four long-context agent benchmarks, Relink achieves 86.9% Relink Rate and Backend Action Rate versus 17.0% for clean-split controls. Existing defenses fail to reliably capture adversarial relinking; our KBRA defense reduces residual Backend Action Rate to 0.0%.

</details>


### [190] [FrogBard-512: Design and Experimental Evaluation of a Four-Voice Permutation-Based Hash Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21779)
*Victor Duarte Melo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FrogBard-512是一种实验性512位哈希函数，使用自定义2048位置换（四路512位声道），支持顺序模式和树模式，经过多项测试验证实现一致性和无明显统计缺陷，但尚未建立安全性论证，属于研究原型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索基于宽置换（2048位）和大容量（1024位）的哈希函数设计空间，结合AES派生替换、ARX四分之一轮和声部间混合层等多种密码原语，构建实验性哈希函数原型。

Method: 设计采用1024位速率、1024位容量、128字节消息块和16轮置换。每轮结合公开轮常数、四个AES派生仿射等价字节替换、ARX四分之一轮、奇偶依赖的声部间混合层和固定通道置换。同时支持域分离的树模式（1 MiB叶子）、多线程处理和AVX2后端。

Result: 通过固定向量测试、流式等价性、置换可逆性、标量与SIMD一致性、消毒器运行、Valgrind内存和竞态分析、随机API测试、libFuzzer/AFL++模糊测试、减轮扩散测量以及PractRand和Dieharder大流统计测试，验证了实现一致性和无显著统计缺陷。

Conclusion: FrogBard-512目前是研究原型，未建立抗碰撞性、抗原像性、可区分性及结构性安全，也未经过独立密码分析。

Abstract: FrogBard-512 is an experimental 512-bit hash function based on a custom 2048-bit permutation organized as four 512-bit voices. The sequential mode uses a 1024-bit rate, a 1024-bit capacity, 128-byte message blocks, and a 16-round permutation. Each round combines public round constants, four AES-derived affine-equivalent byte substitutions, ARX quarter-rounds, a parity-dependent cross-voice mixing layer, and fixed lane permutations. The design also includes a separately domain-separated tree mode with 1 MiB leaves, multithreaded processing, and an AVX2 backend for four independent equal-length messages.
  This paper gives a self-contained description of the construction, constant generation, padding, finalization, tree encoding, implementation profiles, and conformance vectors. It also reports an experimental evaluation covering fixed-vector tests, streaming equivalence, permutation inversion, scalar and SIMD agreement, sanitizer runs, Valgrind-based memory and race analysis, randomized API testing, libFuzzer, AFL++, reduced-round diffusion measurements, and large-stream statistical testing with PractRand and Dieharder.
  The reported results provide evidence of implementation consistency and the absence of obvious statistical defects in the tested configurations. They do not establish collision resistance, preimage resistance, indifferentiability, or structural security. FrogBard-512 remains a research prototype and has not undergone independent cryptanalysis.

</details>


### [191] [Agent-Assisted Side-Channel Attacks on Non-Prefix KV Cache in RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21842)
*He Sun,Shinan Liu,Siyuan Ma,Junhao Li,Mingjun Xiao,Wenhao Jiang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现了非前缀KV缓存融合中存在结构性侧信道漏洞，提出SpliceLeak攻击可在有限熵场景下以100%成功率逐token窃取语义内容，并设计了SpliceDefense防御框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的KV缓存侧信道攻击依赖严格的线性前缀对齐，无法对包含用户特有私密前缀的RAG查询生效。因此需要探索新型侧信道攻击向量，揭示非前缀KV缓存融合中的结构性安全漏洞。

Method: 利用块感知内存调度中确定性微架构机制泄漏的"Step-Wave"时序信号，实施两阶段攻击：首先指纹识别隐藏私密提示的长度，然后通过边界碰撞逐token提取语义内容。

Result: 在vLLM+LMCache生产级框架上，SpliceLeak在有限熵场景下实现高达100%的提取成功率，仅需每token 63次请求，可穿透连续批处理噪声。

Conclusion: 提出SpliceDefense双部防御框架（量化块填充QCP+恒定时间边界融合CTBF），可有效消除侧信道信号（Delta TTFT≈0）且吞吐开销可忽略。

Abstract: Modern Large Language Model (LLM) serving engines increasingly rely on Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) and non-prefix Key-Value (KV) cache fusion to accelerate long-context, multi-tenant inference. While existing KV cache side-channel attacks require strict linear prefix alignment--rendering them ineffective against real-world RAG queries that contain unique, user-specific private prefixes--we uncover a critical class of structural vulnerabilities inherent to chunk-aware memory scheduling. We demonstrate that the deterministic micro-architectural mechanisms used to align and fuse disjoint memory chunks inadvertently leak a continuous "Step-Wave" timing signature. Exploiting this physical observation, we introduce SpliceLeak, the first end-to-end side-channel attack targeting non-prefix KV cache fusion. SpliceLeak executes a systematic two-phase privacy breach: it first structurally fingerprints the exact length of hidden private prompts, and subsequently manipulates boundary collisions to extract exact semantic content token-by-token. Extensive evaluations on production-grade frameworks (vLLM integrated with LMCache) demonstrate that SpliceLeak achieves up to a 100% extraction success rate in bounded-entropy scenarios. Driven by a deterministic +104 ms hardware latency void, the attack requires as few as 63 requests per token, piercing through realistic continuous batching noise. To resolve the inherent conflict between memory deduplication and security, we propose SpliceDefense, a bipartite mitigation framework consisting of Quantized Chunk Padding (QCP) and Constant-Time Boundary Fusion (CTBF). Our evaluations confirm that SpliceDefense effectively flattens the side-channel signal (Delta TTFT ~ 0) with negligible throughput overhead, preserving the critical benefits of global cache sharing.

</details>


### [192] [Mind the Intention: Task-Aware Backdoor Attacks for Forecast-Driven Distribution Network Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21846)
*Yuxuan Chen,Haipeng Xie,Yichi Zhang,Shuo Dai,Zhaohong Bie*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出GridTroj，一个针对配电网运营的后门攻击统一框架。与传统时间序列后门攻击仅关注预测误差不同，GridTroj通过意图规划器和后门实现器两个模块，将攻击意图显式融入攻击优化过程，旨在破坏下游优化运营。实验证明该方法能有效破坏电网运营并优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式能源预测-优化-运营链容易受到网络攻击，但已有研究主要关注对抗性攻击，而对更可控、更持久的后门攻击研究不足。本文旨在探究后门攻击如何破坏配电网运营，并填补该领域的空白。

Method: GridTroj框架包含两个核心模块：1) 意图规划器(Intention Planner)设计破坏运营的攻击目标和投毒策略；2) 后门实现器(Backdoor Realizer)构建相应网络架构和训练策略以学习触发-目标关联。与标准方法不同，该方法明确将攻击者的运营破坏意图纳入优化目标，而非仅优化预测误差。

Result: 在三个下游优化任务上的实验表明，GridTroj能有效破坏电网运营，且性能优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 后门攻击对配电网运营构成切实威胁，GridTroj通过统一框架将攻击意图与运营破坏相结合，比传统仅关注预测误差的后门攻击更具破坏性。

Abstract: Accurate distributed energy resources (DERs) forecasting is critical for downstream optimal operations. However, such forecast-based operation can be highly vulnerable to cyberattacks. While existing research mainly focuses on adversarial attacks, we pivot to a more controllable and persistent threat: backdoor attacks. In time series forecasting, a backdoored model generates an attacker-specified target pattern whenever a trigger is embedded in historical inputs. This paradigm naturally fits the entire DER forecast-optimization-operation chain. In this paper, we investigate whether and how backdoor attacks can compromise distribution network operations and propose GridTroj, a unified backdoor framework tailored for this scenario. Unlike standard time series backdoor approaches that train a poisoned model to match a predefined target only in terms of forecasting error, GridTroj explicitly incorporates the attacker's intention and optimizes the attack toward operational disruption. Specifically, GridTroj coordinates two key modules. The Intention Planner designs operation-damaging targets and poisoning strategies, while the Backdoor Realizer constructs the corresponding network architecture and training strategy to learn the trigger-target association. Experiments on three downstream optimization tasks demonstrate that GridTroj can effectively compromise grid operations and outperforms existing baselines. Our code is available at https://github.com/YuxuanCEE/GridTroj.

</details>


### [193] [Harness-MU: A Safe, Governed, and Effective Harness for Multi-User LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21856)
*Wangxuan Fan,Xiaoyu Nie,Zhongxiang Dai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Harness-MU 是首个模型无关、零微调的基础设施框架，通过将语言生成与安全编排解耦，实现多用户 LLM 智能体中不可破解的权限边界，在隐私保护、效用分数和指令遵循准确率上显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体采用单用户训练范式，无法满足多用户、多主体的协作工作流中的权限控制、权威冲突解决和数据泄露防护需求，基于概率的提示词安全机制在多轮对抗交互中不可靠。

Method: 提出 Harness-MU，一个模型无关、零微调的基础设施框架。核心思想是将治理约束（谁被授权、什么被限制、谁的指令优先）视为确定性运行时变量，通过执行钩子（execution hooks）而非 LLM 本身来强制实施，实现语言生成与安全编排的解耦。

Result: 在 Muses-Bench 基准上，对四个前沿开源和闭源模型的测试中，Harness-MU 在所有访问控制攻击下实现了隐私保护目标，效用分数比标准基线提升 0.28-0.39，指令遵循准确率最高提升 48.9 个百分点。

Conclusion: Harness-MU 推进了"Harness Engineering"理念，证明系统性基础设施是解决 LLM 多主体治理挑战的关键。

Abstract: The increasing deployment of large language model (LLM) agents in collaborative workflows demands robust multi-user, multi-principal interaction mechanisms capable of enforcing access permissions, resolving authoritative conflicts, and preventing unauthorized data disclosure. However, a fundamental mismatch exists between the single-user training paradigm of contemporary LLMs and the hard constraints required for multi-principal governance, rendering probabilistic, prompt-based safeguards vulnerable under multi-turn adversarial interactions.Our key insight is that governance constraints -- who is authorized, what is restricted, and whose instructions take precedence -- are deterministic runtime variables that should be enforced by execution hooks rather than entrusted to the LLM. We present \textbf{Harness-MU}, the first model-agnostic, zero-tuning infrastructure framework for multi-user LLM agents. By decoupling language generation from safety orchestration, Harness-MU guarantees unbreakable permission boundaries while maximizing compliant demand satisfaction. Across four frontier open-weight and proprietary models on the \textit{Muses-Bench} benchmark, Harness-MU achieves the goal of privacy preservation across all access-control attacks, outperforming the standard baseline by 0.28--0.39 in utility score and improving instruction-following accuracy by up to 48.9 percentage points. Harness-MU advances the philosophy of \textit{Harness Engineering}, establishing that systematic infrastructure is essential for solving LLM multi-principal governance challenges. The code and data are available at https://github.com/YuanJrShiuan/Harness-MulUser.

</details>


### [194] [Continuous Behavioral Authentication via Multi-Expert BERT Log Analysis for Secure Data Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21900)
*Stergios Lantzos,Ilias Syrigos,Apostolos Apostolaras,Thanasis Korakis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于BERT的日志分析框架，用于Android系统的持续行为认证。该框架通过解析logcat流、预训练领域适配的BERT编码器，并微调三个专家模型（网络/设备身份、电池转换时序、Wi-Fi拓扑），将专家置信度得分融合后生成正态性评分，供策略决策点进行风险感知访问控制。实验表明该方法在保持低于1%假阳性率的同时，能有效检测语义、电池时序和拓扑偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动和零信任系统需要一种非侵入式的持续认证机制，在初始登录后持续确认已注册用户-设备上下文的有效性，以增强安全性而不影响用户体验。

Method: 提出BERT日志分析框架：1) 解析logcat流为事件模板和动态变量；2) 预训练领域适配的BERT编码器；3) 微调三个专家模型（网络/设备身份、电池转换时序、Wi-Fi拓扑）；4) 通过log空间变换和5-NN距离分类器融合专家置信度得分，生成正态性评分供PDP进行风险感知访问控制。

Result: 在正常轨迹、受控异常注入和良性Wi-Fi扰动实验中，多专家BERT日志分析能检测语义、电池时序和拓扑偏差，同时保持低于1%的假阳性率（FPR）。

Conclusion: Android系统日志是一种实用的无传感器信号，可用于持续认证和用户-设备上下文保障。

Abstract: Continuous authentication for mobile and zero-trust systems requires nonintrusive evidence confirming the enrolled user-device context remains valid after initial login. This paper presents a BERT log analysis framework for continuous behavioral authentication using Android system logs. The proposed pipeline parses logcat streams into event templates and dynamic variables, pre-trains a domain-adapted BERT encoder on Android log syntax, and fine-tunes three expert models for network/device identity, battery-transition timing, and Wi-Fi topology. The expert confidence scores are fused through a log-space transformation and a 5-nearest-neighbor distance classifier to generate a normality score that is provided to a Policy Decision Point (PDP) for risk-aware access control. Experiments on normal traces, controlled anomaly injections, and benign Wi-Fi perturbations indicate that multi-expert BERT log analysis can detect semantic, battery-timing, and topology deviations in the evaluated setting while maintaining sub-1% False Positive Rate (FPR). The results suggest that Android system logs are a practical sensor-free signal for continuous authentication and user-device context assurance.

</details>


### [195] [Guarded Equivalence Predicates for Scalable Formal Hardware Information-Flow Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22063)
*Liangtao Dai,Yimin Gao,Melika Morsali,Mircea R. Stan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对硬件信息流验证（IFV）中自组合方法产生的跨副本等价证明困难，提出**有守卫等价谓词（Guarded Equivalence Predicates）**，通过在特定控制上下文（如控制阶段、事务窗口、循环状态、协议区域）中引入条件性等价关系，辅助PDR引擎发现原本难以自动推导的归纳不变量。实验表明，该方法在两个PDR后端上解决了多个基准测试中超时问题，并最高加速10.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件信息流验证中，自组合方法将问题归约为两个电路副本间的安全（Safety）检查，产生的关系证明目标对通用PDR引擎而言难以仅从位级逻辑自动发现。现有基于PDR的方法通过**副本对称性**和**全局跨副本等价谓词**利用副本间的重复结构，但这类谓词仅在对应内部信号在全可达状态空间上一致时才有效，无法捕获仅在特定控制上下文中才需要满足的等式关系。

Method: 引入**有守卫等价谓词**：将上下文相关的等式关系表达为带守卫条件的等价谓词。不是将条件性等式视为假设，而是将其对应的**不匹配条件**作为辅助阻塞义务提交给PDR后端。守卫的提出通过关系型反例到归纳（CTI）的局部提取和状态拆分搜索来完成；只有后端证明不可达的候选守卫才能影响证明过程。

Result: 在12个IFV基准测试和两个PDR后端上进行实验：有守卫谓词将两个原本超时的基准（1800s限制）转化为在34.2-89.5s内完成的证明，在其余基准上最多减少10.8倍的证明时间。

Conclusion: 有守卫等价谓词有效地将上下文物相关的等价关系暴露给PDR引擎，弥补了全局等价谓词在控制上下文相关场景下的不足，显著提升了硬件信息流验证的可扩展性和效率。

Abstract: Formal hardware information-flow verification is a principled way to rule out secret-dependent functional or timing observations, but scaling such proofs remains difficult. Self-composition reduces information-flow verification to safety checking over two circuit copies, creating relational proof obligations that are hard for a generic PDR engine to discover from bit-level logic alone. Recent PDR-based techniques exploit this duplicated structure through copy symmetry and global cross-copy equivalence predicates. These predicates are effective when corresponding internal signals agree throughout the reachable state space, but they do not capture equalities that are relevant only in a specific control context. We observe that such contextual relations arise naturally in hardware IFV proofs: an internal signal pair may need to agree only within a control phase, transaction window, loop state, or protocol region. We introduce guarded equivalence predicates to expose these relations to PDR. Rather than treating a proposed contextual equality as an assumption, the verifier submits the corresponding mismatch condition as an auxiliary blocking obligation. Guards are proposed from relational counterexamples-to-induction using CTI-local extraction and state-split search; only candidates proved unreachable by the backend affect the proof. Across 12 IFV benchmarks and two PDR backends, guarded predicates convert two contextual baseline timeouts into completed proofs within 34.2--89.5s under an 1800s limit, while reducing proof time by up to 10.8x on additional benchmarks.

</details>


### [196] [$π$-RAG: Oblivious Retrieval via Semantic Quantization and Transcendental Addressing for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22153)
*Aniket Wattamwar,Mrunal Kakirwar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出π-RAG架构，利用π的数字作为不可变熵源，在LLM与敏感数据存储之间创建间接层，通过语义量化层和π-key实现遗忘检索，保障隐私与合规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG架构存在两个核心问题：原始向量嵌入易受反转攻击，以及非确定性检索失败。高合规行业（如金融、医疗）对数据隐私有严格要求，需要一种在保证语义理解的同时将LLM与敏感数据解耦的架构。

Method: 1) 利用π的数字作为超越熵源，构建不可变的间接层；2) 引入语义量化层，将用户输入投影到预计算的规范意图质心流形上；3) 通过加密盐将质心映射为确定性偏移，生成π-key作为指向标准数据载荷的指针，替代LLM直接访问数据存储。

Result: π-RAG在数学上保证了推理过程对数据内容的遗忘性，融合了确定性随机性、可审计性和差分隐私，在高合规场景中展现出高效能。

Conclusion: π-RAG通过利用π的数学特性构建不可变间接层，有效解决了传统RAG的安全隐患，为金融、医疗等对隐私有严格要求的行业提供了一种可审计且隐私保护的检索增强生成方案。

Abstract: This paper introduces $π$-RAG, a novel architecture for oblivious retrieval that decouples Large Language Models (LLMs) from sensitive data storage without sacrificing semantic understanding. Traditional Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) architectures expose raw vector embeddings to potential inversion attacks and nondeterministic retrieval failures. To address this, we utilize the digits of $π$ as a source of transcendental entropy, creating an immutable indirection layer between the LLM and private records. The value $π$ provides immutability, is uneditable and math governs it. The architecture also introduces a Semantic Quantization Layer. This layer projects user inputs onto a pre-computed manifold of Canonical Intent Centroids. RAG performs vector cosine similarity but here it maps the centroids to deterministic offsets via cryptographic salt. The resulting $π$-key is a pointer to standardized payload from the actual datastore. By replacing direct access to the datastore via LLM with this transcendental layer, $π$-RAG mathematically guarantees that the inference remains oblivious to the data. This architecture unifies deterministic randomness, auditability, and differential privacy, demonstrating high efficacy for high-compliance sectors such as finance and healthcare.

</details>


### [197] [2.5D Root of Trust: Securing the Chiplet Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22198)
*Charles Williams,Mohammed Nabeel,Gino Chacon,Ozgur Sinanoglu,Paul V. Gratz,Johann Knechtel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于硅中介层的2.5D芯片集成系统的安全威胁与防御策略，重点分析了互连攻击、缓存一致性攻击和微架构侧信道攻击三类威胁，并提出将主动中介层作为物理隔离的2.5D信任根（RoT），通过嵌入事务监视器和一致性消息检查器来实现运行时防御，无需修改商用芯粒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体行业正从单芯片系统向异构、多供应商的2.5D芯粒生态系统快速转型，虽然带来了良率、成本和上市时间的巨大优势，但来自不可信代工厂和设计方的芯粒引入了硬件木马、IP盗版和系统级通信攻击等新威胁，而传统的芯片级安全机制和信任根方案在此场景下均不充分。

Method: 本文采用综述研究方法，系统调查了2.5D集成中的三类安全威胁（互连攻击、缓存一致性攻击、微架构侧信道攻击），分析了设计时防御（2.5D拆分制造）和运行时防御（主动中介层作为2.5D RoT），核心方法是在可信中介层中嵌入事务监视器和一致性消息检查器，以强制内存访问权限并消除一致性层面的攻击。

Result: 研究发现，通过将安全机制嵌入中介层而非修改商用芯粒，可在不增加芯粒设计负担的情况下有效防御三类攻击；同时，所提出的EDA流程在实现这些防御的同时还能改善功耗和信号完整性，并减小系统整体面积。

Conclusion: 2.5D集成中必须将安全信任根从芯片级迁移至中介层级；主动中介层作为一种物理隔离的2.5D RoT，能够在不依赖芯粒厂商安全协作的前提下，系统性解决互连、缓存一致性和侧信道等关键安全威胁，是未来多供应商芯粒生态系统的可行安全范式。

Abstract: The semiconductor industry is rapidly transitioning from monolithic systems-on-chip toward heterogeneous, multi-vendor 2.5D chiplet ecosystems integrated via silicon interposers. While this paradigm shift offers immense benefits in yield, cost, and time-to-market, it radically expands the attack surface. Integrating chiplets from untrusted foundries and design houses introduces vulnerabilities to hardware Trojans, IP piracy, and system-level communication exploits. Critically, chip-level security features and conventional Root of Trust (RoT) proposals are insufficient in this context: any component, including the interconnect fabric itself, may be sourced from an untrusted vendor. This perspective paper surveys state-of-the-art security strategies for interposer-based 2.5D integration, focusing on three threat categories: interconnect attacks (snooping, spoofing, and man-in-the-middle), cache coherence exploits including complex forging attacks, and microarchitectural side-channel threats. We examine design-time defenses via 2.5D split manufacturing and, more crucially, runtime defenses that establish an active interposer as a physically isolated 2.5D RoT. By embedding so-called transaction monitors and coherence message checkers within the trusted interposer fabric, the system enforces memory access permissions by construction and neutralizes coherence-level attacks without need for modifying/securing the commodity chiplets. Finally, we review the EDA flows required to realize these defenses and show they concurrently improve power and signal integrity while reducing overall system footprint.

</details>


### [198] [TRACE: A Threat Modelling Methodology for Distributed, Cloud-First, and Decentralized Organisations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22214)
*Stefan Beyer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文指出现有威胁建模方法（STRIDE、PASTA、Trike、OCTAVE、LINDDUN、攻击树、MITRE ATT&CK）基于传统软件企业的三个假设（明确安全边界、单一拥有组织、技术与运营风险分离），但现代云原生和SaaS组织已打破这些假设。作者提出TRACE方法论，将威胁行为体、角色、资产、关键不变量、信任/权威边作为一等公民，跨越协议、系统、组织三层建模，并设计了人机协作的循证工作流，旨在应对授权恶意行为、共谋、CI/CD攻陷等现代风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有威胁建模方法基于传统软件企业和明确安全边界设计，无法有效处理现代分布式、云优先、零信任环境中授权恶意行为、共谋、控制平面和CI/CD攻陷等主要风险。

Method: TRACE方法：将威胁行为体、角色、资产、关键不变量、信任/权威边作为一等证据关联对象，跨协议、系统、组织三层建模；包含顺序门控工作流和循证可追溯性规范，支持人类与语言模型协作。

Result: 通过九个框架在十个维度上的比较，系统展示了现有方法在分布式云优先零信任场景中的不足，并完整定义了TRACE的核心模型、三大应用支柱、工作流和人机协作机制。

Conclusion: TRACE虽源于Web3安全实践但堆栈无关，与零信任架构和可问责拜占庭共识相关，仍需实证验证。

Abstract: Established threat modelling methodologies (STRIDE, PASTA, Trike, OCTAVE, LINDDUN, attack trees, and adversary-behaviour catalogues such as MITRE ATT&CK) were designed for software products and enterprises with a discernible security perimeter, a single owning organisation, and a clean separation between technical and operational risk. Modern organisations violate all three assumptions: they run on cloud and SaaS control planes they do not own, distribute privileged authority across founders, contractors, vendors, signers, committees, and automation, and expose value through human approval ceremonies and supply-chain edges rather than a network boundary. The dominant failures are authorised-but-malicious actors, collusion across nominally independent parties, control-plane and CI/CD compromise, and operational mishandling of high-value actions, which existing methods largely omit. We present TRACE, a methodology that treats threat actors, roles, assets, critical invariants, and trust/authority edges as first-class, evidence-linked objects spanning three layers: protocols, systems, and organisations. We compare nine widely used frameworks across ten dimensions, show where each falls short in distributed, cloud-first, zero-trust settings, and specify TRACE: its core model, three application pillars, sequential gated workflow, and an evidence-and-traceability discipline for human-AI co-working in which language models accelerate coverage while senior reviewers retain judgement over invariants, severity, and collusion. TRACE was developed through Web3 security practice but is stack-agnostic. We discuss its relationship to zero trust architecture and accountable Byzantine consensus, its limitations, and open questions around empirical validation.

</details>


### [199] [Investigating The Security of Modern AI and Cloud Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22237)
*Andrew Adiletta*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本论文系统性地解构了深度神经网络和大语言模型在物理内存共享到远程服务接口等不同交互层面的安全假设，通过提出一个统一的分类框架，展示了现代AI堆栈中各抽象层存在的实际攻击方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前安全研究虽然针对单个攻击面进行了大量工作，但缺乏一个统一的框架来推理物理、架构和算法层面的漏洞如何在现代AI堆栈中交互和显现。

Method: 通过提出一个涵盖从物理内存共驻到远程服务接口的交互层级分类法，系统性地解构AI与现代云基础设施的安全假设，并在每个抽象层展示实际攻击。

Result: 证明了在每一层抽象中都存在利用假设的实际攻击方法，揭示了深度神经网络和LLM部署所依赖的隔离假设存在系统性缺陷。

Conclusion: 挑战了AI系统部署中关于隔离的基础假设，表明需要跨层统一的安全框架来应对从物理到算法层面的综合威胁。

Abstract: The widespread deployment of Deep Neural Networks and Large Language Models (LLMs) relies on a foundational assumption of isolation that this dissertation challenges. This work systematically deconstructs security assumptions around AI and modern cloud infrastructure through a taxonomy of interaction levels that ranges from physical memory co-location to remote service interfaces. While significant research has addressed individual attack surfaces in isolation, the security community lacks a unified framework for reasoning about how physical, architectural, and algorithmic vulnerabilities manifest across the modern AI stack. This dissertation addresses that gap by demonstrating practical attacks that exploit assumptions at each layer of abstraction.

</details>


### [200] [Revelio: Cost-Efficient Agentic Memory Safety Vulnerability Detection For Repository-Scale Codebases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22263)
*Yiwei Hou,Hao Wang,Muxi Lyu,Marius Momeu,Eric Nguyen,Taige Yang,Koushik Sen,Dawn Song,David Wagner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Revelio是一个端到端的智能体框架，通过生成可执行漏洞验证（PoV）并用确定性消毒剂验证，结合廉价的LLM和轻量级静态分析，在7个经过多年模糊测试的生产级项目和100个Arvo项目中，以约1小时/项目和总计300美元的成本发现了19个未知的内存安全漏洞，性能优于前沿编码智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内存安全漏洞即使在经过广泛模糊测试和手动审计的项目中仍然构成严重威胁，而现有LLM方法存在幻觉、不可靠且难以扩展到仓库级代码库。

Method: Revelio提出端到端的智能体框架：利用廉价LLM和轻量级静态分析生成并排序漏洞假设，通过生成可执行Proof-of-Vulnerability（PoV）并由确定性消毒剂（sanitizer）验证来消除幻觉问题，仅在可复现且经消毒剂确认时才报告漏洞。

Result: 在7个已连续模糊测试5-8年的生产级项目中发现19个未知内存安全漏洞，在CyberGym基准测试中优于前沿编码智能体，总成本仅300美元，每个项目约1小时。

Conclusion: Revelio实现了可扩展且可信赖的端到端LLM驱动的内存安全漏洞检测，为实际应用提供了成本效益高的解决方案。

Abstract: Memory safety vulnerabilities remain a significant threat even for projects with extensive fuzzing and manual auditing. Recent results suggest that large language models hold great promise for detecting such vulnerabilities, but they are unreliable, at risk of hallucination, and challenging to scale to repository-size codebases. This paper presents Revelio, a cost-efficient end-to-end agentic framework for memory-safety vulnerability discovery. Revelio addresses the problem of hallucination by generating an executable Proof-of-Vulnerability, which is checked with a deterministic sanitizer. It reduces cost using inexpensive LLMs and lightweight static analysis to help generate and rank vulnerability hypotheses, reporting vulnerabilities only when they can be reproduced and confirmed by a sanitizer. We evaluated Revelio on seven production-quality projects that had been continuously fuzzed for five to eight years, as well as on 100 randomly selected Arvo projects from the CyberGym benchmark. With around one hour per project and a total cost of $300, Revelio discovered 19 previously unknown memory-safety vulnerabilities. On benchmarks, Revelio outperformed frontier coding agents across diverse backbone models at comparable token costs. Our results suggest that Revelio enables scalable and trustworthy end-to-end LLM-based memory-safety vulnerability detection.

</details>


### [201] [Control-Aware Manipulation of ArduPilot via Legitimate MAVLink Commands: Simulation and Hardware Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22289)
*Feras Benchellal andLotfi Ben Othmane,Yasaswini Konapalli,Cihan Tunc,Bharat Bhargava*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了针对ArduPilot无人机的控制感知攻击，通过修改PID增益、篡改EKF估计配置和破坏故障安全假设，利用多层控制器间的交互导致无人机失控坠毁。实验在SITL仿真和Pixhawk 2.4.8硬件平台上验证，证明了短序列MAVLink消息即可利用参数处理、命令信任和控制器状态验证的安全漏洞造成坠机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示ArduPilot无人机系统中控制参数篡改攻击的可行性，证明攻击者可以通过操纵飞行控制器参数（PID增益、EKF配置等）利用控制器动态对参数变化的敏感性，导致无人机失控坠毁，从而引起社区对无人机控制系统安全性的重视。

Method: 设计了六种攻击方法，利用多层控制器间的交互：修改PID增益、篡改扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）估计配置、破坏故障安全假设。通过Software-in-the-Loop（SITL）仿真和Pixhawk 2.4.8硬件平台进行验证，使用MAVLink消息序列实施攻击。

Result: 短序列MAVLink消息即可利用控制器对参数值和更新频率的敏感性，影响控制器状态，降低姿态稳定性、角速率行为、轨迹跟踪精度和估计器健康度。多种攻击效果组合可使飞行器进入不安全状态并坠毁。

Conclusion: ArduPilot系统在输入参数处理、命令信任和控制器状态验证方面存在安全漏洞，可被利用导致无人机失控和坠毁，需要加强这些方面的安全防护。

Abstract: This paper investigates control-aware attacks against ArduPilot-based Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs), inwhich an adversary exploits the sensitivity of flight-controller dynamics to parameter changes to cause loss of control and crashes. It describes six attacks that exploit interactions among multi-layer controllers by modifying Proportional-Integral-Derivative (PID) gains, altering Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) estimation configuration, and violating failsafe assumptions, thereby forcing ArduPilot into unsafe operating conditions. We evaluate the attacks in Software-in-the-Loop (SITL) simulation and validate them on a Pixhawk 2.4.8 hardware platform. The results show that short sequences of well-formed MAVLink messages can exploit controller sensitivity to parameter values and updates frequency, affecting controller states and degrading attitude stability, angular-rate behavior, trajectory tracking, and estimator health. We demonstrate that when multiple effects are combined, the vehicle can enter an unsafe state and crashes. These findings show that security gaps in input-parameter handling, command trust, and controller-state validation can be exploited to cause loss of control and crashes in UAVs.

</details>


### [202] [Semantic Non-Assembly: Privacy by Architectural Inertness Under Component Exposure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22311)
*Sam Ryan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种互补性的隐私保证——语义非组合（Semantic Non-Assembly, SNA），其隐私特性不取决于暴露的难度，而取决于暴露发生时的信息产出。SNA通过防止任何低于阈值的合谋者完成对其输入域的充分赋值来阻止特定谓词评估。满足SNA的架构确保任何少于定义阈值的组件合谋都无法组合出这样的赋值：对任何子阈值组件的完全暴露和解密不会产生可操作数据。基础协议中该保证是结构性的，通过架构而非策略运作，隐私属性在组件受损时可预测地退化而非单点崩塌。参考实例化将结构保证与经过审计的组织约束相结合。论文形式化了该保证，并建立了四个ProVerif验证的属性：设备非关联性、注册表观察者非识别性、提交服务器盲目性以及主动防御门正确性，前三个通过双通道溯源架构实现。Birthmark标准在受限采集硬件上实例化了该保证，展示了在ZK方法计算不可行场景下的可部署性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私框架侧重于机密性、访问控制、适当信息流或统计披露限制，但缺乏一种从信息产出的角度来定义隐私的互补性方法。当发生完全暴露时，传统方法无法保证数据不可用，因此需要一种即使在组件被完全解密的情况下仍能保护隐私的结构性保证。

Method: 提出语义非组合（SNA）形式化保证，通过架构性设计防止低于阈值的合谋者组合出能评估特定谓词的赋值。结合双通道溯源架构实现设备非关联性、注册表观察者非识别性、提交服务器盲目性，并引入主动防御门机制。使用ProVerif进行形式化验证，并在Birthmark Standard上进行硬件实例化。

Result: 通过ProVerif验证了四个关键属性：设备非关联性、注册表观察者非识别性、提交服务器盲目性、主动防御门正确性。Birthmark Standard在受限硬件上成功实例化了该保证，证明其在ZK方法计算不可行场景下的可部署性。

Conclusion: 语义非组合提供了一种新的隐私保证维度，其隐私特性在组件受损时可预测地退化而非单点崩溃，且可在资源受限的硬件上部署，弥补了现有隐私框架和ZK方法的不足。

Abstract: Existing privacy frameworks emphasize confidentiality, access control, appropriate information flow, or statistical disclosure limitation. We introduce a complementary class of privacy guarantee (Semantic Non-Assembly) in which privacy is characterized not by the difficulty of achieving exposure but by the information yield of exposure when it occurs. SNA prevents evaluation of a designated predicate by preventing any sub-threshold coalition from assembling a sufficient assignment to its input domain. An architecture satisfies Semantic Non-Assembly when no coalition of fewer than a defined threshold of components can assemble such an assignment: complete exposure and decryption of any sub-threshold component yields no actionable data. In the base protocol, the guarantee is structural: it operates through architecture, not policy, and its privacy properties degrade predictably under component compromise rather than collapsing at a single point. The reference instantiation combines this structural guarantee with audited organizational constraints, as characterized in Appendix A. This paper formalizes the guarantee and establishes four ProVerif-verified properties: Device Non-Correlation, Registry Observer Non-Identification, Submission Server Blindness, and Active Defense Gate correctness, the first three through a two-channel provenance architecture. The Birthmark Standard instantiates the guarantee on constrained capture hardware, demonstrating deployability where ZK-based approaches are computationally infeasible. All formal properties and scope limitations are documented in Appendix A.

</details>


### [203] [A Post-Quantum Secure Lattice-Based Forward-Secure Identity Based Encryption with Applications to Internet of Things Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22340)
*Abhishek Kumar,Vikas Srivastava,Sumit Kumar Debnath,Pantelimon Stănică*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于环上带误差学习（RLWE）假设的格基前向安全身份基加密（FS-IBE）方案，通过陷门委托与最小覆盖二叉树机制，显著降低了公钥、私钥和密文尺寸，适用于资源受限的物联网环境，并具备抗量子安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网设备资源受限且易受侧信道与密钥提取攻击，传统公钥基础设施开销大，现有格基FS-IBE方案密钥和密文过大，无法直接适用于实际物联网系统。

Method: 基于RLWE假设，采用陷门委托与最小覆盖机制结合二叉树结构，设计高效的格基前向安全身份基加密方案，实现紧凑的系统参数与逐周期密钥更新。

Result: 与现有LWE方案相比，公钥、私钥和密文尺寸均显著降低，更具实用性。

Conclusion: 该方案在保持后量子安全的前提下，大幅减小了密钥与密文开销，更适合实际物联网部署。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of the Internet of Things (IoT) has led to an unprecedented scale of data exchange across heterogeneous and resource-constrained devices. Ensuring confidentiality and secure key management in such environments is challenging. Traditional public-key infrastructures require heavy certificate-handling overhead. Identity-Based Encryption (IBE) offers a lightweight alternative by deriving public keys directly from device identities, making it attractive for IoT deployments. However, IoT devices are highly vulnerable to side-channel and key-extraction attacks, motivating the need for Forward-Secure IBE(FS-IBE), where the compromise of a current secret key does not threaten past communications. Existing FS-IBE constructions based on classical hardness assumptions are not secure in the era of post-quantum, while the lattice-based (LWE-based) forward-secure scheme suffer from large key and ciphertext sizes, limiting their suitability for constrained IoT systems. Here, we propose a new lattice-based fs-IBE scheme in the ring setting, relying on the RLWE assumption to achieve post-quantum security and significant efficiency gains. Our design uses trapdoor delegation with a minimal-cover mechanism over a binary tree. It results in compact public parameters and efficient per-epoch key updates. Compared to prior LWE-based constructions, our scheme reduces public key, secret key, and ciphertext sizes, and thus, making it better suited for practical IoT environments.

</details>


### [204] [Cross-Layer Intrusion Detection in 5G O-RAN: Gains and Limits of Fusing Radio Telemetry with Network Flow Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22450)
*Hamed Fard,Ilya Komarov,Gerhard Wunder*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文评估了在5G O-RAN网络中融合DU无线电遥测和CU网络流记录进行跨层入侵检测的效果。实验表明：无线电特征单独使用的性能与网络流相当甚至更优；融合仅在GRU和Transformer上提升检测率，其余5种模型反而下降；所有配置下均存在27%至46%的DoS与良性流量混淆，表明问题源于窗口统计聚合方法而非模型容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Open RAN的开放性和解耦特性使得同时获取DU层无线电遥测和CU层网络流记录成为可能，这为跨层入侵检测提供了新的数据融合机会，但多模态融合是否真的优于单一模态尚待验证。

Method: 使用7种架构（GRU、Transformer等），在真实5G O-RAN数据集上进行10次不同随机种子的运行分离交叉验证，分别评估仅用无线电特征、仅用网络流特征以及两者融合的入侵检测性能，以ROC-AUC和运行级检测率为指标。

Result: 无线电特征在ROC-AUC和检测率上均匹配或优于网络流；融合仅在两种架构（GRU和Transformer）上在1%假阳性率下提升检测率，其余5种模型反而降低；所有42种配置下均存在27%-46%的DoS与良性流量混淆。

Conclusion: 多模态融合并非普遍有益，其优势局限于单模态检测率均低于0.75的架构；当前检测瓶颈主要来自窗口化统计聚合方法对DoS流量的表征能力不足，而非模型容量的限制。

Abstract: Open RAN disaggregation enables joint analysis of DU radio telemetry and CU-side network-flow records, motivating cross-layer intrusion detection. We evaluate whether fusing these two modalities improves over each individually across seven architectures, using run-disjoint splits over ten seeds on a live 5G O-RAN dataset. Radio features match or outperform network flows on ROC-AUC and run-level detection rate across all architectures. Fusion yields selective ROC-AUC gains but at a one-percent false-positive operating point improves detection rate only for GRU and Transformer, reducing it for the other five models. The benefit is confined to architectures where both single-modality detection rates fall below 0.75. A DoS-to-Benign confusion of 27 to 46 percent persists across all 42 tested configurations of architecture, modality, and window duration, pointing to a limitation in the tested windowed statistical aggregation rather than in model capacity. Code is publicly available.

</details>


### [205] [Lingering Authority: Revocable Resource-and-Effect Capabilities for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22504)
*Igor Santos-Grueiro*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PORTICO是一个针对编码代理的引用监控器，通过可撤销的能力令牌来消除"遗留权限"问题——即资源或效果的访问权限在所需子目标完成后仍被保留。该方法在受控实验中实现了零违规执行，同时通过受控授予保持了任务边界内的工作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码代理通常在任务期间获得广泛的工具访问权限，即使某个资源仅在一个子目标中需要。这种"遗留权限"（lingering authority）导致临时资源或效果的暴露在触发该权限的场景结束后仍存在，带来了安全风险。

Method: PORTICO实现了一个请求-授予-调用（request-grant-invoke）的生命周期管理机制：将显式任务契约编译为初始能力、授予规则、可信关闭谓词和全局拒绝规则。能力扩展通过不透明的、按回合绑定的句柄实现具体化，关闭操作将这些句柄从后续规划器接口中移除，并在副作用生效前拒绝重放攻击。该监控器假设工具经过中介处理且使用健全的类型化目录。

Result: 在受控编码代理任务中，PORTICO记录的已执行合约禁止效果为零；受控授予恢复了被固定窄边界阻止的边界工作。与不撤销的对比系统相比，在关闭切片上两者任务成功率和范围合规性一致，但PORTICO拒绝了10/10的关闭后复用，而对比系统允许了10/10。确定性陈旧写入审计为0/6 vs 6/6。在文件写入、git变异和网络出口的脚本跟踪和六个实时模型跟踪中呈现相同分裂。在四回合同策略诊断中，广泛请求暴露保持零违规执行，但阻止提案从67增加到84。

Conclusion: PORTICO通过可撤销能力令牌和生命周期管理有效解决了编码代理中的"遗留权限"问题，在保持任务完成能力的同时，实现了严格的权限撤销和零违规执行，证明了基于契约的细粒度能力管理在代理安全中的有效性。

Abstract: Coding agents often receive broad tool access for an entire task, even when a resource is needed only for one subgoal. We call this gap lingering authority: a temporary resource/effect capability remains exposed after the episode that justified it has closed.
  PORTICO is a reference monitor for revocable capabilities exposed to the planner. It compiles an explicit task contract into initial capabilities, grant rules, trusted closure predicates, and global deny rules. A request-grant-invoke lifecycle materializes expansions as opaque, epoch-bound handles. Closure removes those handles from the next planner interface and rejects stale replay before side effects. The monitor assumes mediated tools and a sound typed catalog.
  In controlled coding-agent tasks, PORTICO records no executed contract-forbidden effects in the evaluated runs, while controlled grants recover boundary work blocked by a fixed narrow envelope. A non-revoking comparator receives the same initial envelope and the same grants at the same turns. On the closure slice, both systems match task success, scope compliance, and all pre-closure decisions; PORTICO then rejects 10/10 post-closure reuses, while the comparator permits 10/10. A deterministic stale-write audit records 0/6 versus 6/6 executed forbidden effects. Scripted traces and six live model traces over file writes, git mutation, and network egress show the same split. In a four-episode same-policy diagnostic, broad request exposure preserves zero executed forbidden effects but raises blocked proposals from 67 to 84. Frozen real-repository runs, with commits and traces recorded, exercise the same lifecycle on real project layouts.

</details>


### [206] [Detecting and Understanding Vulnerabilities in Fully Homomorphic Encryption Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22519)
*Yiteng Peng,Dongwei Xiao,Zhibo Liu,Zhenlan JI,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出HERTA，首个面向全同态加密（FHE）框架的自动化测试工具，利用基于FHE语义的蜕变测试方法，在三个主流工业框架中发现21个未知bug，部分已被开发者确认修复，并揭示了这些bug对FHE服务安全性的严重威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FHE（全同态加密）框架实现逻辑复杂，容易引入bug，这些bug不会导致程序崩溃，而是静默地破坏加密计算结果，可能导致严重经济损失和安全漏洞。现有测试方法（如差分测试）因FHE涉及随机性和独特数据类型而失效，亟需针对FHE框架的自动化测试工具。

Method: HERTA采用蜕变测试（Metamorphic Testing）方法，设计了一套基于FHE语义的新型蜕变关系（MRs），针对FHE管线中最具挑战性的方面（如密钥生成、加密、解密、同态运算的正确性）进行压力测试，无需手动标注真值即可实现自动化正确性验证。

Result: 在3个领先的工业FHE框架上评估，发现了21个先前未知的bug，其中多个已被开发者确认并修复。通过危害分析，揭示了这些bug对FHE服务完整性和可用性的严重安全影响。

Conclusion: HERTA是首个针对FHE框架的自动化测试工具，证明了蜕变测试在FHE领域检测深层实现bug的有效性，为FHE框架的质量保障和安全性提升提供了重要工具支持。

Abstract: Fully homomorphic encryption (FHE) allows computations to be performed directly on encrypted data without decryption, offering strong privacy guarantees for sensitive data analysis. This capability is important for privacy-sensitive applications like secure cloud computing, finance, and healthcare. The complexity of FHE schemes, however, has hindered their practical adoption. To make FHE accessible to a broader range of developers, a new generation of specialized frameworks has emerged to translate high-level FHE programs into complex FHE operations, introducing a new programming paradigm. However, the inherent complexity of FHE frameworks makes them prone to incorrect implementation logic. Unlike mere crashes, logic bugs in these frameworks can silently corrupt encrypted computation, potentially leading to severe financial losses and security vulnerabilities in FHE-enhanced applications.
  In this work, we introduce HERTA, the first automated testing tool tailored for FHE frameworks. HERTA leverages metamorphic testing to uncover deep-seated implementation bugs and vulnerabilities across the multi-layered FHE software stack. To that end, we design a set of novel metamorphic relations (MRs) derived specifically from FHE semantics. These MRs stress the most challenging aspects of the pipeline, enabling automated correctness testing without the need for a manual ground truth. Our evaluation of HERTA on 3 leading industry frameworks discovered 21 previously unknown bugs, several of which have already been confirmed and fixed by developers. Furthermore, our hazard analysis reveals the critical security impact these bugs pose to the integrity and availability of FHE-based services.

</details>


### [207] [Evidence-Bound Gateway-Path Provenance for Third-Party LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22560)
*Fei Wang,Zebai Tian*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于可信执行的证据绑定LLM网关架构，将运营商控制面与受证明的执行面分离，通过在可信执行环境（AWS Nitro Enclaves）中运行受测量的网关运行时（AGR）来独立验证路由、回退、流处理和计费记录的真实性，客户端通过验证签名元数据和远程证明来确保请求仅在受信任的运行时中被处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的第三方LLM网关在运营商控制下执行路由、回退、流处理和计费决策，客户端无法独立区分诚实中介与路由替换、隐藏回退、流篡改或伪造来源等恶意行为，缺乏对网关操作的可验证性保证。

Method: 将网关架构分为运营商控制面和受证明的执行面，核心组件是受测量的Attested Gateway Runtime (AGR)，仅在AGR中解密请求、执行路径策略、构造上游调用并签署证据；客户端在加密请求前验证发布元数据和新鲜远程证明，密钥绑定到AGR测量值；AGR实施请求级路由、回退和端点约束，返回加密响应流，签署绑定策略、选定路由、端点身份、流承诺和完成元数据的证据。

Result: 在AWS Nitro Enclaves上的Rust原型实现显示机制开销适中，且能够对策略、路由、端点和流证据篡改实现fail-closed检测。

Conclusion: 该架构使客户端能够独立验证网关行为的真实性，有效防止运营商控制的执行面外的各类篡改攻击，为LLM网关的可信中介提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Third-party LLM gateways have become a critical infrastructure layer between applications and external LLM providers. Conventional gateways do more than forward traffic: they decide which provider and model are called, whether fallback occurred, which stream is delivered, and what usage record should be billed. Because these decisions and records are authored inside the operator-controlled service, clients cannot independently distinguish honest mediation from route substitution, hidden fallback, stream manipulation, or forged provenance.
  We present an evidence-bound LLM gateway architecture that separates the operator control plane from an attested execution plane. Within the gateway, a measured Attested Gateway Runtime (AGR) is the only component allowed to decrypt requests, enforce path policy, construct upstream calls, and sign evidence. Clients verify signed release metadata and fresh attestation before encrypting requests to keys bound to the AGR measurement. AGR enforces request-scoped routing, fallback, and endpoint constraints, invokes admitted providers, returns encrypted response streams, and signs evidence binding the policy, selected route, endpoint identity, stream commitments, and completion metadata to the attested runtime. An initial Rust prototype on AWS Nitro Enclaves shows modest mechanism overhead and fail-closed detection of policy, routing, endpoint, and stream-evidence tampering outside the attested runtime.

</details>


### [208] [From CVE to CWE: Syscall-Based HIDS Generalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22581)
*Alexander V. Kozachok,Stanislav G. Vyugov,Shamil G. Magomedov*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文探究基于系统调用轨迹的主机入侵检测系统（HIDS）能否在同一CWE（通用弱点枚举）类别的不同CVE（通用漏洞披露）实例间泛化。实验发现：CWE-307（身份认证绕过）在目标假阳率0.05下F1达0.6976，而CWE-89（SQL注入）和CWE-434（无限制文件上传）效果较差（F1≤0.21）。跨CVE迁移效果依赖于源正常行为的广度，而非CWE标签本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HIDS通常针对单个CVE训练和评估，但在实际防御中，安全人员需要识别同一弱点类型的新攻击实例。作者希望验证基于CWE类别训练的单类异常检测器能否泛化到同类别下的未见CVE。

Method: 使用LID-DS-2021数据集中的6个场景，分为3个CWE家族（CWE-307、CWE-89、CWE-434）。提取每滑动窗口66维Peng-Guo风格特征向量，训练Isolation Forest和SGD One-Class SVM检测器，采用仅正常数据的阈值校准至固定目标FPR。

Result: CWE-307检测器在校准目标FPR=0.05时达到F1=0.6976（精确率0.8994，召回率0.5698）；CWE-89和CWE-434在相同协议下F1≤0.21。跨CVE迁移呈现强方向依赖性，主要受源正常行为广度主导而非CWE标签。

Conclusion: CWE级别的泛化在某些弱点家族（如CWE-307）可行，但在其他家族（CWE-89、CWE-434）基于当前系统调用特征无法实现。校准FPR是此类设置中诚实报告的方法论前提。

Abstract: Host intrusion detection systems (HIDS) based on system-call traces are typically trained and evaluated against individual Common Vulnerabilities and Exposures (CVE) instances. In operational settings, however, defenders need to recognise new exploits of an already known type of weakness. We empirically examine whether a one-class anomaly detector trained on the normal behaviour of a set of CVEs that share a Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) class generalises to a different, unseen CVE inside the same class.
