Using six scenarios drawn from LID-DS-2021 and grouped into three CWE families (CWE-307 broken authentication, CWE-89 SQL injection, CWE-434 unrestricted file upload), we extract a 66-dimensional Peng-Guo-style feature vector per sliding window and train Isolation Forest and SGD One-Class SVM detectors with normal-only thresholds calibrated to fixed target false positive rates. We define and answer four research questions covering self-detection, asymmetric cross-CVE transfer, the value of a combined CWE-level normal profile, and the effect of feature filtering on transferability. The combined CWE-307 detector reaches F1 = 0.6976 at calibration target FPR = 0.05 (precision = 0.8994, recall = 0.5698), whereas CWE-89 and CWE-434 collapse to F1 <= 0.21 under the same protocol. Cross-CVE transfer turns out to be strongly direction-dependent and dominated by the breadth of the source normal profile rather than by the CWE label. We conclude that CWE-level generalisation in HIDS is empirically attainable for some but not all weakness families with current syscall features, and we argue that calibrated FPR is a methodological prerequisite for honest reporting in this setting.

</details>


### [209] [ColumnKeeper: Efficient Solutions to the ColumnDisturb Vulnerability in DRAM-based Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22632)
*Andreas Kosmas Kakolyris,F. Nisa Bostanci,Ataberk Olgun,Ismail Emir Yuksel,Harsh Songara,Konstantinos Marios Sgouras,Umut Baser,Konstantinos Kanellopoulos,A. Giray Yaglikci,Onut Mutlu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现了一种新型DRAM读取干扰现象ColumnDisturb（作用于列而非行，影响三个连续子阵列的所有单元），并提出两种低成本缓解方案：确定性的CK-D（每子阵列两个计数器，性能开销0.15%-1.70%）和概率性的CK-P（以概率刷新相邻子阵列，性能开销0.36%-2.73%），面积开销仅0.1mm²和0.03mm²。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RowHammer和RowPress等读取干扰现象仅关注行级别的扰动，且影响范围局限于少数相邻行。作者发现了一种全新的、根本不同的读取干扰现象ColumnDisturb，它作用于列而非行，且影响范围扩展到三个连续DRAM子阵列的所有单元，现有方案无法防御，亟需新的缓解机制。

Method: 提出两种ColumnDisturb缓解方案：(1) ColumnKeeper-D (CK-D) — 确定性机制，利用DRAM开放式位线架构，每个子阵列设置两个计数器分别追踪奇偶列激活次数，达到阈值时刷新子阵列中一行；(2) ColumnKeeper-P (CK-P) — 概率性机制，中间子阵列行激活时以预设概率刷新三个连续子阵列中的一行，提供可配置的安全保障。

Result: 在实验验证的ColumnDisturb阈值（1M）下，CK-D和CK-P平均单核性能开销仅为0.15%和0.36%；在近未来阈值（128K）下，上升至1.70%和2.73%；低阈值（16K）可通过更小子阵列尺寸或子阵列级并行性实现。面积开销分别为0.1mm²和0.03mm²。

Conclusion: ColumnDisturb是一种全新的DRAM读取干扰现象，ColumnKeeper系列方案能以极低的性能、能耗和面积开销有效缓解该问题，且代码已开源。

Abstract: Modern DRAM chips are vulnerable to read disturbance phenomena such as RowHammer and RowPress, which induce bitflips after accessing nearby rows a certain number of times (the read disturbance threshold). ColumnDisturb is a new, fundamentally different DRAM read disturbance phenomenon. Specifically, ColumnDisturb (i) disturbs DRAM columns instead of rows, and (ii) increases the number of affected DRAM cells from those in only a few neighboring rows to all cells across three consecutive DRAM subarrays.
  We propose ColumnKeeper, the first set of ColumnDisturb mitigations, in two variants: ColumnKeeper-D (CK-D), a deterministic mechanism, and ColumnKeeper-P (CK-P), a probabilistic one. CK-D exploits DRAM's open-bitline architecture to provide deterministic security guarantees at low performance and energy overheads: it uses two counters per subarray to track activations affecting the odd and even columns, and refreshes one row in a subarray when either counter reaches a predetermined threshold. CK-P instead refreshes one row in three consecutive subarrays upon a row activation in the middle subarray, with a predetermined probability, providing configurable security guarantees at low area overhead.
  Both mechanisms prevent ColumnDisturb bitflips at low performance, energy, and area overheads. At the current experimentally-demonstrated ColumnDisturb threshold (1M), CK-D and CK-P incur very low average single-core performance overheads of 0.15% and 0.36%, respectively. For near-future thresholds (128K), these rise to a still low average of 1.70% and 2.73%. Mitigating ColumnDisturb at low thresholds (e.g., 16K) remains possible by adopting smaller subarray sizes or enabling subarray-level parallelism. CK-D and CK-P require low area overheads of 0.1 mm^2 and 0.03 mm^2, respectively. ColumnKeeper is freely available at https://github.com/CMU-SAFARI/ColumnKeeper .

</details>


### [210] [RAVEN: Agentic RAG for Automated Vulnerability Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22647)
*Varun Gadey,Zijie Liu,Alexandra Dmitrienko*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出RAVEN框架，一个基于开源LLM、可本地部署的自动化漏洞修复系统，通过智能检索增强生成（RAG）管道和迭代修复机制，在160个真实CVE漏洞上实现了83.13%的修复成功率，超越了现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化漏洞修复框架大多局限于内存相关漏洞和本地可修复漏洞，对未见漏洞类型的泛化能力探索不足；评估通常限于单一编程语言且高度依赖闭源模型。因此，需要一种可扩展、高效、自主且能泛化到多种漏洞类型的修复框架。

Method: RAVEN框架集成了智能检索增强生成（RAG）管道与受控迭代修复机制。具体包括：1) 多面检索管道，从历史漏洞修复中检索相关信息以引导补丁生成；2) Curator Agent，从目标仓库检索跨文件依赖，解决需要跨文件上下文的复杂漏洞；3) 使用开源LLM，完全本地部署，GPU需求有限。

Result: 在160个真实CWE漏洞上评估，涵盖多种漏洞类型、两种编程语言、未见CWE类别和分布外设置，RAVEN实现了83.13%的总体修复成功率，超越了所有现有最先进的修复框架，且修复成本可忽略不计。

Conclusion: RAVEN证明了基于开源LLM、结合RAG和跨文件依赖检索的自动化漏洞修复框架具有强大的泛化能力和高性能，为实际场景中的自动化安全修复提供了可行且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Automated vulnerability repair has emerged as a promising direction to mitigate the growing number of software vulnerabilities. Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have further accelerated research in automated repair. However, existing frameworks remain largely restricted to memory-related vulnerabilities and locally repairable vulnerability settings, leaving generalization to unseen vulnerability types underexplored. Their evaluations are often limited to a single programming language, and largely rely on proprietary models. In this paper, we propose RAVEN, a scalable, efficient and autonomous framework that integrates an agentic retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipeline with controlled iterative repair in a unified framework. The framework utilizes open-source LLMs in a fully locally deployable setting with limited GPU requirements, while building a multi-faceted retrieval pipeline to retrieve historically relevant vulnerability fixes and guide the patch generation. In addition, RAVEN introduces a dedicated Curator Agent that retrieves cross-file dependencies from the target repository, to fix complex vulnerabilities that cannot be addressed using local vulnerable code alone. We evaluate RAVEN on 160 real-world CVE vulnerabilities across diverse vulnerability types, two programming languages, unseen CWE categories, and out-of-distribution settings. RAVEN achieves an overall repair success rate of 83.13%, outperforming all existing state-of-the-art repair frameworks, while also demonstrating strong generalization capabilities and maintaining the repair cost negligible.

</details>


### [211] [Confidently Wrong: Severity-Aware Calibration of Prompt-Injection Detectors under Attack Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22659)
*Md Anas Biswas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究提示注入检测器的置信度问题，发现当攻击分布发生偏移时，三个主流检测器（ProtectAI-v2和两个Prompt-Guard-2检查点）在漏检的攻击上始终以接近100%的置信度漏检，并在间接行为劫持注入上表现出系统性盲区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作多关注检测器的准确率，忽略了漏检时置信度的影响。当攻击分布偏离干净基准时，检测器可能以高置信度犯错，造成更大的安全风险。

Method: 在单个源校准阈值下，冻结并迁移至五种分布偏移场景，评估三个检测器的严重度指标S（漏检攻击的置信度）、假阴性率和区分度。同时进行黑盒重写实验和指令微调模型对照实验。

Result: 所有检测器在所有偏移下漏检攻击的严重度S保持在0.99-1.00，假阴性率范围0.01-0.97。三个检测器在间接行为劫持注入上全部漏检。标准池化校准误差（0.06）无法反映攻击子集上的严重失校准（0.91）。

Conclusion: 检测器普遍存在以高置信度漏检攻击的严重问题，根源在于基于内容关键字的检测而非注入结构识别。黑盒重写器可利用这一盲区制造高置信度漏检。需要重新审视检测器校准评估方法。

Abstract: Prompt-injection detectors are deployed as guards: a model scores an input and a downstream system trusts or blocks it on that score. I study the confidence of these scores, not only their accuracy, when the attack distribution shifts away from the clean benchmark on which the operating point was chosen. I evaluate three released detectors, ProtectAI-v2 and two Prompt-Guard-2 checkpoints, at a single source-calibrated threshold that I freeze and transport across five shifts. I report a severity metric S, how confident a detector is on the attacks it misses, alongside the false-negative rate and discrimination. Across every shift and every detector, severity on the missed attacks stays between 0.99 and 1.00 while the false-negative rate ranges from 0.01 to 0.97: when these detectors miss, they miss with near-certainty. All three confidently pass indirect behavior-hijack injection, a blind spot unanimous across two vendors and a fourfold size range. Standard pooled calibration error does not register this; one detector it rates well-calibrated, at 0.06, is miscalibrated at 0.91 on the attacks alone. Run against live models, the missed injections leak the majority of working exploits, passing them at the rate they catch others. A controlled experiment traces the cause to content-keying rather than injection structure, an instruction-tuned model used as a judge shows the same hijack blind spot, and a black-box rewriter exploits the content-keying to manufacture working confident misses, most effectively on the most dangerous attack category. Code and data are public.

</details>


### [212] [The Geometry of Refusal: Linear Instability in Safety-Aligned LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22686)
*Shivam Ratnakar,Kartikeya Vats*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出对比对数操控（CLS）方法，通过对比安全与不受限系统提示的隐藏状态来分离"拒绝方向"，揭示LLM的安全对齐存在可操控的线性特征。实验表明，该方法能以零优化的方式绕过安全护栏，在Llama-3.1等模型上达到95%的攻击成功率，同时也能反向增强模型安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM依赖广泛的安全对齐，但安全合规的机制基础仍不透明。作者希望探究安全决策是深层的语义判断还是可操控的线性特征，以揭示对齐机制的脆弱性。

Method: 提出对比对数操控（CLS）框架：(1) 通过对比安全/不安全系统提示的隐藏状态，分离出"拒绝方向"；(2) 直接对输出分布进行干预而非内部激活；(3) 结合前缀注入绕过初始拒绝反应，诱导安全护栏崩塌。

Result: 在7个模型家族上验证：(1) Llama-3.1等"延迟决策"拓扑模型容易被CLS绕过（ASR达95%）；(2) Qwen-2.5等"早期发散"模型在计算中途整合安全机制，更具鲁棒性；(3) CLS在Llama 2上ASR达73%（vs. 22.6%），Qwen 7B上达91%（vs. 79.2%），显著优于激活级方法；(4) 反向操控可增强模型防御能力。

Conclusion: 当前对齐技术创造了一个可操控的"安全轴"，既是关键漏洞也是防御的精确基元。安全实现具有架构决定性，不同模型家族的安全拓扑存在本质差异。

Abstract: Modern Large Language Models (LLMs) rely on extensive safety alignment, yet the mechanistic basis of refusal remains opaque. In this work, we investigate whether safety compliance is a deep semantic decision or a manipulable linear feature. We introduce Contrastive Logit Steering (CLS), a zero-optimization framework that isolates the "refusal direction" by contrasting hidden states derived from safe and unrestricted system prompts. Unlike representation engineering methods that intervene on internal activations, CLS operates directly on the output distribution, serving as a diagnostic probe for alignment fragility. When coupled with prefix injection to bypass initial refusal reflexes, this method induces a phase transition where guardrails collapse. Our experiments on 7 model families reveal that safety implementation is architecturally deterministic. While models like Llama-3.1 exhibit a "Late Decision" topology that is easily bypassed by CLS (reaching 95% ASR in approximately one second), others like Qwen-2.5 demonstrate "Early Divergence" by integrating safety mid-computation. Direct comparison with established activation-level steering methods shows that CLS achieves substantially higher attack success rates on Llama 2 (73% vs. 22.6%) and Qwen 7B (91% vs. 79.2%), demonstrating that logit-level intervention exposes alignment vulnerabilities that hidden-state methods underestimate. Beyond attacks, we show that this linearity enables bidirectional control: inverting the steering vector "hardens" models against jailbreaks without retraining. Our findings suggest that current alignment techniques create a steerable "safety axis" that serves as both a critical vulnerability and a precise primitive for defense.

</details>


### [213] [Black-Box Forensics for Conversational LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22698)
*Isadora White,Yasaman Jafari,Taylor Berg-Kirkpatrick*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对由LLM驱动的聊天机器人进行黑盒取证研究：在无需访问模型参数或系统提示的情况下，实现对基础模型的归因（准确率98%）以及对系统提示的指纹识别（对未见提示的AUC达0.943），从而为追踪AI诈骗提供技术手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于LLM的诈骗行为日益猖獗，黑盒取证对于隐藏在匿名端点背后的对话系统显得至关重要。研究人员需要一种方法，在无法获取模型参数或系统提示的条件下，识别聊天机器人背后的基础模型（归因），以及检测两个端点是否运行完全相同的系统提示（指纹识别），从而将诈骗行为追溯到模型提供商，并将单个诈骗链接为犯罪网络。

Method: 进行实证研究：1）归因分类器——通过几轮非对抗性对话，以98%的准确率识别代理背后的基础模型；2）跨编码器指纹识别方法——对完全未见过的系统提示，AUC达到0.768，F1达到0.703；通过聚合每个目标代理50次交互对话，AUC提升至0.943。

Result: 归因分类器在基础模型识别上达到98%准确率；跨编码器指纹识别方法在未见系统提示上AUC为0.768、F1为0.703，聚合50次对话后AUC提升至0.943。

Conclusion: 通过对未见系统提示的聊天代理进行指纹识别，仅需几轮普通对话即可获得鲁棒的识别精度，为监控和打击LLM驱动的诈骗提供了可行的黑盒取证方案。

Abstract: As LLM-powered scams proliferate, black-box forensics for conversational LLM agents offers a path to accountability for systems hidden behind anonymous endpoints. Identifying the base model behind a chatbot endpoint (attribution), without model parameter access or knowledge of the hidden system prompt, would let investigators trace AI-enabled scams back to the providers whose models power them. Detecting when two endpoints run the exact same system prompt (fingerprinting), even one novel and unseen, would link individual scams into criminal networks and expose silent API changes. We conduct an empirical investigation of both capabilities. Our attribution classifiers identify the base model behind an agent with 98% accuracy from a few turns of non-adversarial conversation. Attribution of system prompts, while possible, requires retraining on a large amount of data for each prompt; system prompts in the wild are unbounded and ever-changing, making this approach costly. To tackle this more open-ended setting, our cross-encoder fingerprinting method achieves an AUC of 0.768 and an F1 of 0.703 on entirely unseen system prompts, and aggregating 50 interaction conversations from each target agent boosts AUC to 0.943. Conversational agents with unseen system prompts can thus be fingerprinted with robust accuracy from a few turns of ordinary conversation.

</details>


### [214] [VeriPort: Automated and Verified Patch Backporting at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22704)
*Jonah Ghebremichael,Wenxin Jiang,Mikola Lysenko,Benjamin Barslev Nielsen,William Enck,Alexandros Kapravelos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: VeriPort是一个端到端自动化系统，能够将安全补丁反向移植到所有受影响版本，并通过证据链验证补丁的有效性和功能正确性。在128个任务中成功率达95.3%，已生成5000+验证补丁，并发现大量漏洞报告错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件供应链安全中，第三方开源依赖的已知漏洞修复通常只针对最新版本，开发者面临升级（可能引入破坏性变更）或手动反向移植安全补丁的两难选择。现有反向移植方法仅针对单一版本且无法提供充分的验证证据。

Method: VeriPort是一个端到端智能体系统，能够针对给定漏洞通告，将补丁反向移植到每个受影响版本。为每个反向移植构建证据链，确认补丁阻止了漏洞利用并保留了预期功能。

Result: 在BackportBench的128个反向移植任务中成功率达95.3%，比最佳现有方案Claude Code高出22.7个百分点；在169个高/严重级别CVE上部署，生成5000+已验证反向移植补丁；发现2100个错误标记为受影响的版本和127个先前未识别的脆弱版本，23个通告已被上游修正。

Conclusion: VeriPort不仅实现了可扩展的自动化补丁反向移植，还能主动发现漏洞报告中的错误，提升软件供应链安全生态的准确性，具有超越反向移植本身的广泛价值。

Abstract: One of the key challenges for securing the software supply chain is addressing known vulnerabilities in third-party open-source dependencies. Security patches are frequently only available for the latest version of a dependency, leaving developers with the choice of either upgrading to the latest version (risking breaking changes) or manually backporting the security fix. Prior work backports to a single version that must be specified in advance and does not produce sufficient evidence to demonstrate that their patches block exploitation and preserve functionality. In this paper, we present VeriPort, an end-to-end agentic system that scalably backports a patch for a given vulnerability advisory to every affected version of the package. For each backport, VeriPort builds a chain of evidence to confirm that the patch blocks exploitation and preserves intended behavior. VeriPort reliably resolves 95.3% of 128 backporting tasks in BackportBench, outperforming the best existing solution (Claude Code) by 22.7 percentage points. We further deployed VeriPort on 169 high- and critical-severity CVEs and have generated over 5,000 verified backported patches. Moreover, VeriPort's value extends beyond simply backporting patches. It uncovered 2,100 versions incorrectly reported as affected and 127 previously unidentified vulnerable versions across 92 advisories, and 23 advisories have since been corrected upstream by removing 387 versions and adding 81.

</details>


### [215] [One-Prompt Censorship Evasion via Generative Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22717)
*Shiyi Ling,Yuhang Gan,Chen Qian*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FlowPaint 是一个利用大型扩散模型的"世界知识"将审查流量语义编辑为良性模式的创新框架，将审查规避重新定义为图像到图像的语义编辑任务，用户只需通过自然语言指令即可对抗多种审查机制，在对抗工业级基于规则和基于学习的审查系统方面均优于现有方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有审查规避工具在多样化的审查机制下鲁棒性不足，且由于需要手动调整适应度或使用领域特定语言进行复杂配置，导致可用性差。亟需一种更简单、更通用的规避方法。

Method: 将审查规避重新定义为语义图像到图像的编辑任务，利用大型扩散模型的"世界知识"自动将受审查流量重塑为良性模式，采用指令微调的扩散架构对网络流进行语义编辑。

Result: 在对抗工业级基于规则的中介设备和基于学习的分类器时，FlowPaint 在多种审查范式下均优于现有的审查规避基线方法。

Conclusion: FlowPaint 通过将审查规避转化为语义编辑任务并利用扩散模型，显著提升了规避的鲁棒性和易用性，用户仅需通过自然语言指令即可应对多样化的审查机制。

Abstract: The escalating arms race between Internet censorship and evasion has driven censors to evolve from static rule-based filtering to sophisticated deep learning-based traffic analysis. While recent automated evasion tools have attempted to counter this by leveraging stochastic search and programmable heuristics, they continue to suffer from insufficient evasion robustness across diverse censorship modalities and poor usability due to complex, mechanism-specific configurations that require manual fitness tuning or domain-specific languages. In this paper, we propose a paradigm shift that reframes censorship evasion as a semantic image-to-image editing task, allowing users to execute it with a single prompt. We introduce FlowPaint, a novel generative framework that leverages the "world knowledge" of large diffusion models to automatically reshape censored traffic into benign patterns. FlowPaint utilizes an instruction-tuned diffusion architecture to perform semantic editing on network flows. Evaluations against both industrial-grade rule-based middleboxes and learning-based classifiers demonstrate that FlowPaint outperforms existing censorship evasion baselines, enabling users to counter diverse censorship paradigms solely by varying natural language instructions

</details>


### [216] [DE-FIVE: Detecting Malicious Image Prompts via Fourier Features and Image Vector Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22779)
*Xingwei Zhong,Varun Sharma,Kar Wai Fok,Vrizlynn L. L. Thing*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DE-FIVE是一个无需训练的恶意图像提示检测框架，通过傅里叶特征和图像向量嵌入（基于少量恶意样本）实现黑盒与白盒混合检测策略，在对抗恶意图像提示（包括间接提示注入）方面显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法存在两个关键不足：一是通常需要大量数据集进行重训练或部署额外的复杂分类器；二是极度缺乏专门针对间接提示注入的防御机制，这构成了本文工作的核心动机。

Method: 提出DE-FIVE框架，包含两个检测器：(1) 黑盒检测器，基于傅里叶域特征操作；(2) 白盒检测器，利用仅需少量恶意样本的图像向量嵌入（视觉编码器的隐藏状态表征）。整个框架无需训练。

Result: 大量实验表明，DE-FIVE框架在检测恶意图像提示方面持续优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: DE-FIVE作为一种无需训练的混合检测框架，有效填补了针对间接提示注入防御的空白，为VLM安全提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision language models (VLMs) employ both visual and textual modalities to enable advanced vision-language inference. However, incorporating visual modalities expands the attack surface of VLMs, making them more susceptible to security threats such as adversarial perturbations and indirect prompt injection, wherein crafted malicious image prompts can elicit unintended model outputs. Existing defense methods against malicious image prompts remain insufficient as they typically demand extensive datasets for retraining or the deployment of additional, complex classifiers. Most critically, there is a profound lack of specialized defense mechanisms specifically targeting indirect prompt injections, a gap that serves as a primary motivation for this work. To address these limitations, we introduce DE-FIVE, a novel training-free framework for detecting malicious image prompts by leveraging Fourier features and the hidden state representations of the visual encoder (image vector embeddings) across perturbations. Specifically, we develop a hybrid detection strategy consisting of a black-box detector that operates on Fourier-domain features and a white-box detector that exploits image vector embeddings derived from only a few-shot malicious set. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines against malicious image prompts.

</details>


### [217] [SelPE: Progressive Selection for Private Structured Text Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22817)
*Xuancheng Zhu,Guoshun Nan,Han Zhang,Ben Niu,Yang Yue,Zixu Wang,Yilian Liu,Min Lei,Xiaofeng Tao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SelPE是一个面向小样本隐私结构化文本合成的选择引导渐进演化框架，通过多批次top-1选择集中使用隐私预算，结合两阶段生成流水线和多通道距离核，在严格差分隐私约束下实现了结构有效性、保真度和下游效用的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私敏感领域（如临床分诊笔记、金融交易日志）中结构化文本记录访问受限，仅有少量样本可用于模型开发，而现有差分隐私数据合成方法存在不足：表格方法无法建模自由文本，文本方法常破坏结构约束。

Method: SelPE采用选择引导的渐进演化框架，将隐私预算集中于多批次top-1选择；通过两阶段生成流水线解耦语义抽象与模式实现；使用多通道距离核联合建模文本、类别和数值字段；引入非私有对比扩展机制提升多样性且不增加隐私成本。

Result: 实验表明，SelPE在严格差分隐私预算下，特别是在低数据量场景中，一致性地提升了结构有效性、保真度和下游效用。

Conclusion: SelPE通过创新的隐私预算集中策略和两阶段解耦生成，有效解决了小样本场景下结构化文本的差分隐私合成难题。

Abstract: Many data-driven applications rely on structured textual records, such as clinical triage notes and financial transaction logs, for downstream learning and decision-making. In privacy-sensitive domains, access to such records is strictly regulated, often resulting in only a small number of available private examples for model development and analysis. Yet existing differential privacy data synthesis methods fall short: tabular techniques cannot faithfully model free-form text, while text-based approaches often break structural constraints. We propose SelPE, a selection-guided progressive evolution framework for small-sample private structured text synthesis. Rather than relying on noisy aggregation or private model training, SelPE concentrates privacy budget on a sequence of multi-batch top-1 selections, enabling efficient guidance under tight privacy constraints. To support faithful and valid synthesis, SelPE decouples semantic abstraction from schema realization via a two-stage generation pipeline, and evaluates candidates using a multi-channel distance kernel that jointly models textual, categorical, and numeric fields in their native representations. A non-private contrastive expansion mechanism further promotes diversity without incurring additional privacy cost. Extensive Experiments demonstrate that SelPE consistently improves structural validity, fidelity, and downstream utility under strict differential privacy budgets, particularly in low-data regimes.

</details>


### [218] [What You See Is Not What You Execute: Memory-Based Runtime SBOM Generation for Supply Chain Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22827)
*Hala Alia,Andrew Case,Irfan Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MEM-SBOM，首个基于内存取证从Python应用运行时状态生成SBOM的框架，无需预先埋点，能100%准确提取模块、解析版本、构建依赖图并识别漏洞函数。对51个真实Python应用评估表明，它比现有SBOM工具更准确，能捕获运行时包并发现漏洞调用，为软件供应链安全与事件响应提供取证层面支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SBOM生成依赖元数据或文件系统制品，无法捕获动态语言（如Python）运行时实际加载和执行的组件；基于插桩的方式需提前部署且要求系统全程可观测，难以适用于生产环境和事件响应场景。

Method: 提出MEM-SBOM，基于内存取证框架，从Python解释器内部数据结构恢复运行时模块，解析包版本，并通过字节码分析构建依赖图与识别漏洞函数。实现为Volatility 3插件套件。

Result: 在51个真实Python应用上实现100%提取准确率；识别出Streamlit是唯一调用tornado依赖中易受攻击例程的应用；恢复了现有SBOM工具遗漏的所有运行时包，提供更准确的依赖图和漏洞评估。

Conclusion: MEM-SBOM为软件供应链安全与事件响应提供了具备取证可靠性的运行时视图，能有效弥补现有SBOM工具的不足。

Abstract: Modern software development relies heavily on third-party components from public repositories, expanding the software supply chain attack surface. In response to these growing risks, federal initiatives have advanced the Software Bill of Materials (SBOM) as a standardized mechanism for improving transparency by describing software components, dependencies, and their relationships. However, SBOMs built from metadata or filesystem artifacts fail to capture the components loaded and executed at runtime, especially in dynamic ecosystems such as Python. Moreover, generating runtime SBOMs through instrumentation requires monitoring to be deployed in advance and the system to remain observable throughout execution. Such conditions are difficult to satisfy in production environments and incident-response scenarios. Volatile memory, in contrast, provides a reliable source for recovering the actual runtime state of a running application without requiring prior instrumentation. Therefore, this paper presents MEM-SBOM, the first memory forensics framework that generates SBOMs directly from the runtime state of Python applications. It recovers the modules from the interpreter's internal structures, resolves package versions, and analyzes bytecode to build dependency graphs and identify vulnerable functions. We implemented MEM-SBOM as a suite of Volatility 3 plugins and evaluated it against 51 real-world Python applications. It achieves 100% extraction accuracy, identifies Streamlit as the only application that calls the vulnerable routines of the tornado dependency, and recovers all runtime packages missed by existing SBOM tools, providing more accurate dependency graphs and better vulnerability assessment. These capabilities make MEM-SBOM a practical foundation for software supply chain security and incident response by providing a forensically sound runtime view of what is executed on a system.

</details>


### [219] [HADES: Privacy-Preserving Federated Learning via Selective Feature Encryption and Hybrid Model Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22928)
*Ergün Batuhan Kaynak,Kerem Bayramoglu,Sinem Sav*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: HADES提出了一种混合联邦学习框架，通过PCA识别并仅加密最隐私敏感的特征，结合多同态加密（MHE）与非加密训练管道，在保证隐私保护的同时显著降低计算开销，并匹配原始FL的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习中的完全加密训练管道计算开销过高，亟需一种既能保护隐私又保持计算效率的方案。

Method: HADES使用PCA识别隐私敏感特征，对敏感特征采用多同态加密（MHE）训练加密网络，对剩余特征训练明文网络，并通过融合机制无缝集成两者。同时引入通用打包方案消除冗余旋转操作。

Result: HADES匹配了原始FL的准确率，同时通过选择性加密实现了隐私保护和优化的运行时间。

Conclusion: 选择性加密策略能够在隐私保护与计算效率之间取得良好平衡，为联邦学习中的高效隐私保护训练提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we address the challenge of privacy-preserving training in federated learning (FL) by introducing a novel framework that selectively encrypts only the most privacy-sensitive features while leaving the remaining data and the corresponding model portion unencrypted. We propose HADES, a hybrid system that identifies and encrypts the most critical features, ensuring both privacy protection and computational efficiency. Unlike fully encrypted FL training pipelines, which suffer from high computational overhead, HADES integrates an encrypted and non-encrypted training pipeline via a fusion mechanism, enabling seamless interaction between encrypted and plaintext model representations. To achieve this, we use PCA to identify and encrypt the most privacy-sensitive features, which significantly reduces reconstruction attack success in FL. Building on this insight, we design a hybrid FL system that trains an end-to-end encrypted network via multiparty homomorphic encryption (MHE) on the selected features while simultaneously training a plaintext network on the remaining features. These two networks are then integrated using a fusion mechanism. We also introduce a general packing scheme that eliminates redundant rotations by considering the entire neural network architecture. Finally, we demonstrate that HADES matches the accuracy of vanilla FL while preserving privacy and achieving optimized runtime through selective encryption.

</details>


### [220] [CITADEL: CSI-Based Jamming Detection and Open-Set Classification for IIoT Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22939)
*Aymen Bouferroum,Ildi Alla,Valeria Loscri,Abderrahim Benslimane,Vincent Lenders*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CITADEL，一种基于CSI的轻量级两级分层流水线，用于检测和分类IIoT网络中的射频干扰攻击，在已知攻击检测、零日攻击发现和对抗鲁棒性方面均优于现有方法，推理仅需14.2 ms/95.9 mJ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 射频干扰严重威胁无线IIoT网络的可用性，现有检测分类技术要么特征粗粒度信息不足，要么需要难以在百节点IIoT部署中实现的硬件和吞吐量，缺乏轻量且有效的解决方案。

Method: CITADEL采用轻量级两级分层流水线，仅使用商品IIoT设备原生可获取的信道状态信息（CSI）进行测量，结合闭集分类、开集零日攻击检测以及对对抗逃逸的抵抗机制。

Result: 在6种已知攻击类型和15个零日场景下，已知攻击检测率100%，零日检测率97.1%，端到端假阳性率0.4%；白盒和黑盒威胁模型下梯度逃逸率低于2%，最强CSI攻击生成器平均逃逸率低于5%；推理耗时14.2 ms，能耗95.9 mJ。

Conclusion: CITADEL是首个将CSI特征转化为端到端流水线的系统，在检测、泛化、鲁棒性三个维度上均超越现有基线方法，为大规模IIoT网络安全提供了一种实用的轻量级解决方案。

Abstract: Radio frequency jamming poses a critical threat to the availability of wireless Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) networks. Existing detection and classification techniques are poorly suited to this setting: coarse signal-strength and cross-layer features lack information richness, while raw I/Q baseband approaches require hardware and throughput that is impractical at the scale of hundred-node IIoT deployments. This paper presents CITADEL, a lightweight two-stage hierarchical pipeline that uses only Channel State Information (CSI) measurements, which are natively available on commodity IIoT devices, to detect and classify jamming attacks including previously unseen ones. While prior work has shown that jamming leaves observable CSI signatures, CITADEL is the first system to translate this insight into an end-to-end pipeline that jointly achieves closed-set classification of known attacks, open-set detection of zero-day attacks, and resistance to adversarial evasion. Evaluated across 6 known attack types and 15 zero-day scenarios, CITADEL achieves 100% known-attack detection and 97.1% zero-day detection at a 0.4% end-to-end false positive rate. Under adversarial evaluation spanning white-box and black-box threat models, gradient-based evasion remains below 2% across all tested perturbation budgets and the strongest published CSI attack generator achieves less than 5% average evasion. A systematic comparison against eight baselines confirms that no existing method achieves comparable performance on CSI data across all three axes: detection, generalization, and robustness. The full pipeline completes inference in 14.2 ms at 95.9 mJ on an edge GPU, establishing CITADEL as a practical solution for large-scale IIoT network security.

</details>


### [221] [Public Diffusion Models, Private Images: Key-Controlled Inversion for Conditional Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22988)
*Lijunxian Zhang,Weihai Li,Bin Liu,Zikai Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对白盒扩散模型（参数公开）的密钥控制逆帧框架，通过向逆帧公式注入密钥相关噪声，将扩散模型的误差传播特性转化为安全资产，实现只有持正确密钥的用户才能恢复原始图像，并证明该方案在理论上满足IND-CPA安全，对抗优势随安全参数指数级衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型的访问控制研究多假设黑盒场景（参数保密），而实际中模型参数常被公开（如开源库或发布的检查点），导致任何获取中间潜在表示的用户都能逆帧恢复原始图像，存在严重安全风险。

Method: 提出密钥控制逆帧框架，利用扩散模型误差传播特性（小扰动被指数级放大），在逆帧公式中注入密钥相关噪声，使得只有正确密钥能重构原图，错误密钥输出无法识别的结果。

Result: 理论上证明密文分布满足IND-CPA安全，对抗优势在安全参数下指数级小，对PPT对手可忽略；实验上在多个模型和数据集上验证了安全保证，并展示了跨模型鲁棒性，即注入的密钥噪声不会放大模型差异导致的性能下降。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了白盒扩散模型安全保护的空白，将扩散模型原本的安全缺陷（误差传播）转化为安全优势，提供了一种可证明安全的访问控制方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models are often deployed in settings where model parameters are publicly accessible (e.g., open-source libraries or released checkpoints). This white-box scenario creates a serious security risk: any user who obtains an intermediate latent representation can invert the process to recover the original input image. Most prior work on access control for generative models assumes a black-box model (i.e., parameters are kept secret), typically under an honest-but-curious adversary. By contrast, we address the more challenging and realistic white-box setting where all parameters are public.
  We present a key-controlled inversion framework that turns the inherent error propagation of diffusion models, which exponentially amplifies small perturbations, into a security asset. By injecting key-dependent noise into the inversion formula, we ensure that only a user with the correct key can reconstruct the original image; any other key yields unrecognizable output.
  Theoretically, by leveraging existing error-propagation theory for diffusion models, we prove that the resulting ciphertext distribution is IND-CPA secure and derive that the adversary's advantage is exponentially small in a tunable security parameter, hence negligible for any probabilistic polynomial-time (PPT) adversary. Experimentally, we validate these security guarantees across several models and datasets and further demonstrate cross-model robustness, that the injected key noise does not amplify the performance drop caused by model discrepancies.

</details>


### [222] [VCT: A Verifiable Transcript System for LLM Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23003)
*Ruilin Xing,Feihong Li,Jiayue Liu,Jiali Zheng,Wei Liu,Wanzhi Xie*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Verifiable Conversation Transcript (VCT)，一种三层次加密数据结构，通过账户级认证状态转换来捕捉LLM对话的非线性演化（如重新提示、回复再生、会话删除、多设备并发和选择性共享），保证对话记录的完整性、一致性、可验证共享性和不可否认性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统线性防篡改日志无法捕捉LLM对话固有的非线性演化特性（如基于历史查询的重新提示、回复再生、会话删除、多设备并发和选择性共享），迫切需要一种能够完整记录并验证复杂LLM对话交互过程的取证方案。

Method: VCT构建三层次加密数据结构：(1) 原子级问答对形成分支级哈希链；(2) 分支尾部聚合为会话级Merkle根；(3) 所有会话根进一步聚合为账户级Merkle根，由用户和服务器联合签名锚定。引入带删除屏障的序列化状态转换协议消除删除与修改冲突，确定性状态合并协议保留并发的非删除增量操作，增量拒绝检查与gossip协议使异步设备自主检测视图分叉。

Result: 安全分析证明在标准密码学假设下VCT保证了完整性、一致性、可验证共享性和不可否认性。Python原型评估显示核心操作密码学延迟在亚毫秒到低毫秒级别，在21 KB文本的典型配置下，安全元数据的存储开销仅为0.9%。

Conclusion: VCT可有效部署于生产级LLM平台的高风险取证审查场景，在极小的性能与存储开销下提供了对非线性LLM对话记录的完整安全保障。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) interaction records are increasingly vital in digital forensics and compliance auditing. However, traditional linear tamper-evident logs fail to capture the inherent non-linear evolution of LLM conversations, such as re-prompting based on historical queries, response regeneration, session deletion, multi-device concurrency, and selective sharing. To address this issue, this paper proposes Verifiable Conversation Transcript (VCT), which abstracts complex non-linear LLM semantic operations into account-level authenticated state transitions. VCT constructs a three-tier cryptographic data structure: atomic Q&A pairs form branch-level hash chains, branch tails aggregate into session-level Merkle roots, and all session roots are further aggregated into an account-level Merkle root anchored by joint signatures from both the user and the server. VCT introduces a serialized state transition protocol with deletion barriers to eliminate conflicts between deletion and modification, complemented by a deterministic state-merge protocol to preserve concurrent non-deletion incremental operations. Furthermore, incremental denial checks and a gossip protocol enable asynchronous user devices to autonomously detect view forks caused by malicious servers and generate non-repudiable forensic evidence. Security analysis demonstrates that, under standard cryptographic assumptions, VCT guarantees the integrity, consistency, verifiable shareability, and non-repudiation of account-level conversation records. Evaluation of a Python prototype shows that the cryptographic latency of core operations is within sub-millisecond to low-millisecond ranges. Under a realistic configuration with 21 KB of text, security metadata introduces a negligible storage overhead of only 0.9%, validating the deployment feasibility of VCT for high-stakes forensic review on production-grade LLM platforms.

</details>


### [223] [Understanding the Stealthy BGP Hijacking Risk in the ROV Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23071)
*Yihao Chen,Qi Li,Ke Xu,Zhuotao Liu,Jianping Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了ROV部分部署导致的隐蔽BGP劫持威胁，通过实证和理论分析揭示了14.1%的整体成功概率，并开发了SHAMAN框架实现高效的互联网规模风险评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ROV部分部署会引发一种未被充分研究的隐蔽BGP劫持威胁，即恶意路由在不触发受害者警报的情况下劫持流量。缺乏真实世界案例记录和系统性特征描述，亟需填补这一空白。

Method: 形式化定义隐蔽BGP劫持；通过路由表不一致性提出启发式发现方法；进行首次实证研究追踪真实案例；开发SHAMAN框架，整合多源ROV部署数据、基于矩阵的高效路由推理方法、以及"受害者-目标-劫持者"三元组统计模型进行风险评估。

Result: SHAMAN将互联网规模路由生成时间从3个月以上降至5.22小时；评估83亿条路由发现隐蔽BGP劫持整体成功概率14.1%，特定目标攻击成功率达99.5%；真实数据集验证显示事件级准确率高达95.9%。

Conclusion: 隐蔽BGP劫持在现实ROV部署下构成严重安全威胁，SHAMAN框架能够高效准确地进行大规模风险评估，为网络安全防御提供重要支撑。

Abstract: The partial deployment of Route Origin Validation (ROV) poses an unexpected security threat known as stealthy BGP hijacking, i.e., a particularly elusive form of BGP hijacking where malicious routes divert traffic without reaching (and thus alerting) the victims. This risk remains largely unexplored, with neither documented real-world incidents nor systematic characterization available. To bridge this gap, we formalize stealthy BGP hijacking and propose heuristics to discover potential instances through routing table discrepancies. We conduct the first empirical study to track and profile stealthy BGP hijacking in the wild, contributing a curated real-world incident dataset and a long-term monitoring service. Inspired by the empirical insights, we further conduct an analytical study to exhaustively assess the risk. This requires accurate ROV deployment data, complete Internet-wide routes, and tailored analytical models. To address these challenges, we develop SHAMAN, a BGP route inference framework dedicated to assessing stealthy BGP hijacking risk. SHAMAN consolidates multiple sources to construct an accurate view of ROV deployment, infers complete Internet-wide routes through a highly efficient matrix-based approach, and facilitates statistical risk analysis via a "victim-target-hijacker" 3-tuple model. By reducing the time for generating Internet-scale routes from over three months to just 5.22 hours, SHAMAN enables systematic risk assessment across 8.3 billion generated routes under real-world ROV deployment. Our findings reveal a 14.1% overall success probability for stealthy BGP hijacking, with targeted attacks reaching 99.5% success in specific cases. Validation against our real-world dataset shows up to 95.9% incident-level accuracy, demonstrating the fidelity of our analytical results.

</details>


### [224] [Safety in Self-Evolving LLM Agent Systems: Threats, Amplification, and Case Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23075)
*Ruixiao Lin,Xinhao Deng,Qingming Li,Jianan Ma,Yunhao Feng,Yuqi Qing,Zhenyuan Li,Yechao Zhang,Shiwen Cui,Changhua Meng,Tianwei Zhang,Xingjun Ma,Qi Li,Ke Xu,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了自进化LLM智能体系统的新型安全威胁，通过MLAS矩阵识别出25个攻击面单元中17个面临严重威胁，并发现进化原生设计激活了3.5倍攻击面且攻击持久率达100%，静态防御仅能拦截2.5%攻击，表明自进化使所有已知攻击从会话级升级为血统级持久威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自进化LLM智能体系统能够自主更新模型参数、记忆、工具和架构，引入了全新的威胁景观，其中对抗性影响会被永久编码、跨代自我放大并通过种群传播而无需持续攻击者访问，亟需系统性安全分析。

Method: 提出模块-生命周期攻击面（MLAS）矩阵，将攻击面分解为5个功能模块（大脑、认知资源、执行、自设计、集体）×5个生命周期阶段（引导、提议、评估、提交、服务），并通过两个开源框架的对比案例研究进行实证分析。

Result: 25个攻击面单元中17个面临无有效部分缓解的严重威胁；识别出7种交叉放大效应；进化原生设计激活3.5倍攻击面单元，攻击持久率100%（40/40负载），而共置安全扫描仪仅拦截2.5%攻击。

Conclusion: 自进化将所有已知攻击类别从会话级转变为血统级持久威胁，催生全新攻击类别，使静态防御在结构上失效，亟需进化感知的安全框架和自修改系统的形式化验证。

Abstract: Self-evolving LLM agent systems, which autonomously update their model parameters, memory, tools, and architectures, introduce a qualitatively new threat landscape in which adversarial influences become permanently encoded, self-amplify across generations, and propagate through populations without sustained attacker access. We present a systematic security and privacy analysis organized around the Module-Lifecycle Attack Surface (MLAS) matrix, which decomposes the attack surface into five functional modules (Brain, Cognitive Resource, Execution, Self-Design, Collective) $\times$ five lifecycle stages (Bootstrap, Propose, Evaluate, Commit, Serve). Analysis of the resulting 25 cells reveals that 17 face critical threats for which no effective partial mitigation. We identify seven cross-cutting amplification effects that interact synergistically and cannot be addressed by securing individual modules in isolation. Comparative case studies of two open-source frameworks demonstrate that evolution-native design activates $3.5\times$ more attack surface cells and achieves a 100% attack persistence rate (40/40 payloads across all CIA+Privacy categories), while co-located security scanners block only 2.5% of attacks. Our findings establish that self-evolution converts every known attack category from session-bounded to lineage-persistent, gives rise to entirely new attack classes, and renders static defenses structurally inadequate, motivating evolution-aware security frameworks and formal verification for self-modifying systems.

</details>


### [225] [Understanding the (In)Security of Vibe-Coded Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23130)
*Junquan Deng,Zhiyu Fan,Ruijie Meng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了通过"氛围编码"（vibe coding）开发的应用程序的安全性，发现这类应用存在特有的漏洞模式（如占位逻辑、未过滤输入、密钥泄露），根源在于AI智能体在开发周期中的系统性局限（如记忆丢失、局部优化目标、安全知识不足），且即使提升LLM能力也无法消除根本性安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI智能体驱动的氛围编码范式迅速普及，开发大量委托给AI系统，但其安全性尚不明确，迫切需要系统性的实证研究来回答"氛围编码开发的应用有多（不）安全"这一根本问题。

Method: 收集了大量使用流行AI智能体开发的真实应用语料库，设计了结合AI辅助代码审计与人工验证的漏洞分析框架，对部署的应用进行漏洞流行度、严重程度和根本原因分析。

Result: (1) 氛围编码应用存在特有漏洞模式（占位逻辑、未过滤输入、密钥泄露）; (2) 漏洞源于AI智能体的系统性局限（记忆丢失、局部优化、安全知识不足）; (3) 提升LLM能力和改进提示策略可降低漏洞出现率但无法消除安全风险。

Conclusion: 研究为氛围编码应用的安全格局提供了实证理解，为应对软件开发向AI系统委托带来的安全挑战奠定了基础。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have enabled vibe coding, an emerging software development paradigm in which users create applications primarily through natural-language interactions with AI agents. Due to its low barrier to entry, vibe coding is rapidly gaining adoption in practice. Unlike conventional AI-assisted programming, where developers remain responsible for implementation and code review, vibe coding delegates a substantial portion of development to AI systems. This shift raises a fundamental question: how (in)secure are applications developed through vibe coding? In this paper, we conduct a systematic study of the security of vibe-coded applications. We collect a large corpus of real-world applications developed using popular AI agents and design a vulnerability analysis framework that combines agent-assisted code auditing with human validation. Using this framework, we examine the prevalence, severity, and root causes of vulnerabilities in the deployed vibe-coded applications. Our study reveals several key findings: (1) vibe-coded applications exhibit recurring vulnerability patterns that differ from those commonly observed in conventional software development workflows, including placeholder logic, unfiltered input, and secret exposure; (2) these vulnerabilities arise from systematic limitations of AI agents throughout the vibe-coding lifecycle, such as memory loss, locally optimized objectives and insufficient security knowledge; and (3) while advances in LLM capabilities and improved prompting strategies can reduce the incidence of vulnerabilities, they do not eliminate the underlying security risks. Overall, our study provides an empirical understanding of the security landscape of vibe-coded applications and lays the groundwork for addressing the security challenges introduced by the growing delegation of software development to AI systems.

</details>


### [226] [Rising From the Ashes: How Agentic AI is Unblocking Challenges in Cybersecurity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23138)
*Gabriela F. Ciocarlie,Kathrin Grosse,Somesh Jha,Daryna Oliynyk,Andrew Paverd,Christian Wressnegger*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文探讨了Agentic AI在解决安全领域高成本且效率低下问题方面的潜力，通过16个案例研究（包括供应链分析）展示了AI如何通过直接处理自然语言或代码来扩展可行防御的范围，缓解安全防御中的劳动密集型瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全领域长期存在高成本、低效率的挑战，许多问题因人力成本过高而被视为难以解决或无法有效应对，需要新的技术途径来突破这些瓶颈。

Method: 采用映射分析的方法，将开放的安全问题与新兴的Agentic AI能力进行对应，并通过16个案例研究（如供应链分析）进行具体说明。

Result: Agentic AI能够直接摄入和推理自然语言或代码，从而扩大可行防御的范围，在16个案例中展现出缓解安全防御瓶颈的潜力。

Conclusion: Agentic AI有望成为解决安全领域劳动密集型瓶颈的有效工具，能显著扩展防御者的能力边界。

Abstract: Security remains a high-cost challenge, with many problems historically deemed inefficient to address or effectively unsolvable. A significant number of these problems stem from labor-intensive tasks that create bottlenecks in defensive approaches. Agentic AI has the potential to alleviate these bottlenecks by directly ingesting and reasoning over natural language or code, thereby expanding the scope of feasible defenses. In this paper, we map open security problems to emergent agentic AI capabilities. To illustrate this potential, we examine 16 case studies, including supply chain analysis, highlighting how agentic AI may benefit defenders.

</details>


### [227] [A Hybrid Intrusion Detection System for Electric Vehicle Charging Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23236)
*Charukeshi Joglekar,Chijioke Eze,Danni Xiang,Antonello Monti*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合入侵检测系统（IDS），通过整合网络层（NIDS）和主机层（HIDS）的双层检测机制，对电动汽车充电站（EVCS）生态系统中的多种网络攻击（如虚假数据注入、拒绝服务、后门、 cryptojacking 等）进行多分类检测。基于CICEVSE2024数据集，NIDS达到99.99%准确率，HIDS达到83.47%准确率，显著优于单源检测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EVCS入侵检测系统存在两大缺陷：一是缺乏同时覆盖网络层（cyber）和物理层（physical）的综合性检测方案；二是未能有效应对EVCS生态系统互联特性带来的独特安全挑战。本文旨在填补这些空白。

Method: 提出双层混合IDS架构：NIDS监控网络流量，HIDS监控主机级活动；采用CICEVSE2024数据集进行多分类（涵盖FDIA、侦察、DoS、后门、cryptojacking等攻击类型）；通过双层次集成方法实现网络层与物理层的协同检测。

Result: NIDS组件对基于网络的攻击检测准确率达99.99%；HIDS组件对FDIA、cryptojacking、后门、所有DoS、除Slowloris扫描外的所有Recon攻击检测准确率达83.47%。双层检测显著优于文献中的单源检测方法。

Conclusion: 提出的混合IDS有效解决了EVCS生态系统的网络-物理协同安全检测问题，双层架构相比单一检测源具有显著性能优势，为智能电网中EVCS的安全防护提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The integration of Electric Vehicle Charging Stations (EVCSs) into the smart grid necessitates sophisticated digital infrastructure for their management and coordination, which expands the attack surface and makes both the power grid and EVCSs vulnerable to cyberattacks. This research addresses critical gaps in existing EVCS Intrusion Detection Systems (IDS) by proposing a hybrid IDS that integrates attack detection on both the cyber and physical layer of the EVCS ecosystem. The proposed hybrid IDS utilizes a dual-layer integration method, which combines network-based IDS (NIDS) and host-based IDS (HIDS). This approach facilitates for comprehensive monitoring of both network traffic through the NIDS and host-level activities via the HIDS, effectively addressing the unique challenges posed by the interconnected nature of EVCS ecosystems. Utilizing the recent CICEVSE2024 dataset, the IDS presented in this work performs multiclass classification across various attack types, including False Data Injection Attacks (FDIAs), reconnaissance, denial of service, backdoor, and cryptojacking attacks. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach achieves excellent detection accuracy, with the NIDS component reaching 99.99\% accuracy for network-based attacks and the HIDS component achieving 83.47\% accuracy on FDIA, cryptojacking, backdoor, all DoS, all Recon except Slowloris Scan attacks. This dual-layer detection significantly outperforms single-source detection approaches previously presented in literature.

</details>


### [228] [TooBad: Backdoor Diffusion Models with Ultra-Low Poison Rate and Imperceptible Trigger](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23362)
*Vu Tuan Truong,Long Bao Le*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TooBad提出了一种针对扩散模型的后门攻击框架，通过触发优化技术，在极低投毒率（0.5%）和极少训练轮次（3-5轮）下实现高攻击成功率（>85%），同时能规避现有防御机制，揭示了对扩散模型的新型安全威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型后门攻击方法在攻击性能、隐蔽性、时间复杂度和投毒率之间存在关键权衡，高攻击性能通常需要高投毒率和长时间训练，导致隐蔽性降低且易被防御机制检测。

Method: 提出了一种针对扩散模型定制的触发优化技术（trigger optimization），通过优化后门触发器设计，在极低投毒率下实现高效的后门注入。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上，TooBad在0.5%投毒率下达到>85% ASR（传统方法需10%）；在5%投毒率下仅需3-5轮注入即可接近100% ASR（现有方法需30-50轮及双倍投毒率）；能轻松规避最先进的防御方法并保持高实用性。

Conclusion: 揭示了扩散模型面临的严重安全威胁，强调了针对此类隐蔽高效攻击需要更鲁棒防御机制的必要性。

Abstract: Diffusion models (DMs), despite their impressive capabilities across a wide range of generative tasks, have been shown to be vulnerable to backdoor attacks. However, existing backdoor methods face critical trade-offs among key factors: attack performance, stealthiness, time complexity, and required poison rates. For example, achieving high attack performance typically demands a high poison rate and prolonged training, which undermines stealthiness, making the attack more detectable by backdoor defenses. This paper proposes TooBad (trigger optimization for backdoor diffusion models), a backdoor framework which introduces a novel DM-tailored trigger optimization technique to dramatically enhance the performance of backdoor attacks on DMs. Experiments on representative benchmarks such as CIFAR-10 show that TooBad can achieve high ASRs ($> 85$%) at only 0.5% poison rate, significantly lower than the 10% typically required by prior work on the same datasets. At 5% poison rate, TooBad reaches nearly 100% ASR within just 3-5 backdoor injection epochs, whereas existing methods need at least 30-50 epochs at double the poison rate for comparable results. Despite its potency, TooBad easily evades SOTA defenses and maintains high utility. These results reveal a critical threat on DMs and highlight the need for more robust defenses against such stealthy yet efficient attacks.

</details>


### [229] [FlexServe: A Fast and Secure LLM Serving System for Mobile Devices with Flexible Resource Isolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23370)
*Yinpeng Wu,Yitong Chen,Lixiang Wang,Jinyu Gu,Zhichao Hua,Yubin Xia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FlexServe提出了一种基于ARM TrustZone的快速安全LLM推理系统，通过解耦安全资源的访问权限与管理权限，实现了可回收的安全内存和安全NPU，在保证安全性的同时显著降低了性能开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在移动设备上使用ARM TrustZone保护LLM推理时，存在两个挑战：一是资源隔离机制不灵活，导致安全世界和普通世界的资源分配僵化；二是安全资源管理效率低下，带来巨大的性能开销。这严重影响了安全推理和普通应用的性能。

Method: 核心思路是解耦安全资源的访问权限与管理权限：普通世界的OS不能访问安全资源，但可以像管理普通资源一样对其进行分配和回收。具体包括三个组成部分：1) Recallable Resource Isolation机制，构造Recallable Secure Memory (Flex-Mem)和Recallable Secure NPU (Flex-NPU)；2) 基于上述机制，在安全世界中运行LLM推理；3) FlexServe Framework实现安全世界中LLM推理与普通世界OS协同进行安全内存管理。

Result: 与两种基于TrustZone的基线方案相比，FlexServe实现了平均TTFT加速比分别为10.05倍（对比朴素基线）和2.44倍（对比优化基线）。

Conclusion: FlexServe通过解耦安全资源的访问与管理权限，有效解决了TrustZone保护LLM推理时的资源隔离僵化和管理效率低下问题，为移动设备上的安全LLM推理提供了高性能方案。

Abstract: Device-side Large Language Models (LLMs) have grown explosively, offering stronger privacy and higher availability than their cloud-side counterparts. During LLM inference, both the model weights and the user data are valuable, and attackers may compromise the OS kernel to steal them. ARM TrustZone is the de facto hardware-based isolation technology on mobile devices, used to protect sensitive applications from a compromised OS. However, protecting LLM inference with TrustZone incurs significant overhead to both the secure inference and the normal aplications, due to two challenges: the inflexible resource isolation and the inefficient secure resource management.
  To address these challenges, this paper presents FlexServe, a fast and secure LLM inference system for mobile devices. The key idea is to decouple the access permission from the management permission of secure resources, so that the normal-world OS cannot access them but can still manage them as usual. First, FlexServe introduces a Recallable Resource Isolation mechanism to construct Recallable Secure Memory (Flex-Mem) and a Recallable Secure NPU (Flex-NPU). They can only be accessed by the secure world, but can be efficiently allocated and reclaimed by the normal-world OS. Based on them, FlexServe further introduces a FlexServe Framework to run secure LLM inference in the secure world. It works together with the normal-world OS to perform cooperative secure memory management. We implement a prototype of FlexServe and compare it with two TrustZone-based strawman designs. The results show that FlexServe achieves average TTFT speedups of 10.05X over the strawman and 2.44X over an optimized strawman.

</details>


### [230] [OptChain: Achieving Optimal Throughput of Permissionless Blockchains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23405)
*Chunjiang Che,Songze Li,Xuechao Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: OptChain 是一个无许可区块链 SMR 协议，通过优化垂直和水平可扩展性实现理论最优吞吐量，并首次逼近了固定错误概率下 SMR 协议的吞吐量理论上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无许可区块链 SMR 协议的吞吐量远未达到物理极限，缺乏理论上界及逼近该上界的设计方案。

Method: 提出 Shardis（垂直优化：最大化分片内吞吐量至物理极限）和 diffusion mining（水平优化：每个分片至少一个诚实节点即可保证安全，支持最大分片数）。

Result: 形式化安全与效率分析表明 OptChain 逼近理论上界；AWS EC2 多区域部署原型实验证明其优于现有协议，接近理论最优吞吐量。

Conclusion: OptChain 首次逼近 SMR 协议吞吐量理论上界，同时保持稳健的安全性。

Abstract: We introduce \textit{OptChain}, a permissionless blockchain state machine replication (SMR) protocol that achieves optimal throughput. We first establish a theoretical upper bound on the throughput of any SMR protocol under a fixed error probability, and OptChain is the first protocol to approach this limit. Conceptually, OptChain is a sharding protocol that optimizes both vertical and horizontal scalability. Vertically, we introduce \textit{Shardis}, a novel permissionless verifiable information dispersal mechanism that maximizes intra-shard throughput to its physical limit, determined by the fastest node's bandwidth within each shard. Horizontally, we propose \textit{diffusion mining}, which ensures security as long as each shard includes at least one honest node, thereby allowing for the maximum number of shards. We provide a formal security and efficiency analysis, demonstrating that OptChain approaches the established upper bound while maintaining robust security. Finally, we implement a full prototype of OptChain and deploy it on AWS EC2 nodes across various regions. Experimental results indicate that OptChain outperforms state-of-the-art permissionless protocols and closely approaches the theoretical optimal throughput.

</details>


### [231] [Detecting Malicious Agent Skills in the Wild using Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23416)
*Bacem Etteib,Daniele Lunghi,Tégawendé F. Bissyandé*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Locate-and-Judge，一种针对LLM agent技能市场新型攻击面的两阶段检测器。它通过轻量级定位器识别高注意力指令片段，再用判断器详细审查，相比直接LLM扫描降低一个数量级成本，成功检测出数十个真实恶意技能，包括被现有工具漏检的伪装技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent中第三方技能（自然语言指令包）以用户权限执行，单个恶意技能即可造成数据泄露、agent劫持或供应链持久化。现有prompt注入防御依赖可信指令与不可信数据的边界，但技能本身是指令集合，注入命令混入合法指令中继承其权威，因此传统防御无效，亟需针对这一新攻击面的检测方案。

Method: 提出Locate-and-Judge两阶段检测器：（1）轻量级定位器（Locator）根据指令跟随注意力分数对技能结构跨度评分，保留Top-K高注意力跨度；（2）判断器（Judge）对保留的跨度进行详细审查。通过将昂贵的判断聚焦于少数高注意力跨度，实现对整体市场而非样本的审计。

Result: 相比直接LLM扫描，成本降低一个数量级，可扩展性显著提升，召回率仅小幅下降；在同等成本下全面优于关键词和正则基线。在真实市场部署中，以高精度检测出数十个真实恶意技能，多数经人工确认，包括伪装成良性功能的技能，以及SkillSpector和Cisco Skill Scanner漏检的技能。

Conclusion: Locate-and-Judge以极低成本实现市场规模的恶意技能检测，弥补了现有方法在LLM agent技能供应链安全领域的空白，并发布了标注数据集。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly load skills, file-based packages of natural-language instructions written by third parties and distributed through marketplaces, that execute with the user's privileges. A single malicious skill can exfiltrate data, hijack the agent, or persist as a supply-chain foothold, which turns the skill marketplace into a new attack surface for agentic systems. Prompt-injection defenses do not carry over to this setting. They rely on a boundary between trusted instructions and untrusted data, whereas a skill is itself a body of instructions, so an injected command sits among many legitimate ones and inherits their authority. We present Locate-and-Judge, a two-stage detector designed for this regime. A lightweight locator scores the structural spans of a skill by the instruction-following attention each span draws and retains only the top-K. A judge then examines the retained spans in detail. Concentrating the costly judgment on a few high-attention spans lets the detector audit an entire marketplace instead of a sample. Compared to direct LLM-based scanning, this approach offers an order-of-magnitude cost reduction, dramatically increasing its scalability at a small cost to recall, and it dominates keyword and regex baselines at comparable expense. Deployed at marketplace scale and at negligible cost, Locate-and-Judge flags skills with high precision, the majority of which we manually confirmed as malicious, surfacing dozens of live malicious skills, including several disguised as benign functionality and many that SkillSpector and Cisco Skill Scanner fail to detect. We release the resulting labeled dataset.

</details>


### [232] [A Watermark for Vision-Language-Action and World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23574)
*Yule Liu,Shuai Liu,Jiaheng Wei,Xinlei He*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于密钥的潜在来源验证方法，通过向VLA/WAM模型的高斯噪声种子中嵌入指纹来保护机器人策略模型的IP，在几乎不影响任务性能的情况下实现可靠的模型归属检测，并具有抗攻击鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作(VLA)模型和世界-动作模型(WAM)作为通用机器人控制的核心生成模型，训练过程需要专有数据和大量算力，部署后的模型本身成为高价值知识产权。然而这些模型越来越多地以黑盒服务形式部署，合作伙伴通过接口使用策略而所有者保留模型权重私有，因此需要一种方法保护模型IP不被盗用或窃取。

Method: 提出keyed latent-provenance verification方法：在注入阶段，所有者将模型生成前采样的高斯噪声种子替换为与普通噪声同分布的密钥化种子，使指纹化动作在统计上与普通运行无异；在验证阶段，所有者在授权访问下运行嫌疑模型，记录机器人执行的动作通道，通过基于梯度的最大后验(MAP)优化从部分且可能后处理的输出视图中恢复种子，检测其是否包含密钥，聚合多个rollout的分数做出最终归属判定。

Result: 在两个代表性模型和两个机器人套件上的实验表明：指纹可以被可靠检测，对任务性能影响极小；在输出端移除攻击和权重级编辑下仍保持可检测性；能够识别嫌疑模型携带的是哪个密钥。

Conclusion: 该方法通过在高斯噪声种子中嵌入密钥指纹，实现了对黑盒部署的VLA/WAM模型的有效IP保护，兼具隐蔽性、可验证性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models and world-action models (WAM) are the generative models now driving general-purpose robot control, turning raw camera input directly into motor commands. They are increasingly deployed as black-box services, where a partner runs the policy through an interface while the owner keeps the weights private. Training such a model takes proprietary data and heavy computational power, making the deployed model itself a valuable intellectual property.
  To address this, we propose the \emph{keyed latent-provenance verification} method, which fingerprints the policy through the seed of the Gaussian noise vector that the models draw before generation. At the injection stage, the owner swaps this seed for a keyed one with the same distribution as ordinary noise, so the fingerprinted actions are statistically identical to those of an ordinary run and an adversary watching the output finds no signal to detect or remove. At the verification stage, the owner runs the suspect model under authorized access and records the action channels the robot executes, a partial and possibly post-processed view of the policy's output. From this view, the verifier recovers the seed by gradient-based maximum a posteriori (MAP) optimization, tests it for the secret key to score each rollout, and aggregates these scores into a single decision on whether the suspect model belongs to the owner.
  We evaluate the method on two representative models across two robot suites. The experiments cover detection of the fingerprint, identification of which of several keys a suspect carries, robustness to a range of attacks, and an analysis of why the design works. Across both models, the fingerprint can be detected reliably with little change to task performance, and it remains detectable under output-side removal attacks and weight-level edits.

</details>


### [233] [Ghost Vectors: Soft-Deleted Embeddings Remain Reconstructible in HNSW Vector Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18497)
*Chandranil Chakraborttii,Jackeline García Alvarado,Sitora Abdulofizova,Shivanshu Dwivedi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了现代RAG系统中HNSW向量数据库的软删除漏洞：被标记删除的向量嵌入仍可绕过API直接从原始索引文件中物理恢复。通过在多个真实数据集（维基百科BLP、NIH Synthea、医学图像、人脸嵌入）上的实验，证实了敏感信息（人名、地理位置、年龄、性别、组织分类、人脸身份）可被高精度恢复。作者提出"Epoch密钥轮换"方案，通过加密向量并在删除时丢弃密钥，将PII恢复率降至0%，同时生成ECDSA签名加密证明作为可审计的删除记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统中的数据删除仅执行软删除（标记删除而不物理擦除嵌入），这违反了GDPR第17条和HIPAA等数据保护法规的删除与留存要求。需要验证该漏洞的实际风险并提出合规的解决方案。

Method: 1) 对三个HNSW实现进行存储层直接访问分析，验证软删除向量的物理可恢复性；2) 使用Vec2Text反转模型（无需领域特定微调）从向量恢复原始文本；3) 在多种数据类型（文本、结构化数据、图像嵌入）和数据集上评估恢复精度；4) 提出Epoch密钥轮换方案：对向量进行加密，删除时丢弃密钥，并生成ECDSA签名删除证明。

Result: 维基百科BLP数据集：25.5%精确人名恢复、46.4%地理位置恢复（ROUGE-L 0.185）；NIH Synthea数据集：患者年龄和性别标记100%恢复（ROUGE-L 0.290）；组织病理学图像嵌入：100%组织分类恢复（p=1.02e-07）；人脸嵌入：top-1身份恢复达99%（p<0.01）。Epoch密钥轮换使PII恢复率降至0%，处理500个向量仅需2.5ms（约0.005ms/条）。

Conclusion: 软删除在HNSW向量数据库中无法保证数据真正不可恢复，构成合规风险。Epoch密钥轮换方案能有效解决该问题，兼具安全性（零恢复率）、高性能（毫秒级）和可审计性（加密签名证明）。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) allows large language models to access external and private corpora for factual, domain-specific responses. Modern RAG pipelines use hierarchical navigable small world (HNSW) vector databases for efficient similarity search. When a user requests data deletion, the systems typically only mark the record as deleted, leaving the embedding on disk physically unchanged. This soft-delete operation raises compliance concerns under data-erasure and retention requirements such as GDPR Article 17 and HIPAA. Analysis on three HNSW implementations confirms that deleted vectors remain physically recoverable by accessing the raw index files at the storage layer, bypassing API access. Using the Vec2Text inversion model without domain-specific fine-tuning, we show this vulnerability on multiple real-world datasets and data modalities. On Wikipedia biographical living persons dataset (BLP), we successfully recover 25.5% of exact person names and 46.4% of geographic locations (ROUGE-L 0.185). Recovery reaches 100% for both patient age and gender markers (ROUGE-L 0.290) on highly structured, sensitive data (NIH Synthea dataset). On soft-deleted image embeddings, we show 100% tissue classification on histopathology patches (p=1.02e-07) and top-1 identity recovery reaches 99% on facial embeddings (p<0.01). This work introduces Epoch Key Rotation, which encrypts vectors and discards the key upon deletion. Epoch key rotation reduces observed PII recovery to 0% and completes in 2.5 ms for 500 deleted vectors (approximately 0.005 ms/record). Additionally, it generates an ECDSA-signed cryptographic proof as an auditable record of the deletion event.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [234] [On the Identifiability of User Adaptation in Co-Adaptive Neural Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20569)
*Philip Waggoner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析了共适应人机系统中的可辨识性问题，证明闭环编码器估计无法唯一识别用户适应性，而反映的是联合系统的特性，并讨论了行为适应性解读的意义及辨识条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共适应人机系统中，用户与机器相互适应，导致闭环系统下的观测数据无法区分用户适应性与机器适应性的各自贡献，从而引发可辨识性问题。理解这一问题对正确解读行为适应实验至关重要。

Method: 通过理论分析，研究闭环编码器估计的数学性质，展示其无法唯一识别用户适应性，而是反映人机联合系统特性的机制，并提出确保可辨识性的条件。

Result: 证明闭环条件下编码器估计不能唯一确定用户适应，而是表征联合系统行为；揭示了现有行为适应解读中可能存在的混淆问题。

Conclusion: 提出了在共适应人机系统中实现可辨识性的必要条件，为未来实验设计和数据分析提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We analyze identifiability in co-adaptive human-machine systems. We show that closed-loop encoder estimates do not uniquely identify user adaptation, but instead reflect properties of the joint system. We discuss implications for interpreting behavioral adaptation and propose conditions for identification.

</details>


### [235] [Beyond Fixed Budgets: Characterizing the Inelasticity and Limitations of Tree-of-Thought Reasoning Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20599)
*Atkia Mahila,Avinash Maurya,M. Mustafa Rafique,Bogdan Nicolae*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了两种代表性树思维搜索方法（DPTS和SSDP）在不同计算预算、模型规模和问题难度下的表现，发现DPTS存在低预算冷启动瓶颈，SSDP则受限于前沿耗尽问题，表明固定探索或剪枝策略无法适应计算连续体，需要自适应搜索策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tree of Thought (ToT)搜索在提升大语言模型推理能力方面展现出潜力，但不同搜索策略在不同计算预算、模型规模和问题难度下的行为缺乏系统研究，这阻碍了实际部署中的资源优化配置。

Method: 选取两种代表性ToT方法（DPTS：基于蒙特卡洛树搜索；SSDP：基于语义去重），在两种数学推理基准（Math500和GSM8K）、两种模型规模（Llama-3B和Llama-8B）以及四种token预算（3k-10k）下进行系统性对比评估。

Result: DPTS在低预算下存在冷启动瓶颈，需充分探索后才能获得可靠价值估计，虽在高预算下表现良好的缩放行为，但不适用于资源受限场景；SSDP虽能高效生成候选解，但其激进节点合并策略永久丢弃未探索路径，导致前沿耗尽，即使剩余预算充足也无法进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: 两种方法表现出相反方向的局限性，表明固定探索策略或固定剪枝策略均无法适用于整个计算预算连续体；科学推理智能体的有效搜索需要能根据搜索进度和可用资源自适应调整行为的策略。

Abstract: Tree of Thought (ToT) search has become a promising direction for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models, but deploying these methods in practice raises a question that has received little systematic attention: how do different search strategies behave under varying compute budgets, model sizes, and problem difficulties? In this work, we evaluate two representative ToT methods; DPTS, a Monte Carlo tree search based approach, and SSDP, a semantic deduplication based approach, across two mathematical reasoning benchmarks (Math500 and GSM8K), two model scales (Llama-3B and Llama-8B), and four token budgets (3k--10k). Our analysis reveals that the two methods exhibit limitations that pull in opposite directions. DPTS suffers from a cold-start bottleneck at low budgets: it requires sufficient exploration before its value estimates become reliable, making it a poor fit for resource-constrained settings despite strong scaling behavior at higher budgets. SSDP, on the other hand, reaches candidate solutions efficiently but is prone to frontier depletion; its aggressive node merging permanently discards unexplored paths, leaving it unable to improve regardless of how much budget remains. Together, these findings suggest that neither a fixed exploration strategy nor a fixed pruning strategy is sufficient across compute continuum. We argue that effective search for scientific reasoning agents requires strategies that can adapt their behavior based on search progress and available resources.

</details>


### [236] [The New Associationism: Lessons from Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20600)
*Daniel Rothschild*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文认为现代AI的成功支持了一种温和的联想主义学习观：监督学习（基于评价性反馈的学习）是当代AI系统的核心机制，从大语言模型到游戏智能体皆然，这证实了联想主义关于统一、渐进、错误驱动的学习机制跨领域运作的理念，但也指出深度学习依赖的计算架构远超经典联想主义的设想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨现代AI的成功对人类学习机制的理论启示，反驳联想主义机制不足以解释人类认知能力的传统观点。

Method: 通过对当代AI系统（大语言模型、游戏智能体等）的分析，论证监督学习作为核心学习机制的统一性，并比较其与经典联想主义的异同。

Result: 监督学习是多种AI系统的共同基础，差异仅在于生成反馈信号的工作量不同；这支持了联想主义的核心主张，但深度学习的计算架构超出了经典联想主义的范畴。

Conclusion: 现代AI的成功为温和的联想主义提供了经验支持，但学习机制需在更复杂的计算架构中理解，监督学习是其中的一个组成部分而非全部。

Abstract: What can the success of modern AI tell us about how humans learn? This paper argues that taking AI seriously as a model of human learning supports a modest but genuine associationism. The central finding is that supervised learning -- learning driven by evaluative feedback -- underlies a surprisingly wide range of contemporary AI systems, from large language models to game-playing agents, differing primarily in how much work is required to generate the relevant feedback signal. This vindicates associationist ideals of a uniform, gradual, error-driven learning mechanism operating across domains, and defuses the once-influential argument that associationist mechanisms are too limited to account for human cognitive capacities. At the same time, the successes of deep learning depend on computational architectures that go well beyond anything classical associationists envisaged, and supervised learning operates within these as one component rather than a complete account of learning.

</details>


### [237] [Specifying AI-SDLC Processes: A Protocol Language for Human-Agent Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20615)
*Ylli Prifti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于AI-SDLC流程的领域特定语言，通过形式化语法、操作语义和执行不变量来明确人机协作中的责任边界、审批关卡和治理约束，并论证了结构性强制比行为性合规更能有效控制系统故障率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI代理参与软件开发全生命周期时，缺乏专门的语言来表达人机之间的责任边界、审批关卡和治理约束。现有方法要么将流程编码在代理提示中（易偏移），要么针对相邻领域（工作流管理、业务流程），或仅解决碎片化问题（访问控制、审批关卡），无法系统性地满足AI-SDLC协作需求。

Method: 提出一种领域特定语言，将AI-SDLC流程形式化为协议，包含形式语法、良构条件、操作语义和执行不变量。语言区分策略（声明意图）与机制（结构性强制），通过验证令牌和能力边界等原语约束流程非确定性。定义了2+N团队模式（两个人工控制角色+N个专业代理成员）以形式化经典职责分离原则。

Result: 故障率分析表明：结构性强制将系统故障率限制为代理与验证器故障率的加权乘积，而行为性合规则导致累积或接近饱和的故障增长。2+N团队模式形式化了AI-SDLC中的职责分离。Kleene闭包编排循环和自反协议合规验证作为设计属性自然涌现。已有工作原理解实现，实证评估为未来工作。

Conclusion: 本文贡献在于对多代理框架（MetaGPT）、工作流规范（FlowAgent、BPMN扩展）和基于能力的安全（SAGA）的特定整合创新，而非任何单一原语。该语言为AI-SDLC中的人机协作治理提供了形式化基础，有望提升流程的可验证性和可靠性。

Abstract: AI agents now participate as first-class team members across the software development lifecycle, yet no specification language exists for expressing the human-agent responsibility boundaries, approval gates, and governance constraints this collaboration requires. Existing approaches encode process in agent prompts (subject to drift), target adjacent domains (workflow management, business processes), or address only fragments (access control, approval gates).
  We propose a domain-specific language for specifying AI-SDLC processes as protocols, with formal syntax, well-formedness conditions, operational semantics, and enforcement invariants. The language distinguishes policy (declared intent) from mechanism (structural enforcement), enabling implementations to bound process non-determinism through primitives such as validation tokens and capability boundaries.
  Three results follow. A failure rate analysis shows that structural enforcement bounds system failure rates at a weighted product of agent and validator rates, while behavioral compliance permits cumulative or near-saturating growth. The 2+N team pattern (two human-in-control roles plus N specialized agent members) formalizes classical Separation of Duties for AI-SDLC. Kleene closure of orchestration loops and reflexive protocol-adherence validation emerge as design properties rather than special-case constructs.
  We position the contribution against multi-agent frameworks (MetaGPT), workflow specification (FlowAgent, BPMN extensions), and capability-based security (SAGA): the novelty lies in the specific integration, not any single primitive. A working implementation demonstrates feasibility; empirical evaluation is future work.

</details>


### [238] [PEAR: Permutation-Equivariant Adaptive Routing Multi-Agent Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20621)
*Yang Feng,Ziwei Xu,Xia Hu,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PEAR方法，通过排列等变自适应路由机制动态重组多智能体辩论中的角色分配和稀疏拓扑结构，解决了固定拓扑导致的位置偏见和角色敏感性问题，在多个推理基准和LLM骨干网络上显著提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于多智能体辩论的方法使用固定拓扑结构，导致持续性的位置偏见、不可靠智能体被放大以及对角色分配高度敏感等问题，缺乏动态调整机制。

Method: 提出排列等变自适应路由多智能体辩论协议（PEAR），在连续辩论轮次中动态重新配置通信角色和稀疏拓扑，根据智能体状态变化策略性地切换智能体到角色的分配，防止任何智能体永久占据特权网络位置。

Result: 在四个推理基准和六个不同LLM骨干网络上，PEAR的平均准确率显著优于最强的辩论基线方法。

Conclusion: PEAR作为一种等变稀疏路由器，能够在保持智能体重标号下准确性的同时降低路由复杂度并提升泛化能力，是一种更鲁棒的多智能体辩论方法。

Abstract: Multi-agent debate improves the reliability of large language models (LLMs) through iterative peer critiques. However, fixed topologies often introduce persistent positional biases, amplify unreliable agents, and cause high sensitivity to role assignments. We introduce \textit{Permutation-Equivariant Adaptive Routing Multi-Agent Debate (PEAR)}, an inference-time protocol that dynamically reconfigures communication roles and sparse topologies across consecutive debate rounds. By strategically switching agent-to-role assignments based on evolving agent states, PEAR prevents any agent from permanently occupying a privileged network position or distributes influence more evenly across the debate. We theoretically characterize PEAR as an equivariant sparse router: it preserves accuracy under agent relabeling while reducing routing complexity and improving generalization. Comprehensive empirical evaluations across four reasoning benchmarks and six diverse LLM backbones demonstrate PEAR significantly improves average accuracy over the strongest debate baselines. The code is at https://github.com/EVIEHub/PEAR.

</details>


### [239] [Darwin Mobile Agent: A Roadmap for Self-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20622)
*Daniel Beechey,Derek Yuen,Jianheng Liu,Dezhao Luo,Tiantian He,Weilin Luo,Jun Wang,Kun Shao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出通过系统性地移除人类先验知识，让智能体在巨大的移动GUI世界中自主强化学习，并介绍了Darwin Mobile Agent基础设施作为实现这一目标的基础平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创造能够在开放环境中具备通用、自适应行为的智能体是AI的终极目标。受"苦涩教训"启发，作者认为最有效的路径是移除人类先验，让智能通过与远超自身复杂度的"大世界"交互来自然涌现智能。

Method: 提出移动GUI作为"大世界"的实用代理，并构建Darwin Mobile Agent开源框架。该框架采用异步智能体-环境循环，在并行云手机实例上进行数据收集，解决了真实移动交互中的数据收集瓶颈问题。

Result: 验证了Darwin基础设施能够提供GUI领域策略优化所需的稳定性和可扩展性，为自主进化智能体的第一阶段（策略优化）奠定了基础。

Conclusion: 本文为构建真正自主、自进化的GUI智能体奠定了实践和理论基础，并提出了一个从任务课程、结果验证和记忆管理三个基本支柱系统性地移除人类先验的概念路线图。

Abstract: The goal of artificial intelligence is to create agents capable of general, adaptive behaviour in open-ended environments. Guided by the "Bitter Lesson", we argue that the most effective path toward this goal is to systematically remove human priors and allow intelligence to naturally emerge through interaction with a "Big World" that is orders of magnitude more complex than the agent itself. We propose the mobile Graphical User Interface (GUI) as a practical proxy for such a world and introduce Darwin Mobile Agent, an open-source infrastructure designed as a foundation for autonomous reinforcement learning in this domain. This framework addresses the data-collection bottleneck in real-world mobile interactions by using an asynchronous agent-environment loop across parallel cloud-phone instances. We further propose a conceptual roadmap to systematically remove human priors from three fundamental pillars of a self-evolving agent: task curricula, outcome verification, and memory management. We validate that the Darwin infrastructure provides the stability and scalability required for the first stage of this roadmap: policy optimisation in the GUI domain. This work establishes the practical and theoretical foundation necessary to move toward truly autonomous, self-evolving GUI agents.

</details>


### [240] [Path-dependent program induction under resource constraints explains human sequence learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20623)
*Hanqi Zhou,David G. Nagy,Peter Dayan,Charley M. Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文整合率-失真理论与程序归纳，提出层级适应文法（HAG）来模拟人类在有限认知资源下从序列经验中构建抽象、可复用知识的过程。HAG在模拟中取得更优的率-失真权衡和泛化表现，并在在线旋律序列学习实验中优于所有替代模型，揭示了经验顺序如何塑造学习者的抽象构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究人类如何在有限认知资源下，从序列性经验中构建抽象的、可复用的知识结构。

Method: 将率-失真理论与程序归纳相结合，提出层级适应文法（HAG），在局部（任务内）和全局（任务间）两个层次上设置不同的知识库，并受记忆和计算约束共同调控。

Result: HAG在模拟任务中实现了更优的率-失真权衡和更强的泛化能力；在在线旋律序列学习实验中，参与者的回忆误差反映了系统性的简化，反应时在推断的程序边界处增加；逐试次拟合表明层级库最能解释个体差异。

Conclusion: 结构化学习可以理解为有界程序归纳，其中经验顺序塑造了学习者未来构建的抽象结构。

Abstract: How do people build abstract, reusable knowledge from sequential experience under bounded cognitive resources? To answer this question, we integrate rate-distortion theory with recent advances in program induction to describe how prior knowledge shapes which future structures are cheap to encode and easy to discover. We formalize this in a hierarchical Adaptor Grammar (HAG) with distinct local (within-task) and global (across-task) libraries, governed jointly by constraints on memory and computation. In simulations, HAG achieves better rate-distortion trade-offs and stronger generalization than fixed grammars or shallow chunking methods. In an online melodic sequence-learning experiment, participants' recall errors reflected systematic simplifications and reaction times increased at inferred program boundaries. Trial-by-trial fits further showed that hierarchical libraries best explained individual differences in both recall and out-of-sample continuation choices, outperforming all alternative models. These findings cast structured learning as bounded program induction in which the order of experience shapes future abstractions a learner builds.

</details>


### [241] [In LLM Reasoning, there is Irrationality on top of Value Misalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20624)
*Kejiang Qian,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"理性价值风险"概念，指LLM即使经过对齐训练，在推理时仍可能无法最大化对齐价值的现象。通过数学形式化将风险分解为有限候选、有限提示和不完美验证器三个来源，并在多个模型和基准上验证了该风险的普遍性、对齐无法消除性、对推理策略的敏感性，以及长推理收益递减的规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM对齐方法（如RLHF）主要关注后训练阶段的价值对齐，但即使模型在对齐训练中表现良好，在实际推理过程中仍可能无法最大化对齐价值。作者试图形式化和量化这一"对齐-推理"之间的差距。

Method: 提出理性价值风险（Rational Value Risk）的数学形式化定义：模型部署的推理策略与理性策略（在最陡方向上最大化期望效用的响应）之间的效用差异。进一步将风险估计误差分解为三个来源：有限候选、有限提示和不完美验证器。在多个模型家族（Llama-3.1、Qwen-2.5、Tülu-3 7B-72B、GPT-5.2、GPT-5.5、DeepSeek-V4）和多个基准（UltraFeedback、AlpacaEval、GSM8K、MATH、HumanEval、MathArena）上进行实验验证。

Result: (1) 理性价值风险普遍存在；(2) 价值对齐可以降低但无法消除该风险；(3) 该风险对推理时的推理策略高度敏感；(4) 更长的推理能改善理性但存在收益递减效应。

Conclusion: LLM在推理阶段存在固有的理性价值风险，即使经过充分对齐训练也无法完全消除。这提示未来的研究需要在推理阶段进一步优化，而不仅仅关注训练阶段的对齐。此外，推理策略的选择和推理长度的控制是提升模型理性行为的关键因素。

Abstract: Significant progress has been made in aligning LLMs with target value functions. We argue that, even when an LLM has been well aligned in (post-)training, it may still fail to maximise the aligned value in reasoning. We mathematically formalise this gap as rational value risk: the utility discrepancy between a model's deployed reasoning strategy and its rational counterpart, which is defined to be the responses that maximise expected utility in the steepest direction. The estimation error of rational value risk is further decomposed into three components from finite candidates, finite prompts, and imperfect verifiers. Extensive experiments are conducted, covering models Llama-3.1, Qwen-2.5, T{\"}ulu-3 families (7B-72B), GPT-5.2, GPT-5.5, and DeepSeek-V4, and benchmarks UltraFeedback, AlpacaEval, GSM8K, MATH, HumanEval, and MathArena. The results validate that (1) rational value risk is widespread; (2) value alignment can reduce, but cannot eliminate, it; (3) the risk is highly sensitive to inference-time reasoning strategy; and (4) longer reasoning improves rationality with diminishing returns. The code is at https://github.com/EVIEHub/LLM-Rationality.

</details>


### [242] [AlphaMemo: Structured Search-Process Memory for Self-Evolving Alpha Mining Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20625)
*Hang Yu,Zifan Zheng,Jeff Z. Pan,Tongliang Liu,Zhiyong Wang,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AlphaMemo提出了一种具有结构化搜索-过程记忆的自进化因子挖掘智能体，通过记录AST差异中的编辑模式、置信门控残差记忆和非对称否决控制，解决了LLM因子挖掘中的组合搜索空间大、反馈噪声非平稳、冗余发现和过拟合等问题，在CSI 500和S&P 500上取得了更优的样本外表现和固定预算发现效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM因子挖掘智能体面临组合搜索空间过大、非平稳噪声反馈、冗余发现以及因简单复用过去成功经验导致过拟合风险等挑战，亟需一种更有效的记忆机制来缓解这些问题。

Method: AlphaMemo采用结构化搜索-过程记忆，不直接记忆最终因子或完整轨迹，而是记录特定父因子上下文中编辑模式的成功/失败经验。具体包括：从AST差异中提取编辑模式、置信门控残差记忆（叠加在搜索账本先验之上）、以及通过非对称否决控制抑制高置信失败模式。

Result: 在CSI 500和S&P 500上的实验表明，AlphaMemo在样本外表现和固定预算发现效率上均优于基线方法。消融实验验证了残差学习、置信门控、AST差异模式和否决记忆各模块的有效性。

Conclusion: AlphaMemo通过结构化搜索-过程记忆机制有效提升了LLM因子挖掘智能体的泛化能力和搜索效率，为金融量化因子发现的自动化提供了新范式。

Abstract: LLM agents are promising for alpha mining via combining financial priors, symbolic reasoning, executable factor generation, and feedback-driven refinement. Yet, they face a combinatorial search space, noisy non-stationary feedback, redundant discoveries, and overfitting risks from naively reusing past successes. To address these challenges, we propose AlphaMemo, a self-evolving alpha mining agent with Structured Search-Process Memory. Rather than memorizing only final factors or full trajectories, AlphaMemo records reusable evidence about which edit motifs work or fail under specific parent-factor contexts. It extracts motifs from Abstract Syntax Tree (AST) differences, applies confidence-gated residual memory on top of a search-ledger prior, and uses asymmetric veto control to suppress high-confidence failure patterns. Experiments on CSI 500 and S\&P 500 show improved out-of-sample performance and fixed-budget discovery efficiency, with ablations validating the roles of residual learning, confidence gating, AST-diff motifs, and veto memory. Code is at https://github.com/jarrettyu/AlphaMemo.

</details>


### [243] [Latent Goal Prediction from Language for Model-Based Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20627)
*Samuel Barbeau,Simon Roy,Giovanni Beltrame,Christian Desrosiers,Nicolas Thome*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LAGO通过从语言指令预测中间潜在子目标序列，并结合动作条件 rollout，在统一潜在空间中进行规划，解决了世界模型中的复合预测误差和优化目标定义难题，实现了稳健的长期规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型规划受困于复合预测误差和优化目标难以定义的问题：视觉目标提供精确局部梯度但缺乏远程引导，语言灵活但受限于跨模态对齐噪声或依赖不适合高采样规划的大生成模型。

Method: 提出Latent Goal Prediction from Language (LAGO)框架，从语言指令预测中间潜在子目标序列，同时进行动作条件 rollout（均在相同潜在空间）；在线更新子目标，规划中使用软最小轨迹代价（soft minimum trajectory cost）动态分解指令为局部可处理的潜在子目标。

Result: 在多个环境和规划跨度上，LAGO避免了先前方法的性能急剧退化，实现了稳健且精确的长期规划。

Conclusion: LAGO弥合了视觉目标的精确性与文本引导控制的灵活性之间的鸿沟，纯从语言实现鲁棒的长期规划。

Abstract: Planning with world models is bottlenecked by compounding prediction errors and the difficulty of defining optimizable goals. Visual targets provide precise local gradients but poor distant guidance, while language is flexible yet limited by noisy cross-modal alignment or dependence on large generative models unsuited for the high-sampling nature of model-based planning.
