Result: 发现身份真空和安全盆地两种条件机制；等边探针基线显示身份规范创造55种独特响应模式（基线模型仅1种）；漂移实验发现幅度下降是重复填充伪影，多样填充在150K token内无变形。

Conclusion: 框架的理论基础（扰动理论和选择规则）支持同调诊断潜力（各向异性收缩和结构坍塌检测），但经验证实仍待完成。

Abstract: AI agents in long-context applications drift from their specified identity. Current methods detect this only after qualitative degradation is visible. We present a geometric framework for measuring identity structure using $\sqrt{\mathrm{JSD}}$ metric spaces and magnitude homology from enriched category theory, where identity is non-geodesic structure and drift is its relaxation toward the geodesic.
  Validated on a persistent AI agent, the framework's strongest empirical finding is a two-mechanism conditioning structure: cross-condition distances reveal an identity-vacuum cluster where the identity specification fills a behavioral void, and a safety-basin cluster where it displaces from post-training attractors. An equilateral probe baseline confirms that the identity specification creates measurable behavioral richness (55 unique response patterns vs. 1 for the base model) at maximum probe separation. A first-order perturbation theory for equilateral configurations predicts magnitude changes from perimeter changes alone, with shape perturbations first-order cancelled by the $S_n$ symmetry; the formula is self-consistent at the observed perturbation amplitudes.
  A drift experiment measuring magnitude decrease under context pressure was subsequently found to reflect repetitive-padding artifacts rather than genuine context-length drift; diverse padding produces no measurable deformation through 150K tokens. The magnitude homology framework's full diagnostic promise -- detecting anisotropic contraction and structural collapse via homological simplification -- is architecturally grounded in the perturbation theory and selection rules but remains empirically unconfirmed.

</details>


### [316] [ForEx: A Formal Verification Framework for Explainable Reasoning in Logical Fallacy Detection and Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21867)
*Pei-Cing Huang,Chienyu Liu,Chan Hsu,Ci-Siang Chen,Pei-Ju Lee,Yihuang Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ForEx框架，将LLM生成的逻辑谬误检测解释翻译成Lean4形式语言进行验证，发现90%以上可形式化证明的推理与人工标注一致性仅约20%，揭示了形式可推导性与标签一致性之间的系统性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM逻辑谬误检测评估仅关注预测标签正确性，未验证模型提供的推理过程是否真正支持其预测结论。

Method: 提出ForEx框架，将LLM生成的解释翻译为Lean4形式语言，验证翻译后的推理链是否能在编码前提条件下被推导；引入LLM论证验证矩阵，区分标签一致性与形式验证状态。

Result: 在LOGIC-Climate数据集上，超过90%的LLM输出可翻译为通过形式验证的推理链，但与人工标注的一致性仅约20%。

Conclusion: 形式可推导性与标签一致性之间存在系统性差距，这是基于预测的评估指标无法发现的；ForEx将LLM评估从标签正确性推进到可机器检查的形式化推理链分析。

Abstract: Current evaluations of Large Language Models (LLMs) on logical fallacy detection focus on predicted labels, but do not establish whether those labels are supported by the reasoning the models provide. We propose ForEx (Formal Verification for Explainable Reasoning), a framework that translates LLM-generated explanations into Lean4 and verifies whether the translated rationale is derivable under encoded premises, not the logical validity of the original natural language argument. To distinguish prediction outcomes from the formal status of the supporting reasoning, we introduce the LLM Argument Verification Matrix, which separates label consistency from formal verification status. Experiments on LOGIC-Climate show that over 90% of LLM outputs can be translated into formal reasoning chains that pass verification, while agreement with human annotations remains around 20%. These results expose a systematic gap between formal derivability and label agreement, a distinction invisible to prediction-based metrics. ForEx moves LLM evaluation beyond label correctness toward machine-checkable analysis of formalized reasoning chains.

</details>


### [317] [Learning the ARTS of Search for Automated Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21891)
*Gurusha Juneja,Arnav Kumar Jain,Deepak Nathani,William Yang Wang,Xin Eric Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ARTS框架，利用推理语言模型在假设-实验空间中导航搜索，通过测试时训练将搜索树知识注入模型权重，解决MCTS类方法中假设价值与执行质量混淆以及搜索历史超出上下文窗口的问题。在22个任务中，ARTS超越领先算法15.3%以上，Qwen3-4B模型经测试时训练后性能可匹敌闭源前沿模型且推理成本降低5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学发现方法（如MCTS）将假设的价值与实验执行的质量混为一谈，导致有价值但初步执行的假设被降级；同时搜索过程中累积的历史超出上下文窗口，导致早期信息丢失。

Method: ARTS框架利用推理语言模型作为搜索代理，通过检查历史执行日志诊断失败原因（是假设错误还是实现错误），并据此选择下一步要构建的假设。为解决上下文长度问题，采用测试时训练（test-time training）将搜索树知识注入模型权重。

Result: 在MLGym和MLEBench的22个任务上，ARTS相比领先算法有15.3%以上的相对提升。Qwen3-4B经测试时训练后性能匹敌Gemini-3 Pro和GPT o3-reasoning，推理成本降低5倍。在部分可观测RL任务中，测试时训练的Qwen3-4B科学家超越了使用o3科学家的ARTS，重新发现了启发式方法剪枝掉的人类最优循环记忆解决方案。

Conclusion: ARTS通过推理语言模型和测试时训练有效解决了科学发现搜索中的假设-执行混淆和上下文窗口限制问题，实现了更优的搜索性能和成本效益，并能重新发现被传统启发式方法忽略的经典解决方案。

Abstract: Scientific discovery can be formulated as an iterative search process over the space of hypotheses and experiments. Contemporary methods navigate this space using heuristics such as MCTS. These algorithms conflate the merit of a hypothesis with the quality of its experimental execution. A promising hypothesis with preliminary execution is therefore ranked below a modest hypothesis whose execution is refined. Moreover, prior methods prune the search logs as the search progresses because the accumulated history outgrows the context window. We propose Agentic Reasoning for Tree Search (ARTS), where we deploy a reasoning language model to navigate this space. The model inspects prior execution logs, diagnoses whether earlier failures arose from faulty implementations or bad hypotheses, and selects the hypothesis to build on next. To mitigate challenges with context length, ARTS uses test-time training to instill the knowledge of search tree in the model weights. Across 22 tasks from MLGym and MLEBench, we show that ARTS outperforms leading algorithms, with over 15.3% relative improvement in the normalized score. With test-time training we show that a Qwen3-4B agent can match performance with closed-source frontier models like Gemini-3 Pro and GPT o3-reasoning with upto 5x lower inference cost. We further observe that on partially observable RL tasks, the test-time trained Qwen3-4B scientist surpasses ARTS with the o3 scientist by rediscovering the human-best recurrent-memory solution that heuristic methods prune away.

</details>


### [318] [Holmes: Multimodal Agentic Diagnosis for Mixed-Language Mobile Crashes at Industrial Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21963)
*Jia Li,Wenyuan Ma,Ting Peng,Haibin Zheng,Yuetang Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Holmes是一个多智能体系统，通过综合多模态运行时信号（堆栈跟踪、日志、线程状态）在无需复现的情况下自动进行根因分析。它采用分层检索-探索-推理架构，利用底层构件（寄存器、汇编）弥合开源业务逻辑与闭源系统框架间的语义鸿沟，在7000万行代码库中实现函数级缺陷定位，准确率87.6%，平均排查时间减少98%（约77秒）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超大规模工业应用中的移动崩溃诊断面临巨大挑战：代码量庞大、混合语言环境复杂、无法本地复现故障。传统静态分析难以扩展，现有基于LLM的智能体依赖可复现环境，不适用于事后分析场景。

Method: 提出了分层检索-探索-推理（Retrieve-Explore-Reason）架构：利用底层运行时信号（寄存器、汇编代码等）作为低层级线索，动态缩小搜索空间，在7000万行级别的代码库中精准定位非局部缺陷。

Result: 在微信真实崩溃数据上评估，函数级故障定位准确率达87.6%，平均调查时间降低98%以上（约77秒）。

Conclusion: Holmes展示了将劳动密集型调试工作转化为高效验证工作流的可行性，特别适用于无法复现的事后崩溃分析场景。

Abstract: Diagnosing mobile crashes in ultra-large-scale industrial applications is a formidable challenge due to the sheer volume of code, the complexity of mixed-language environments, and the inability to reproduce failures locally. Traditional static analysis struggles with scalability, while existing LLM-based agents often rely on reproducible environments unavailable in post-mortem scenarios. We present Holmes, a multi-agent system that automates root cause analysis by synthesizing multimodal runtime signals--stack traces, logs, and thread states--to reconstruct failure contexts without reproduction. Holmes introduces a hierarchical Retrieve-Explore-Reason architecture that leverages low-level artifacts (e.g., registers, assembly) to bridge the semantic gap between open-source business logic and closed-source system frameworks. By dynamically compressing the search space using runtime clues, Holmes precisely navigates 70-million-line codebases to identify non-local defects. Evaluated on real-world crashes from WeChat, Holmes achieves 87.6% accuracy in function-level fault localization and reduces average investigation time by over 98% (to ~77 seconds), demonstrating its effectiveness in transforming labor-intensive debugging into an efficient verification workflow.

</details>


### [319] [REBA: A Revealed Belief Automaton Framework for Online Planning in Continuous POMDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21971)
*Xiangwei Chen,Lingling Fang,Andreas Holzinger,Liming Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出REBA框架，通过信息论门在线发现连续信念空间中的可靠锚点，构建有限状态信念自动机，实现基于ω-正则规范的连续POMDP在线规划，性能提升17%-47.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在连续POMDP中处理ω-正则规范时，要么缺乏符号记忆以跟踪长期逻辑进展，要么难以从含噪在线信念中离散化并认证，因此需要一种能在线从连续观测中获取可靠符号状态的方法。

Method: 提出REBA框架：(1) 在线揭示方法，通过信息论门从连续信念空间动态分析并建立信念抽象，发现含噪信念中的可靠锚点；(2) 增量拓扑适应机制，在已认证锚点上构建在线有限信念自动机；(3) 结合ω-正则规范支持形式化奇偶策略综合，引导蒙特卡洛树搜索超越局部视界；(4) 误差分解分析评估离散引导的有效性和可靠性。

Result: 在巡逻和导航场景的实证评估中，REBA匹配或超越所有基线方法，主要指标相较最先进方法提升17.0%至47.4%。

Conclusion: REBA将全局信念空间离散化推进到在线认证揭示事件的新范式，无需预定义离散抽象即可实现连续POMDP的可靠在线规划。

Abstract: Online planning in continuous partially observable Markov decision processes (POMDPs) using $ω$-regular specifications requires handling continuous belief dynamics within the finite symbolic memory in order to track temporal progress. Existing methods based on either direct search in belief space or predefined discrete abstractions suffer from drawbacks, e.g., lack of symbolic memory for long-horizon logical progress or difficult to certify from noisy online beliefs. As such, obtaining reliable symbolic states online from continuous observations remains a challenge. To address this issue, we introduce the Revealed Belief Automaton (REBA), an event-driven framework that advances the research from global belief-space discretization to a fundamental new way of thinking, namely online certification of revelation events. Specifically, we propose an online revelation method that, through information-theoretic gates, can dynamically analyse and establish belief abstraction from the continuous belief space by discovering reliable anchors among noisy beliefs. We then develop an incremental topology adaptation mechanism over the certified anchors to realise the online finite Belief Automaton. By combining with the $ω$-regular specification, REBA is able to support formal parity policy synthesis without a predefined discrete abstraction, which in turn can guide the Monte Carlo Tree Search process to perform online search beyond its local horizon. In addition, we design an error decomposition analysis which can assess the effectiveness and reliability of this discrete guidance for the underlying continuous POMDP. Empirical evaluations in patrolling and navigation scenarios show that REBA matches or exceeds all evaluated baselines, with primary metric gains of +17.0\% to +47.4\% over state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [320] [Human vs Machine Mathematical Difficulty on Project Euler: An Experimental Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21972)
*David Holmes,Johannes Schmitt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了前沿AI系统在Project Euler问题上的努力程度与成功概率如何随人类难度标度变化，发现机器成本与人类时间呈次线性幂律关系（b<1），并验证了指数衰减成功概率模型，最强配置在人类最快基线任务上达到约2.5-4.3小时的50%任务长度视界，SOTA视界约每75天翻倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Timothy Gowers提议启发，检验机器与人类在问题难度上的标度关系，验证此前关于机器随难度增长表现更差的预测是否成立。

Method: 基于MathArena基准的Project Euler数据集（50个问题、26个模型配置、3840次尝试），以人类公开解题时间衡量问题难度；拟合幂律关系 t_machine = a·t_human^b 检验机器成本与人类时间的标度；采用分箱方法拟合指数衰减成功概率模型 p_success = e^{c·t_human}；参照METR方法拟合逻辑斯蒂成功曲线并提取50%任务长度视界h_50。

Result: 25个可拟合模型中有20个的b<1，表明机器成本随难度增长慢于人类，不支持此前相反预测；指数衰减模型在中位分箱R²=0.92上获得中等支持；最强配置的h_50约2.5-4.3小时（基于人类最快基线），SOTA h_50约每75天翻倍。

Conclusion: 机器在困难问题上的标度表现优于此前预期，且AI系统能力随难度增长的趋势稳健，SOTA能力呈快速指数增长。

Abstract: We study how the effort and success probability of frontier AI systems scale with human difficulty on problems from Project Euler, an online platform of computational mathematics problems. Our dataset, from the MathArena benchmark, consists of 3840 attempts across 50 problems and 26 model configurations, with problem difficulty measured by the site's public human solve times. Motivated by a proposal of Timothy Gowers, we test a power-law relation $t_{\text{machine}} = a \cdot t_{\text{human}}^b$ between generated-token cost per successful answer and human time, and find $b < 1$ for 20 of the 25 models with usable fits, including the strongest base models; this operationalization therefore does not support an earlier prediction that machines scale worse than humans with difficulty. We also investigate whether success probability on the tested problems can be modeled by a simple exponential decay $p_{\text{success}} = e^{c t_{\text{human}}}$, predicting a linear relation between $\log p_{\text{success}}$ and $t_{\text{human}}$. Using a binning approach for data aggregation we find moderate empirical support (median bin-level $R^2 = 0.92$ across the 22 best-covered configurations) for this model. Following METR, we also fit logistic success curves and extract 50\% task-length horizons $h_{50}$; the strongest configurations in our 20 April 2026 snapshot reach roughly $2.5$--$4.3$ hours on our fastest-five human baseline, with a log-linear fit through the state-of-the-art frontier giving a descriptive doubling time of about $75$~days for the SOTA $h_{50}$.

</details>


### [321] [IRumAI: Reinforcement Learning for Indian Rummy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21975)
*Vignesh Mohan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: IRumAI是首个基于强化学习的印度拉米纸牌游戏智能体，使用PPO算法、 meld感知编码、死牌驱动奖励塑造和双分支卷积架构，仅靠弱启发式对手训练便能击败包括最强搜索对手在内的所有基线，推理速度比搜索方法快7000倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印度拉米纸牌游戏拥有庞大的玩家基础和复杂的隐藏信息动态，但此前没有任何强化学习研究。现有智能体依赖组合搜索，战术虽强但推理速度慢。

Method: 使用PPO算法，结合meld感知观测编码、死牌驱动奖励塑造和双分支卷积架构。先通过行为克隆进行热启动（基于更强示范数据），然后仅对弱启发式对手进行RL训练。

Result: 能够击败整个基线层次，包括对最强的搜索对手取得53.9%胜率。每步动作仅需0.33毫秒，比最先进的启发式搜索快7000倍以上。消融实验验证了架构选择的有效性，线性探测表明网络能隐式地从公开交互中建模对手的隐藏手牌。

Conclusion: 无需显式搜索，仅通过RL训练即可在印度拉米游戏中达到超越搜索方法的性能，同时大幅提升推理速度，且网络能隐式学习对手手牌模型。

Abstract: Despite its massive player base and complex hidden-information dynamics, Indian Rummy has received no reinforcement learning attention. Existing agents rely on combinatorial search, which is tactically strong but slow at inference. We present IRumAI, the first RL agent for the domain. IRumAI integrates Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), meld-aware observation encoding, deadwood-driven reward shaping, and a dual-branch convolutional architecture. IRumAI is RL-trained solely against weak heuristics, after a one-time behaviour-cloning warm-start on stronger demonstration data. It generalises to defeat the entire baseline hierarchy, including a 53.9% win rate against the strongest search-based opponent unseen during RL training. Bypassing explicit search, IRumAI requires just 0.33 ms per action, which is over 7,000x faster than the state-of-the-art heuristic. Ablations validate our architectural choices, and linear probing reveals that the network implicitly models the opponent's hidden hand from public interactions.

</details>


### [322] [CFAgentBench: A Reproducible Environment and Benchmark for Autonomous Construction-Finance Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22000)
*Rishi Srivastava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CFAgentBench是一个可复现、可自托管的自动化施工财务智能体基准测试环境，包含1,014个任务规范、35个模拟应用和9种原型，强调资金流动安全（278个任务要求停止并等待人工审批），实验表明最强模型pass@1=0.67但pass@5=0.38，单次准确率高估了实际部署能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准多基于静态轨迹或简化环境，缺乏针对施工财务领域完整软件栈（ERP、项目管理、支付申请、工资单、 lien豁免、银行门户等）的、可执行且可复现的评估平台，导致模型在真实部署中的能力被高估。

Method: 构建了包含1,014个机器可评分的任务规范（覆盖8个领域、77个族）、35个模拟应用（31个与同一公司账本对齐+4个PM平台）和9种原型的环境；采用WebArena风格的可执行环境而非静态轨迹，通过状态差异、禁止副作用检查和必要输出正则表达式进行功能正确性评分；特别设计资金流动安全机制（278个任务嵌入支付/工资单/电子签名/电子申报步骤，正确行为是停止并等待人工审批）。

Result: 最强模型（开源权重）在5次运行中pass@1=0.67但pass@5=0.38，pass@5较pass@1下降43%；存在明显的域间异质性。

Conclusion: 单次尝试的准确率显著高估了施工财务智能体在实际部署中的胜任能力，需采用多次运行评估和域特定分析来反映真实部署表现。

Abstract: We introduce CFAgentBench, a reproducible, self-hostable environment and benchmark for autonomous construction-finance agents: a CFO/controller-class agent operating across the real software stack a US construction finance team runs - ERP, project management, email, documents, pay applications, payroll, certified payroll, lien waivers, and bank/treasury portals. It contains 1,014 machine-gradeable task specifications across 8 domains and 77 families, every family grounded in a real source; a self-validated subset of 40 tasks (54 with a project-management extension) is compiled into oracle-validated executable evaluators, the runnable suite reported here. Following WebArena, the benchmark runs on an executable environment rather than static traces: 35 mock applications (31 reconciled to one company book, plus 4 PM platforms) over 9 archetypes, each implementing a uniform self-hostable app contract, so every task is graded by functional correctness - a state diff plus forbidden-side-effect checks plus required-output regexes - with an LLM judge used only for reply quality, never as reward. A distinguishing principle is a money-movement guard: 278 instances embed a payment, payroll, e-signature, or e-filing step where the correct behavior is to stop and stage for human approval, and executing even the correct transaction fails the task. The public split (n=711) is sized for a 95% Wilson half-width of +/-4.1%; a private, contamination-protected split (n=303) is reserved for remote scoring. In a first three-model open-weight sweep (k=5), the strongest agent reaches pass^1 = 0.67 but only pass^5 = 0.38 - losing 43% of its successes when required to repeat them under temperature-0 decoding. The within-model pass^1 to pass^5 collapse and sharp per-domain heterogeneity are clear evidence that single-attempt accuracy overstates deployable construction-finance competence.

</details>


### [323] [Nous: A Predictive World Model for Long-Term Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22030)
*Pranav Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Nous提出了一种基于"知识即预测"原则的新型智能体记忆架构，将记忆视为维护实体-属性的概率分布而非事实存储，通过信息论惊喜度评分和贝叶斯后验更新实现记忆。该架构在LoCoMo基准上以GPT-4o-mini为骨干，在单跳、多跳、时序和开放域四个维度上分别达到63.50、55.32、58.57和62.50的F1分数，超越A-MEM和BeliefMem，且无需外部向量数据库或图引擎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体记忆架构（数据库记录、向量嵌入、知识图谱三元组）将知识视为静态存储，难以动态适应新观测，且遗忘机制不自然。作者认为知识本质是预测而非存储，因此提出基于概率分布的预测性记忆模型。

Method: 1) 维护"维度"（dimensions）：每个实体-属性对对应一个分类概率分布；2) 新观测通过信息论惊喜度S = -log2 P(obs|D)评分；3) 使用封闭形式的贝叶斯后验更新分布；4) 存储"增量"（delta）即先验到后验的偏移，而非事实本身；5) 遗忘通过向均匀分布的熵衰减自然实现；6) 身份消歧通过实体维度集之间的互信息处理。

Result: 在LoCoMo基准上10个对话（1,540个问题）以GPT-4o-mini为骨干：单跳F1=63.50，多跳F1=55.32，时序F1=58.57，开放域F1=62.50。自我报告数据在三个类别上超过A-MEM，在所有四个类别上超过BeliefMem。

Conclusion: 基于概率预测的记忆范式是可行的，记忆应被视为动态的信念状态而非静态存储。同时指出了对比实验中的可重复性问题，展示了开放学术讨论的态度。

Abstract: We present Nous, a novel agent memory architecture grounded in the principle that knowledge is prediction, not storage. Rather than persisting facts as database records, vector embeddings, or knowledge-graph triples, Nous maintains a predictive world model: a collection of categorical probability distributions, called dimensions, one per entity-attribute pair observed in conversation. Each incoming observation is scored by its information-theoretic surprise S = -log2 P(obs | D), and the distribution is updated via a closed-form Bayesian posterior. The primary stored artifact is the delta, a record of the shift from prior to posterior belief, rather than the fact itself. Forgetting emerges naturally as entropy decay toward the uniform distribution, and identity resolution is handled through mutual information between entity dimension sets. Evaluated on the LoCoMo long-term conversational memory benchmark across ten conversations (1,540 questions) using GPT-4o-mini as backbone, Nous achieves F1 of 63.50 (single-hop), 55.32 (multi-hop), 58.57 (temporal), and 62.50 (open-domain). Against A-MEM's self-reported GPT-4o-mini numbers, Nous shows substantial gains in three of four categories, though we note that independent citations of A-MEM's results disagree with each other on category assignment, a reproducibility issue we discuss openly rather than resolve unilaterally. We additionally compare against BeliefMem, a concurrently developed system built on the same core premise of belief-based rather than deterministic memory; on the same benchmark and backbone, Nous's self-reported numbers exceed BeliefMem's self-reported numbers on all four categories, though we flag several uncontrolled differences between the two evaluation pipelines that prevent this from being a fully controlled comparison. Nous requires no external vector database or graph engine.

</details>


### [324] [Attractor Domain Theory: A Mathematical Framework for Cardiovascular Attractor Analysis with Wearable Photoplethysmography (PPG) Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22039)
*Timothy Oladunni,Farouk Ganiyu Adewumi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出吸引子域理论(ADT)，证明心血管吸引子重构信息可划分为三个互非冗余域：几何域G（伪影抑制）、遍历域S（稳定性估计）和变分域V（血流动力学推断），并验证了几何域在176,742个PPG片段上的有效性（AUC=0.757, NPV=0.966）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非线性心脏动力学方法（如Lyapunov指数、递归统计、样本熵）虽能重构吸引子，但缺乏对哪些吸引子属性捕获哪些心血管量的原理性解释，特征选择沦为搜索问题，负面结果无法解读。

Method: 提出Attractor Domain Theory (ADT)，证明重构吸引子信息可划分为三个互非冗余域：Geometry Domain（时延嵌入，原生能力：伪影抑制）、Ergodic Domain（渐近统计不变量，原生能力：稳定性估计）、Variational Domain（有限时间Lyapunov指数场，原生能力：血流动力学推断）。证明域充分性定理（吸引子信息的Parseval模拟），建立三域的必要性和充分性。通过SCSI框架在176,742个PPG片段上进行几何域验证。

Result: 几何域验证：AUC=0.757 [0.686-0.828]，NPV=0.966（校正三个系统性评估伪影后，净通胀+0.179）。消融实验确认C_NL为几何域主导成分（Delta AUC=-0.413），五个成分存在域内冗余。

Conclusion: ADT为心血管吸引子分析提供了原理性框架，将特征选择从搜索问题转化为理论指导的域选择问题，并通过大规模实验验证了几何域在伪影抑制方面的有效性。

Abstract: The cardiovascular system evolves along a bounded trajectory in physiological state space that converges to a compact geometric object: the cardiac attractor. A wearable photoplethysmograph (PPG) or electrocardiograph (ECG) observes a one-dimensional projection of this attractor; by Takens' embedding theorem, delay coordinates reconstruct its full geometry. Three decades of nonlinear cardiac dynamics have extracted Lyapunov exponents, recurrence statistics, and sample entropy from reconstructed attractors, yet no principled account exists of which attractor properties capture which cardiovascular quantities, or why, leaving feature selection as a search problem and negative results uninterpretable. We introduce Attractor Domain Theory (ADT), which proves that the reconstructed attractor's information partitions into three mutually non-redundant domains: the Geometry Domain G (delay embedding; native capability: artifact rejection), the Ergodic Domain S (asymptotic statistical invariants; native capability: stability estimation), and the Variational Domain V (finite-time Lyapunov exponent field; native capability: hemodynamic inference). We prove a Domain Sufficiency Theorem (the Parseval analog for attractor information) and establish that three domains are necessary and sufficient. Geometry Domain validation via the SCSI framework across 176,742 PPG segments from four datasets yields AUC = 0.757 [0.686-0.828] and NPV = 0.966 after correcting three systematic evaluation artifacts (+0.179 net inflation). Ablation confirms C_NL as the dominant Geometry Domain component (Delta AUC = -0.413) and intra-domain redundancy across five components.

</details>


### [325] [When Does a Video-Language Model Stop Watching? Reward Strength Controls the Formation and Reversal of Visual Shortcuts in Multimodal RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22043)
*Zekun Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多模态强化学习（RLVR）中视觉捷径（visual shortcut）的形成-逆转动力学，发现其具有突然onset、单调剂量-反应以及关键干预窗口等特性，并将视觉捷径崩溃重新定义为可控、时间依赖且非对称的过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有研究记录了多模态大语言模型（LVLM）在RLVR中可能忽略视觉信息而利用语言先验（即视觉捷径）的现象，但其如何形成、能否被逆转以及何时干预仍然未知。

Method: 将接地惩罚强度（lambda）作为控制旋钮，在训练时间轴上刻画视觉捷径的形成-逆转动力学，并在一个留出的分布外诊断集上进行评估。

Result: (i) 突然onset：捷径依赖在窄窗口内突然出现，且对不同随机种子鲁棒；(ii) 单调剂量-反应：增大lambda逐步抑制捷径，中等剂量下轨迹先形成后逆转，暴露出获得与移除之间的滞后（hysteresis）不对称性；(iii) 关键干预窗口：在onset前施加惩罚可阻止捷径形成，而合并后施加效果显著减弱。

Conclusion: 视觉捷径崩溃不是二元缺陷，而是一个可控、时间依赖且非对称的过程，对多模态RLVR中何时以及以多强力度进行正则化具有直接启示。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is increasingly applied to large vision-language models (LVLMs), yet outcome-only optimization can drive a model to stop attending to the video and instead exploit linguistic priors -- a failure we call a visual shortcut. While the existence of such perception bypass is by now documented, how it forms, whether it can be undone, and when intervention still helps remain open. We treat the strength of a grounding penalty, lambda, as a control knob and characterize the formation-reversal dynamics of visual shortcuts along the training time axis. On a held-out, out-of-distribution diagnostic set, we find: (i) a sharp onset -- shortcut reliance emerges abruptly over a narrow window of optimization steps and is robust across random seeds; (ii) a monotone dose-response -- increasing lambda progressively suppresses the shortcut, and at an intermediate dose the trajectory first forms and then reverses the shortcut, exposing a hysteresis-like asymmetry between acquiring and removing it; and (iii) a critical intervention window -- applying the penalty before onset arrests shortcut formation, whereas the same penalty applied after consolidation is markedly less effective. Together these results recast visual-shortcut collapse not as a binary defect but as a controllable, time-dependent, and asymmetric process, with direct implications for when and how strongly to regularize multimodal RLVR.

</details>


### [326] [Can Reasoning Models Detect Changes to their Chains of Thought?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22085)
*Sathvik Napa,Utkarsh Singh,Chengyuan Xue,Miriam Wanner,William Walden*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究推理模型是否能检测到对其思维链（CoT）的编辑干预，发现模型检测能力非常有限，且难以识别修改方式，对自己和他人的CoT检测能力相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了安全等目的需要编辑模型的思维链（如预填充更强模型的推理或移除不安全步骤），但这种干预的成功性取决于模型是否无法察觉篡改，因此需要研究模型对CoT编辑的检测能力。

Method: 在多种条件下测试推理模型是否能检测对其CoT的干预：推理过程中和之后，以及预填充自身CoT和其他模型CoT的情况。

Result: (i) 模型的检测准确率非常低；(ii) 模型难以识别CoT被修改的方式；(iii) 模型检测自身CoT变化的能力与检测其他模型CoT变化的能力相当。

Conclusion: 当前推理模型对CoT编辑的检测能力很弱，这为安全干预提供了可能性，但也意味着模型可能无法意识到潜在的不安全修改。

Abstract: There are many reasons one may want to edit a model's chain of thought (CoT) -- e.g., to prefill it with reasoning from a stronger model or to remove steps that may yield unsafe outputs. The success of these interventions plausibly depends on a model's inability to notice them, as the model may alter its behavior if it suspects tampering. In this work, we study whether recent reasoning models are able to detect such interventions on their CoTs under a variety of conditions: both during reasoning and after it, and when prefilled both with their own CoTs and with those of other models. Broadly, we find that (i) models exhibit only very modest detection accuracy; (ii) models struggle to identify *how* their CoT was modified; and (iii) models are about as good at detecting changes to their own CoTs as to those of other models.

</details>


### [327] [Geometry-Aware Online Scheduling for LLM Serving: From Theoretical Bound to System Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22327)
*Li Kong,Qi Qi,Yinyu Ye,Zijie Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出几何感知的在线调度算法SVF及其高效变体1-bit SVF，用于优化LLM推理中Key-Value缓存的动态内存管理。通过全新的证明方法将最坏情况竞争比从48收紧至5，并在vLLM中作为即插即用层实现，在Llama-3.1模型上显著降低平均和尾部延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理系统依赖基于时间的调度启发式算法（如SJF），但这些算法无法捕捉LLM推理中高度动态的二维时空几何增长特性，导致KV缓存的内存管理成为性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出Smallest Volume First (SVF)算法及其高效变体1-bit SVF。通过新的证明方法将竞争比从48收紧至5，并针对不同流量场景和信息可用性构建了完整的理论分类体系。在vLLM中作为即插即用层集成。

Result: SVF显著降低了平均和尾部延迟；1-bit SVF仅需单比特信息即可达到有竞争力的吞吐量和延迟。在Llama-3.1模型上进行了广泛评估。

Conclusion: 本文建立了理论上严谨且实证有效的方法，解决了现代LLM部署中的内存约束调度问题，代码已开源。

Abstract: The explosive demand for interactive Large Language Model serving has highlighted the management of the Key-Value cache's dynamic memory footprint as a critical area for performance optimization in inference engines. Modern inference systems overwhelmingly rely on time-centric scheduling heuristics, such as Shortest Job First. However, their theoretical optimality is rooted in traditional schedule modeling, failing to capture the highly dynamic, 2D spatio-temporal geometric growth specific to LLM inference mechanisms. To resolve this, we propose the geometry-aware online scheduling by introducing the Smallest Volume First (SVF) algorithm and its highly efficient variant, 1-bit SVF. Theoretically, we provide a rigorous mathematical foundation for our approach. Utilizing a novel proof methodology, we tighten the worst-case competitive ratio ($\text{CR} \le 48 \rightarrow \text{CR} \le 5$) for SVF with known output lengths. Building upon this core breakthrough, we complete a comprehensive theoretical taxonomy analyzing our algorithms across different traffic scenarios and information availability. Practically, we seamlessly integrate our approach as a plug-and-play layer in vLLM. Extensive evaluations on Llama-3.1 models demonstrate comprehensive performance gains: SVF delivers strong reductions in both average and tail latency, while 1-bit SVF, with merely a single bit information, achieves competitive throughput and latency. This work establishes a theoretically sound and empirically proven approach for resolving memory-constrained scheduling in modern LLM deployments. To facilitate future research, our code is available at https://github.com/Aurora-Kl/Geometry-Aware-Online-Scheduling.git.

</details>


### [328] [Hypothesis-Driven Skill Optimization for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22330)
*Fangxin Shang,Yehui Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HDSO提出了一种无需训练的框架，通过可证伪假设机制对冻结LLM智能体的技能进行安全策展与验证，在ALFWorld任务中提升+6.9 Avg. SR（Qwen3-8B），并能抵抗20%随机翻转的噪声反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏或噪声轨迹中蒸馏的外部技能可能包含虚假捷径或目标执行器无法可靠遵循的规则，现有方法缺乏对技能有效性的系统验证机制，存在不安全持久更新的风险。

Method: HDSO框架包含两个冻结推理端点：策展器观察执行器轨迹→提出可证伪假设及验证方案→实例化为候选技能包→通过配对控制/实验执行验证→审查行为差异→仅将受支持的候选技能纳入批准仓库；执行器通过渐进式披露消费技能，无技能选择时保留纯执行器路径。

Result: 在ALFWorld上，HDSO对Qwen3-8B提升+6.9 Avg. SR，对Qwen3.6-27B提升+4.0；在20%随机翻转成功/失败反馈下仍保持+7.1增益；跨模型策展仅在策展器诊断、执行器能力与验证证据一致时成功。

Conclusion: HDSO为冻结动作智能体提供了一个可审计的技能生命周期管理机制，而非无约束的记忆累积过程；验证后的技能库可在生成它的运行之外复用，但跨模型策展需要多方对齐。

Abstract: External skills can improve action-oriented LLM agents without changing model weights, but persistent skill updates are risky when they are distilled from sparse or noisy trajectories. A plausible reflection may encode a useful procedure, a spurious shortcut, or a rule that the target executor cannot reliably follow. We propose Hypothesis-Driven Skill Optimization (HDSO), a train-free framework in which both the skill curator and the agent executor are frozen inference endpoints. The curator observes executor traces, proposes a falsifiable hypothesis with an explicit validation plan, instantiates the hypothesis as a candidate skill package, validates the package through paired control/treatment executions, reviews behavior differences, and consolidates only supported candidates into an approved repository. The executor consumes approved skills through progressive disclosure, preserving the executor-only path when no skill is selected. On ALFWorld, HDSO improves executor-only baselines by +6.9 Avg. SR points for Qwen3-8B and +4.0 points for Qwen3.6-27B. Under 20% randomly flipped success/failure feedback during skill discovery and validation, HDSO preserves a +7.1-point gain for Qwen3-8B. Transfer and heterogeneous-pair diagnostics further show that validated repositories can be useful beyond the run that produced them, but cross-model curation succeeds only when curator diagnosis, executor capability, and validation evidence align. HDSO provides an auditable skill lifecycle for frozen action agents rather than an unconstrained memory accumulation procedure.

</details>


### [329] [Reference-Free Assessment of Physical Consistency in World Model-based Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22363)
*Yun Oh,Sukmin Yun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需参考的物理一致性评估方法，结合相对和绝对评估，利用DROID-SLAM和SEA-RAFT量化视频中的物理不一致性，可将任务成功率提升8%以上，并实现时空定位可视化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成评估方法（如Elo、FVD）需要昂贵的人工投票或无法获取的真实参考，且物理保真度差距导致生成环境无法准确复现VLA模型在真实世界中的任务成功率。

Method: 提出无参考的物理一致性评估指标，结合相对评估（基于WorldScore思想）和绝对评估，利用DROID-SLAM和SEA-RAFT检测物理不一致性，用于视频筛选和时空定位。

Result: 经相对一致性评估筛选后的视频，任务成功率提升超过8%，有效缩小了仿真到现实的差距；绝对评估可实现物理伪影的时空定位可视化。

Conclusion: 所提出的无参考物理一致性评估方法能有效提升生成视频的物理保真度，缩小仿真与现实差距，并提供伪影定位能力，优于需要昂贵标注或真实参考的现有方法。

Abstract: We introduce reference-free measures for evaluating the physical consistency of generated videos, combining relative and absolute approaches to assess fidelity. Although tools like WorldGym or WorldEval enable robotic simulation via video generation, physical fidelity gaps often prevent these environments from accurately reproducing real-world task success rates of VLA models. Unlike existing evaluation methods, which require costly human voting (Elo) or unavailable ground-truth references (FVD), our approach utilizes DROID-SLAM and SEA-RAFT to quantify physical inconsistencies, motivated by WorldScore. Videos filtered using our relative consistency assessment show an improvement in task success rates of over 8%, effectively narrowing the simulation-to-reality gap. Furthermore, our absolute assessment enables spatio-temporal localization, providing visualization of when and where physical artifacts occur.

</details>


### [330] [ARIA: A Causal-Aware Framework for Rescuing LLM Reasoning in Trustworthy Materials Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22375)
*Yi Cao,Liaoyaqi Wang,Jieneng Chen,Benjamin Van Durme,Alan Yuille,Paulette Clancy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ARIA是一种因果感知框架，通过三级级联（直接因果推理、物理启发的类比迁移、显式参数回退）解决材料发现中LLM增强知识图谱时的"上下文隧道"问题，在2D材料的前向预测和逆向设计任务中优于基线模型，并生成可审计的因果轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型在材料发现中常违背物理因果性，LLM结合知识图谱时会出现"上下文隧道"现象——模型过度锚定检索到的狭窄证据，抑制全局物理推理。

Method: ARIA框架采用三级级联路由：(i) 当完整的Process-Structure-Property (PSP)证据链可用时进行直接因果推理；(ii) 对于稀疏或新型材料系统执行物理信息类比迁移；(iii) 外部证据不完整时显式参数回退。构建包含2,839条PSP关系的知识图谱，在2D材料的前向预测和逆向设计任务上评估。

Result: ARIA缓解了上下文隧道现象，优于未增强和朴素KG增强的基线模型，在线文献搜索增强时获得进一步性能提升，并生成可审计的因果轨迹。

Conclusion: ARIA提供了物理上可信赖的AI辅助材料发现方法，通过因果感知的知识使用条件化实现了更可靠的预测。

Abstract: Generative models have revolutionized the process of materials discovery, yet they often fail to satisfy underlying physical causality. Through an analysis of Large Language Models (LLMs) augmented with knowledge graphs derived from current literature, we uncover a phenomenon termed contextual tunneling, where models "over-anchor" on narrow, retrieved evidence while suppressing global physical reasoning. To address this problem, we introduce ARIA, a causal-aware framework that conditions knowledge use on mechanistic completeness. ARIA routes each query through a three-tier cascade: (i) direct causal reasoning when complete evidence chains of Process-Structure-Property (PSP) are available, (ii) physics-informed analogical transfer for sparse or novel material systems, and (iii) explicit parametric fallback when external evidence is incomplete. As a proof of concept, we construct a Knowledge Graph (KG) containing 2,839 extracted PSP relations from peer-reviewed articles in the materials literature and evaluate ARIA on forward prediction and inverse design tasks for two-dimensional (2D) materials. ARIA mitigates contextual tunneling, improves over unaugmented and naive KG-augmented baselines, and provides further gains when an online literature search is used for evidence enrichment. Crucially, ARIA produces auditable causal traces, enabling physically grounded and trustworthy AI-assisted materials discovery.

</details>


### [331] [MetaPS: Adaptive Programmatic Strategy Selection for Market Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22385)
*Jiaxiang Chen,Aotian Luo,Zhouyi Zheng,Weiyi Huang,Chi Zhang,Zenglin Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MetaPS框架，通过模拟市场环境指导大型语言模型从预定义的程序化策略库中选择最优策略，而非直接生成市场操作。实验表明该方法在多股票交易和商品交换场景中优于固定策略、直接决策和API提示等方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一市场策略无法在所有市场条件下获胜，动量、均值回归、风险控制等策略在不同市场条件中表现各异。因此需要一种能够根据市场状态自适应选择策略的机制。

Method: 提出MetaPS框架：1）在模拟或回测市场中展开候选策略；2）识别特定状态下能带来更好未来结果的策略；3）将状态-策略对转化为监督微调数据；4）推理时仅观察当前市场状态和候选策略上下文，选择合适策略程序，由所选程序生成最终操作。

Result: 在0.8B到9B参数规模的模型上均有持续改进；优于固定策略基线、直接决策智能体和API提示的LLM智能体；紧凑微调模型在多个设置中甚至超过更强的API模型。

Conclusion: 市场模拟可以为学习自适应、可解释且可执行的策略选择提供可扩展且有目标导向的监督信号。

Abstract: No single market strategy always wins: momentum, mean reversion, risk control,and event-driven rules can each succeed or fail as market conditions change.Rather than asking large language models to directly generate market actions,we study an executable decision paradigm where an agent selects from a library of programmatic strategies, each implemented as a code module mapping market observations to actions.We propose \textbf{MetaPS}, a simulation-guided framework for adaptive programmatic strategy selection. MetaPS rolls out candidate strategies in simulated or backtested markets, identifies states where particular strategies lead to better future outcomes, and converts these state--strategy pairs into supervised fine-tuning data. During inference, the simulator is no longer queried: MetaPS observes only the current market state and candidate strategy context, selects a suitable strategy program, and the selected program produces the final action. Experiments on multi-stock trading and a controlled goods-exchange sandbox show that MetaPS consistently improves across model scales from 0.8B to 9B parameters. It outperforms fixed-strategy baselines, direct decision-making agents, and prompted API-based LLM agents; in several settings, compact fine-tuned models even surpass stronger API models. These results demonstrate that market simulations can provide scalable and targeted supervision for learning adaptive, interpretable, and executable strategy selection.

</details>


### [332] [PlanBench-XL: Evaluating Long-Horizon Planning of LLM Tool-Use Agents in Large-Scale Tool Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22388)
*Jiayu Liu,Qihan Lin,Cheng Qian,Rui Wang,Emre Can Acikgoz,Xiaocheng Yang,Jiateng Liu,Zhenhailong Wang,Xiusi Chen,Heng Ji,Dilek Hakkani-Tür*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PlanBench-XL是一个包含327个零售任务、1665个工具的交互式基准测试，用于评估LLM智能体在检索受限和工具失效条件下的长程规划能力。实验表明，GPT-5.4在无阻塞场景下准确率为51.90%，但在严重阻塞条件下降至11.36%，揭示了大规模工具环境中自适应规划的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试很少评估智能体在检索受限的工具可见性条件下的规划能力，缺乏对真实世界中工具生态大规模、动态变化特性的模拟。

Method: 构建了包含327个零售任务、1665个工具的PlanBench-XL基准测试，引入可选阻塞机制（缺失、失败、干扰工具函数），模拟真实世界的不确定性，要求智能体迭代检索可用工具并适应运行时路径中断。

Result: GPT-5.4在无阻塞设置下准确率51.90%，在最严重阻塞条件下降至11.36%；智能体在缺乏显式错误信号或需要更长替代工具路径时尤其脆弱。

Conclusion: PlanBench-XL可作为诊断智能体规划失败的测试平台，研究结果强调了在大型、不完美工具环境中进行长程任务鲁棒自适应规划的必要性。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly operate in large tool ecosystems, where real-world tasks require discovering relevant tools, inferring implicit sub-goals, and adapting to dynamic environments over long horizons. However, existing benchmarks rarely evaluate planning under retrieval-limited tool visibility. To address this gap, we introduce PlanBench-XL, an interactive benchmark of 327 retail tasks over 1,665 tools that tests whether agents can iteratively retrieve usable tools, invoke them to uncover intermediate evidence for subsequent calls toward the final goal. PlanBench-XL further features an optional blocking mechanism that simulates real-world unpredictability through missing, failing, or distracting tool functions, forcing agents to detect disrupted paths and adapt at runtime. Experiments on ten leading LLMs show that massive-tool planning remains challenging: while GPT-5.4 achieves 51.90% accuracy in block-free settings, it collapses to 11.36% under the most severe blocking condition. Further analysis shows that agents are especially vulnerable when failures lack explicit error signals or when recovery requires longer alternative tool-use paths. These results establish PlanBench-XL as a testbed for diagnosing agentic planning failures and highlight the need for robust adaptive planning in long-horizon tasks with large, imperfect tool environments.

</details>


### [333] [Code Isn't Memory: A Structural Codebase Index Inside a Coding Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22417)
*Ishaan Bhola,Adithyan Krishnan,Sravanth Kurmala,Mukunda NS*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在固定编码智能体框架和模型下，结构化的代码库索引不会增加成本，但能显著提升定位和修复性能，且每解决成本低于agentic grep基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前编码智能体广泛结合LLM与代码库检索，但检索实现差异巨大。需要回答的核心问题是：在固定框架和模型下，增加结构化代码库索引是否会改变成本或解决率。

Method: 在SWE-PolyBench Verified和SWE-bench Pro上，固定使用Claude Opus 4.7，设置三组对比实验：带索引的框架、不带索引的同一框架、agentic grep基线，每个任务三颗种子，在泄漏审计后的沙箱中运行。

Result: 带索引的框架在定位和解决率上均有统计显著的提升；无成本惩罚（每次调用成本不变），且每解决成本更低；在解决率和定位上未出现对agentic grep基线的退化。

Conclusion: 结构化代码库索引的部署问题不在于成本过高（其每解决成本低于agentic grep），而在于工作负载是否包含结构化排序能发挥优势的多文件变更场景。

Abstract: Coding agents now interleave LLMs with retrieval over the working repository, and retrieval implementations vary widely across deployed harnesses. Inside a fixed coding-agent harness on a fixed model, does adding a structural codebase index actually change cost or resolve? We ran three arms (the harness with the index, the same harness without it, and an agentic-grep comparator) on SWE-PolyBench Verified and SWE-bench Pro with Claude Opus 4.7 held fixed throughout, across three seeds, inside a leak-audited per-task sandbox. The within-harness ablation produces a large localization gain and a statistically separated resolve gain, with no cost penalty per cell and lower cost per solve. The cross-harness check shows that the index does not regress against an agentic-grep baseline on resolve or localization, again at no cost penalty. We release the per-cell exclusion ledger, the leak-audit script, the localization extractor, and the results database. The deployment question for a structural codebase index is thus not whether it is too expensive to run (across seeds, the index lands at a lower $/solved than agentic grep) but whether the workload includes multi-file changes where structural ranking pays off.

</details>


### [334] [SVGym (SciVerseGym): An Environment for Reinforcement Learning and Bayesian Optimization in Crystal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22425)
*Bin Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SciVerseGym是一个基于Gymnasium的晶体发现环境，将晶体设计建模为马尔可夫决策过程，支持多种编辑操作、可配置评估器和多种搜索算法，为闭环晶体发现提供开放、可复现、可扩展的测试平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习原子间势能已实现交互式材料发现的高效原子级评估，但闭环晶体搜索方法仍分散在不同的定制化流程中（编辑、弛豫、评分、约束和记录），缺乏统一、可复现的平台。

Method: 将晶体设计建模为马尔可夫决策过程，设计Gymnasium兼容环境。支持局部和全局动作（元素替换、晶格扰动、原子位移、空位创建、原子插入），可配置化学空间、结构池、观测方式（原子级/图结构）、自定义奖励、可选弛豫及稳定性/声子诊断。每一步执行编辑后用机器学习原子间势能或ASE兼容计算器评估，返回标准(obs, reward, terminated, truncated, info)元组。

Result: 构建了一个解耦智能体逻辑与材料基础设施的开放测试平台，支持强化学习、贝叶斯优化、进化搜索和语言智能体工作流等多种闭环晶体发现方法。

Conclusion: SciVerseGym通过解耦智能体逻辑与材料基础设施，提供了一个开放、可复现、可扩展的闭环晶体发现测试平台，代码已开源。

Abstract: Machine-learned interatomic potentials now enable efficient atomistic evaluation for interactive materials discovery, yet closed-loop crystal search methods remain fragmented across bespoke pipelines for editing, relaxation, scoring, constraints, and bookkeeping. We introduce SciVerseGym, a Gymnasium-compatible environment for sequential crystal discovery that frames crystal design as a Markov decision process. Agents observe an atomistic structure, apply chemically meaningful edits, and receive feedback from a configurable evaluator. SciVerseGym supports local and global actions, including elemental substitution, lattice perturbation, atomic displacement, vacancy creation, and atom insertion, along with configurable chemical spaces, structure pools, atomistic and graph-based observations, custom rewards, optional relaxation, and stability or phonon-related diagnostics. Each step applies an edit, evaluates the candidate using a machine-learned interatomic potential or any ASE-compatible calculator, and returns the standard (obs, reward, terminated, truncated, info) tuple. By decoupling agent logic from materials infrastructure, SciVerseGym provides an open, reproducible, and extensible testbed for reinforcement learning, Bayesian optimization, evolutionary search, and language-agent workflows in closed-loop crystal discovery. Code is available at: https://github.com/Bin-Cao/SciVerseGym.

</details>


### [335] [Efficient Multimodal Clinical Question Answering for Pulmonary Embolism Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22442)
*Xiangyuan Xue,Yang Yu,Yan Gao,Junyan Wang,Bin Chen,Lingyan Ruan,Ting Dang,Hong Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于包含23,248例CTPA检查的多模态PE数据集INSPECT，构建了利用高效多模态大语言模型进行肺栓塞诊断和预后评估的基准，发现Gemma4系列模型在CTPA+EHR联合输入下表现最佳，其中PE诊断任务优于预后任务（尤其是再入院预测），表明紧凑型多模态模型在早期PE风险检测方面具有巨大潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺栓塞（PE）是一种高风险心肺疾病，其管理需要及时诊断和可靠的临床风险评估。由于PE诊疗常规结合了CTPA影像、放射科解读和纵向EHR数据，为评估紧凑型多模态语言模型提供了临床上有意义的场景。

Method: 使用INSPECT多模态PE数据集（19402名患者的23248例CTPA研究），构建了8个诊断和预后任务的结构化临床问答问题。在CTPA仅、EHR仅、CTPA+EHR三种设置下，采用零样本和少样本提示，评估典型的高效多模态大语言模型。

Result: Gemma4 E4B和Gemma4 E2B在EHR可用时表现更强，尤其在CTPA+EHR输入下。任务层面分析显示，PE诊断性能高于预后任务，特别是再入院预测。

Conclusion: 紧凑型多模态模型在早期PE风险检测和解释方面具有巨大潜力。

Abstract: Pulmonary embolism (PE) is a high risk cardiopulmonary condition whose management requires both timely diagnosis and reliable assessment of future clinical risk. Because PE care routinely combines computed tomography pulmonary angiography (CTPA), radiology interpretation, and longitudinal electronic health record (EHR) evidence, it provides a clinically meaningful setting for evaluating compact multimodal language models. In this work, we build a benchmark using efficient multimodal large language models (MLLMs) on INSPECT, a multimodal PE dataset containing 23,248 CTPA studies from 19,402 patients. We formulate eight diagnostic and prognostic tasks as structured clinical question answering problems and evaluate on typical efficient MLLMs under CTPA-Only, EHR-Only, and CTPA+EHR settings with zero-shot and few-shot prompting. Results show that Gemma4 E4B and Gemma4 E2B perform more strongly when EHR evidence is available, especially under CTPA+EHR input. Task level analysis further shows that PE diagnosis achieves higher performance than prognostic tasks, particularly readmission prediction. These observations suggest that compact multimodal models have the great potential in early stage PE risk detection and explanation.

</details>


### [336] [A Differentiable Atari VCS:A Complex, Fully Known Ground Truth for Explainable AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22447)
*Andreas Maier,Siming Bayer,Patrick Krauss*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过将Atari 2600 VCS重新实现为端到端可微模拟器（jutari和jaxtari），解决了XAI中缺乏可验证"真实机制"的困境。该系统兼具真实复杂性与完全可观测性，为梯度可解释性方法提供了可验证的测试平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XAI面临根本困境：简单系统（决策树等）机制已知但无需解释；复杂系统（深度网络）需要解释但缺乏内部运作的真实基准（ground truth），导致解释可能看似合理却无法验证。本文旨在创造一种既真实复杂又完全可规范说明的研究对象，打破这一二分法。

Method: 将Atari 2600 VCS（真实计算机架构，深度强化学习的发源地）重新实现为两个独立且端到端可微的模拟器：Julia版jutari和JAX版jaxtari。将卡带ROM视为权重张量、RAM视为软磁带、控制流视为门控，证明可微（软）执行在任意有限温度下前向传递与原始（硬）执行逐位等价，同时暴露比特逻辑原本无梯度的代理梯度。

Result: 两个模拟器均通过xitari验证，支持全部64个ALE游戏，达到64/64字节一致RAM和64/64像素一致屏幕。JAX版本在单块商用GPU上实现批处理可微rollout，达到每秒数百万环境步。整个系统在29个日历日约137活跃小时内构建，大部分由编码智能体自主编写。

Conclusion: 论文构建并验证了基础平台，从理论和定性梯度研究两方面表明基于梯度的XAI在该系统上可行。两个端口的完整代码以MIT许可证开源。

Abstract: Explanation requires ground truth: to verify an account of a system we must know its inner functioning-just what is missing where explainable AI (XAI) is most needed. Systems we can study fall into two camps. Simple, procedural one-decision trees, rule lists, sparse linear models-have a known but trivial mechanism, so explaining them tests nothing; genuinely complex ones-deep networks, real-world tasks-need XAI but have no ground-truth inner functioning, so an explanation can be plausible, confident, and wrong with no way to tell. We remove this dichotomy with a study object both genuinely complex and fully specified-inspectable by construction-and, so gradient methods apply, fully differentiable. We reimplement the Atari 2600 Video Computer System (VCS)-a real computer architecture, and the cradle of deep reinforcement learning-as two independent end-to-end differentiable emulators in Julia (jutari) and JAX (jaxtari), each validated bit-for-bit against xitari. Both reproduce xitari on all 64 supported Arcade Learning Environment (ALE) games: 64/64 byte-identical RAM and 64/64 pixel-identical screens. Treating the cartridge ROM as a weight tensor, RAM as a soft tape, and control flow as gates, we prove the differentiable (soft) execution equals the original (hard) one bit-for-bit in the forward pass at any finite temperature, while exposing surrogate gradients where the bit logic has none. The JAX port also opens a GPU path: batched differentiable rollouts reach millions of environment-steps/s on one commodity GPU. The system was built in roughly 137 active hours over 29 calendar days, much of it written autonomously by coding agents. This paper builds and validates the foundation, showing-theoretically and in a qualitative gradient study-that gradient-based XAI on it is feasible. Both ports' full code is available under the MIT license at https://github.com/akmaier/UnderstandingVCS.

</details>


### [337] [Self-Evolving Cognitive Framework via Causal World Modeling for Embodied Scientific Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22449)
*Yi Yu,Tetsunari Inamura*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于因果世界模型的自进化认知框架，将具身智能从预测型世界模型转向认知型系统，通过因果发现、干预驱动推理和反事实推理实现认知的持续演化，并将具身交互重新诠释为因果假设生成与知识获取的认识论过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身世界模型主要优化预测目标，导致其在分布偏移下泛化能力有限，且难以系统性地推理未见情境和假设性干预。作者认为具身智能应超越预测型世界模型，转向能够通过与环境的交互持续构建和精炼内部因果表征的自进化认知系统。

Method: 提出一个三组件框架：(1) 因果世界建模——通过因果发现构建内部因果结构；(2) 干预驱动因果推理——基于干预和反事实推理进行假设检验；(3) 持续认知精炼——利用干预反馈和反事实分析不断修正和扩展因果模型。

Result: 该框架将具身交互从轨迹优化重新诠释为认识论过程（因果假设生成→干预驱动实验→持续知识获取），并提出了干预驱动的因果-认识论基准测试范式用于评估自进化具身科学智能。

Conclusion: 本文为从预测型智能向认识论型智能的转变提供了概念和理论基础，认为智能产生于通过与环境的交互持续构建、修正和精炼因果世界模型的过程。

Abstract: Current embodied world models are primarily optimized for predictive objectives, limiting their ability to generalize under distribution shifts and reason systematically about unseen situations and hypothetical interventions. We argue that embodied intelligence should move beyond predictive world modeling toward self-evolving cognitive systems that continually construct and refine internal causal representations through interaction with the environment. To this end, we propose a self-evolving cognitive framework via causal world modeling for embodied scientific intelligence, which integrates three complementary components: causal world modeling, intervention-driven causal reasoning, and continual cognitive refinement. The proposed framework continuously revises and expands its internal causal world model through causal discovery, intervention-driven feedback, and counterfactual reasoning, supporting continual cognitive refinement and enabling cognition itself to evolve over time. Furthermore, we reinterpret embodied interaction not merely as a means of trajectory optimization, but as an epistemic process for causal hypothesis generation, intervention-driven experimentation, and continual knowledge acquisition. This work provides a conceptual and theoretical foundation for a transition from predictive intelligence toward epistemic intelligence, in which intelligence emerges through the continual construction, revision, and refinement of causal world models via interaction with the environment. Accordingly, an intervention-driven causal-epistemic benchmarking paradigm is suggested for evaluating self-evolving embodied scientific intelligence.

</details>


### [338] [PRIME: Evaluating Prompt Resolution Under Incompatible Instructions in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22470)
*Tehreem Javed,Shumaim Fatimah,Masooma Bakhtiari,Gibrail Islam,Mehwish Fatima*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PRIME框架，系统评估大语言模型在冲突指令下的行为表现，发现冲突类型比模型规模对行为影响更大，揭示了多种失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令遵循基准将元指令孤立评估，无法揭示模型在处理冲突指令时的行为特性。

Method: 构建PRIME框架，在回应长度、输出格式和推理三个维度上生成校准冲突，使用确定性行为分类法对模型响应进行分类，在平衡分布和自然分布两种设置下评估五个开源指令微调LLM。

Result: 冲突类型对模型行为的影响比模型规模更显著，不同冲突类别下存在多种失败模式。

Conclusion: 开发冲突感知能力具有重要价值，仅通过孤立约束无法充分评估LLM的指令遵循能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often encounter conflicting prompts, although current instruction following benchmarks assess those meta-instructions in isolation, limiting the insights about how models process conflicting instructions. We introduce a framework \textit{PRIME}(\textit{Prompt Resolution under Incompatible Meta-Instructions Evaluation}) to analyze behavior of LLMs when provided with conflicting instructions. \textit{PRIME} purposefully produces calibrated conflicts across response length, output format, and reasoning; classifying model responses with a deterministic behavioral taxonomy. We are evaluating five instruction tuned open weight LLMs in two distinct settings, balanced and naturally distributed. The conclusion we reach upon analysis is that conflict type is more significant in affecting behavior than model scale, and various failure modes across different categories of conflict. Our findings emphasize the value of developing conflict awareness and suggest ability of LLM to follow instructions cannot be assessed through isolated constraints alone.

</details>


### [339] [VADAOrchestra: Neurosymbolic Orchestration of Adaptive Reasoning Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22485)
*Teodoro Baldazzi,Luigi Bellomarini,Andrea Coletta,Michela Iezzi,Carsten Maple,Alessandro Pesare,Emanuel Sallinger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VADAOrchestra是一个神经符号框架，结合LLM驱动的动态工作流编排与Datalog+/-符号推理引擎，在保持灵活性的同时提供可审计、可扩展且忠实于逻辑的决策过程，并在金融场景中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BPM系统缺乏运行时适应性，而基于LLM的智能体系统虽灵活但存在不透明、不可靠和扩展性差的问题。为融合两者的互补优势，需要一种既能动态适应执行流程、又能保证可审计性和可扩展性的混合框架。

Method: 提出VADAOrchestra框架，采用混合架构：(1) LLM编排器根据用户查询和数据源增量式规划并调整工作流；(2) 将工作流编码为Datalog+/-逻辑程序，谓词对应工具调用，规则表示领域依赖和按需合成的逻辑结构；(3) 所有逻辑推理任务由Datalog+/-符号引擎执行，实现推理过程可验证。

Result: 在真实金融用例上的评估表明，VADAOrchestra相比标准智能体架构在忠实性（faithfulness）、可扩展性（scalability）和可解释性（explainability）方面均表现更优。

Conclusion: 通过解耦高层编排与符号推理，VADAOrchestra成功融合了BPM的严谨性与LLM的灵活性，实现了可审计、可复现且可扩展的复杂推理，为神经符号方法在动态决策中的应用提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Decision-making in real-world settings rarely follows a fixed script. Instead, it unfolds as a dynamic reasoning process in which the appropriate course of action evolves as new context and data become available. Traditional Business Process Management systems provide rigor, determinism, and auditability, yet they generally struggle to adapt their execution at runtime. Conversely, agentic systems based on Large Language Models (LLMs) bring flexibility to decision-making, but they are inherently opaque, often unreliable, and suffer from significant scalability constraints when operating over large datasets. To combine these complementary paradigms, we introduce VADAOrchestra, a neurosymbolic framework that models complex workflows as evolving reasoning processes. The framework adopts a hybrid approach: given a user query and a collection of data sources, an LLM-based orchestrator incrementally plans and adapts the workflow. This is encoded as a logic program in a fragment of Datalog+/- where predicates correspond to tool invocations and rules represent both predefined domain dependencies and logic constructs synthesized on demand to manipulate intermediate results. All logical inference tasks are then executed by a state-of-the-art Datalog+/- symbolic engine. This approach provides a verifiable reasoning trace, supporting the auditability and reproducibility of the entire process. Furthermore, by decoupling high-level orchestration from symbolic inference, it addresses scalability concerns, enabling complex reasoning over large datasets through targeted data querying. We evaluate VADAOrchestra on real-world financial use cases, demonstrating faithfulness, scalability, and explainability compared to standard agentic architectures.

</details>


### [340] [SCOPE: Evolving Symbolic World for Planning in Open-Ended Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22488)
*Yundaichuan Zhan,Minghe Gao,Zhongqi Yue,Wendong Bu,Wenqiao Zhang,Guoming Wang,Jisheng Dang,Juncheng Li,Siliang Tang,Yueting Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SCOPE是一个自适应符号规划框架，通过符号执行模拟器和自适应符号记忆两大模块，在开放环境中迭代优化动作计划和完善符号世界表征，显著提升任务成功率与跨场景适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将视觉语言模型与经典规划器结合时，感知获取的符号表征在开放环境中往往不完整，导致规划性能次优。

Method: 提出SCOPE框架，包含两个协同模块：(1) 符号执行模拟器(SESim)，进行符号验证和实际执行，利用反馈优化计划并演化符号世界；(2) 自适应符号记忆(SASMem)，将反馈提炼为演化式符号知识，增强长期规划能力。

Result: 在开放环境实验中，SCOPE显著提升了符号世界的完整性、环境扰动下计划的成功率，以及跨任务的基础理解力和适应性。

Conclusion: SCOPE通过自我演化的符号世界和自适应的规划优化，有效解决了开放环境中符号表征不完整的问题，为复杂具身任务的长程规划提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Recent works have explored integrating Vision-Language Models (VLMs) with classical planners that rely on symbolic representations of planning problems to generate long-horizon plans for complex embodied tasks. However, in open-ended environments, these symbolic representations obtained from perception are often incomplete, leading to suboptimal performance. To address this, we introduce SCOPE, a self-adaptive symbolic planning framework that supports refining action plans and evolving the symbolic world, i.e., the symbolic representations of open-ended environments. SCOPE comprises two synergistic modules: a Symbolic Execution Simulator (SESim) that conducts symbolic validation and real execution of action plans, leveraging the feedback to refine the plans and evolve the symbolic world; and a Self-Adaptive Symbolic Memory (SASMem) that further distills feedback into evolving symbolic knowledge to enhance long-horizon planning and modeling of the symbolic world. Experiments in open-ended environments show that SCOPE significantly improves the completeness of the symbolic world, the success rate of plans under environment perturbations, and cross-task grounding and adaptability across diverse embodied scenarios.

</details>


### [341] [Deep Learning-Based Sign Language Recognition from Videos and Cross-Lingual Translation to Indian Vernaculars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22494)
*Chandranath Adak,Ramesh Nandipalli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段深度学习流水线，先用VideoMAE视频Transformer对印度手语视频片段进行13类英文标签分类（验证准确率78%），再用NLLB-200多语言翻译模型将预测的英文标签翻译为印地语、泰卢固语和孟加拉语，并构建了Streamlit演示系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为全球聋人和听障社区自动将手语视频翻译为自然语言文本的工具仍然有限，特别是对于低资源的印度语言，现有工作缺乏针对印度手语的视频到多语言文本的端到端解决方案。

Method: 两阶段流水线：(i) 使用在AI4Bharat印度手语视频语料库（13类、197片段、80-20划分）上微调的VideoMAE视频Transformer，输入16帧224×224均匀采样帧进行英文标签分类；(ii) 使用Meta AI的NLLB-200多语言翻译模型将预测的英文标签翻译为印地语、泰卢固语和孟加拉语。

Result: 微调后的VideoMAE模型在13类小规模数据集上达到99%训练准确率和78%验证准确率。通过混淆矩阵和分类报告分析发现主要失败模式为易混淆的形容词对（如ugly、deaf、blind、hat、dress）。

Conclusion: 该方法在印度手语小规模词汇分类和跨语言翻译方面展示了可行性，但存在标签集小、仅支持孤立词而非连续手语、对单人手语风格敏感、单词级机器翻译歧义等局限性，未来工作将向句子级生成和更大词汇量扩展。

Abstract: Sign language is a primary mode of communication for the global deaf and hard-of-hearing community, yet automated tools that recognize sign gestures from video and translate them into natural language text remain limited, particularly for low-resource Indian languages. We present a two-stage deep learning pipeline that (i) classifies short sign language video clips into English word labels using a fine-tuned VideoMAE video transformer, and (ii) translates the predicted English label into Hindi, Telugu, and Bengali using Meta AI's No Language Left Behind (NLLB-200) multilingual translation model. The classification model is fine-tuned on a 13-class subset of the AI4Bharat Indian Sign Language video corpus from IIT Madras, processing 16-frame clips sampled uniformly from each video at 224 x 224 resolution. Under a small-scale academic setting (13 classes, 197 clips, 80-20 split), the fine-tuned model reaches 99% training accuracy and 78% validation accuracy after 15 epochs. We provide a per-class breakdown via a confusion matrix and classification report, identify the dominant failure modes (confusable adjective pairs such as ugly, deaf, blind, hat, and dress), and describe a Streamlit-based inference demo that takes a user-uploaded video and returns the predicted English label alongside its Hindi, Telugu, and Bengali translations. We discuss the scope, limitations (small label set, isolated-word rather than continuous signing, single-signer style sensitivity, ambiguity of single-word machine translation), and directions for future work, including expanding to sentence-level generation and a larger vocabulary. Code is released to support reproducibility.

</details>


### [342] [Grounded Scaling: Why Agentic AI Needs Deterministic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22495)
*Liang Ding,Xintong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出环境确定性是制约长链智能体任务成功的关键因素，推导出确定性-效率边界、验证者-古德哈特底线和技能演化的收敛条件，并构建了供应确定性指数(SCI)、确定性成熟度模型(DMM)及可证伪的开放问题纲领。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AGI-to-ASI的讨论聚焦于算力增长与各类摩擦（数据墙、抽象障碍、具身瓶颈、多智能体信任）之间的竞赛，但忽略了环境确定性作为横跨所有四类摩擦的互补性约束维度。

Method: 形式化推导三个理论结果：确定性-效率边界（链式任务成功率与步数指数衰减）、验证者-古德哈特底线（不完美奖励下的飞轮上限）、环境侧技能演化收敛条件。在此基础上提出SCI（5个可测量属性）、DMM（5级成熟度模型）和OQ1-OQ5（可证伪开放问题）。

Result: 在每步确定性δ<1的环境中，k步链式任务成功率以δ^k衰减；不完美奖励导致验证者古德哈特化，限制飞轮效应上限；环境侧技能演化存在收敛条件，超越该条件需环境本身确定性提升。

Conclusion: 环境确定性是独立于算力和对齐的核心约束维度，对sim-to-real充分性、对齐充分性、AI即普通技术三种竞争立场均构成挑战，平台无关的确定性评估框架可用于预测和指导长期智能体系统的部署。

Abstract: Long-chain agent execution fails exponentially in environments designed for human tolerance: with per-step determinism $δ< 1$, $k$-step chain success degrades as $δ^k$. The AGI-to-ASI scaling debate (Genewein et al., 2026) has so far framed progress as a race between compute growth and a list of frictions (data wall, abstraction barrier, embodied bottleneck, multi-agent trust); we argue that environment determinism is a complementary binding axis cutting across all four, for the broad class of agentic AI tasks whose outcomes are verifiable economically, physically, or through multi-party settlement. Three formal results pin down the regime: a Determinism-Efficiency Bound on chain-task success, a Verifier-Goodharting Floor on flywheel ceilings under imperfect rewards, and a convergence condition for environment-side skill evolution. We operationalise the framework as a Supply Certainty Index (SCI) over five measurable properties, a five-level Determinism Maturity Model (DMM) as adoption ladder, and a falsifiable open-question programme (OQ1-OQ5) with explicit null results that would force retraction. The position is platform-agnostic. We engage three competing positions: sim-to-real sufficiency, alignment sufficiency, and AI-as-normal-technology.

</details>


### [343] [Imagine to Ensure Safety in Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22509)
*Gregory Gorbov,Artem Latyshev,Aleksandr I. Panov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合可学习世界模型和双层策略（高层策略生成安全子目标，低层策略通过世界模型想象轨迹减少不安全行为）的安全强化学习方法，在长时域任务中显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全强化学习方法在长时域任务中面临复合估计误差和探索能力受限的根本性局限，亟需一种能有效处理长时域安全探索问题的新方法。

Method: 结合可学习世界模型与双层策略：高层策略生成中间子目标，引导探索朝向安全区域；低层策略利用在世界模型中的想象轨迹，减少到达子目标过程中的不安全行为。

Result: 在高维动作空间的长时域导航和操作任务中，所提方法在成功率和约束满足方面显著优于现有安全强化学习基线，能在不同随机种子下持续满足预设安全预算。

Conclusion: 双层分层策略结合世界模型能有效解决长时域任务中的安全探索问题，已有方法无法有效应对这类复杂场景。

Abstract: This work investigates the safe exploration problem in reinforcement learning, where an agent must maximize cumulative performance while simultaneously satisfying safety constraints. This challenge becomes even more pronounced in long-horizon tasks, where existing safe methods face fundamental limitations due to compounding estimation errors and restricted exploration capabilities. To address this problem, we propose a method that combines a learnable world model with two complementary policies a high-level policy and a low-level policy to promote safety at both hierarchical levels. The high-level policy generates intermediate subgoals that bias exploration toward safe regions, while the low-level policy uses imagined rollouts in the learned world model to reduce unsafe behaviors when reaching these subgoals. The proposed method was evaluated on challenging long-horizon navigation and manipulation tasks with high-dimensional action spaces, where it significantly outperforms existing Safe RL baselines in both success rate and strong empirical constraint satisfaction, consistently meeting the prescribed safety budget across seeds, while prior approaches fail to effectively solve these complex long-horizon scenarios.

</details>


### [344] [Governance Decay: How Context Compaction Silently Erases Safety Constraints in Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22528)
*Shiyang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM代理的上下文管理（压缩/摘要/驱逐）是一个安全关键失败面：原本遵守的治理约束可能在上下文压缩后失效，导致违规行为。论文提出了Governance Decay概念、ConstraintRot基准、压缩驱逐攻击，以及Constraint Pinning缓解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM代理依赖上下文压缩来管理长会话的token预算，但这一层可能无意中移除安全治理约束，造成安全隐患。现有研究忽略了上下文管理作为安全关键接口的重要性。

Method: 构建ConstraintRot基准（包含长时序代理场景和确定性工具调用评分），在7个模型家族、1323个episode上测量压缩引起的违规率；设计Compaction-Eviction Attack（用对抗性内容引导摘要器忽略合法策略）；提出Constraint Pinning（将治理约束隔离在有损压缩之外的无训练缓解方法）。

Result: 策略完整时违规率0%，压缩后升至30%（某些模型达59%）；约束幸存时违规率0%，被丢弃时达38%；对抗性注入可击败所有被评估模型；Constraint Pinning将违规率恢复至0%。

Conclusion: 上下文管理应被视为LLM代理部署中的一级治理接口；Constraint Pinning是一种简单有效的缓解方案；需引起对代理系统中上下文压缩安全性的重视。

Abstract: Modern LLM agents increasingly rely on context compaction, summarization, or eviction to keep long-running sessions within a token budget. We show that this context-management layer is a safety-critical failure surface: in-context governance constraints that agents reliably obey while visible can be silently removed by compaction, causing the same agent to perform prohibited tool actions later in the session. We call this failure mode Governance Decay. We introduce ConstraintRot, a benchmark of long-horizon agent scenarios with deterministic tool-call grading, and measure compaction-induced violations across seven model families. Across 1,323 episodes, violation rises from 0% with the policy in full context to 30% after compaction, reaching 59% for some models; when the constraint survives the summary, violation remains 0%, but when it is dropped, violation reaches 38%. We further study a Compaction-Eviction Attack, in which adversarial in-context content biases the summarizer to omit a legitimate policy, and show that optimized injections defeat every evaluated model. Finally, we propose Constraint Pinning, a simple training-free mitigation that quarantines governance constraints from lossy compaction and restores violation to 0% in our benchmark. These results identify context management as a first-class governance surface for deployed LLM agents.

</details>


### [345] [MacAgentBench: Benchmarking AI Agents on Real-World macOS Desktop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22557)
*Yikun Fu,Bowen Fu,Zhenyu Wu,Shuang Cheng,Xiaowei Sun,Bowen Yang,Zehao Li,Yibo Zhao,Zichen Ding,Zhoumianze Liu,Shijie Wang,Biqing Qi,Bowen Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MacAgentBench 是一个针对 macOS 桌面自动化 agent 的综合性基准测试，包含 676 个任务、25 个应用，其中近 60% 涉及 GUI 和 CLI 混合交互。采用确定性规则评估和细粒度多检查点评分。实验表明，Claude Opus 4.6 + OpenClaw 达到 73.7% Pass@1，优势主要来自技能库而非框架设计；细粒度指标显示相似 Pass@1 的模型在子目标完成度上存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 macOS agent 基准测试缺乏对框架增强能力的评估，且采用二元评价，无法捕捉现代 CUA 利用的框架能力以及在长时间、多应用任务中的部分进展。

Method: 构建 MacAgentBench 基准，包含 676 个任务、25 个应用，近 60% 任务涉及 GUI+CLI 混合交互；采用确定性规则评估和细粒度多检查点评分，并为多应用任务标注能力标签。在三个框架、16 个模型上进行实验。

Result: 最佳配置（Claude Opus 4.6 + OpenClaw）取得 73.7% Pass@1；优势主要来自技能库而非框架设计。细粒度指标显示相似 Pass@1 的模型在子目标完成度上差异显著。

Conclusion: MacAgentBench 能更全面地评估 CUA 能力，揭示了技能库的关键作用以及传统 Pass@1 指标的局限性，细粒度评估有助于更精确地理解 agent 性能差异。

Abstract: Computer use agents (CUAs) have advanced rapidly in desktop automation, and a growing number of users deploy CUAs such as OpenClaw on Mac Mini for always-on automation. However, existing benchmarks, including those for macOS, evaluate agents without framework augmentation and rely on binary evaluation. As a result, they fail to capture both the framework capabilities leveraged by modern CUAs and the partial progress on long-horizon, multi-application tasks. We present MacAgentBench, a comprehensive macOS agent benchmark comprising 676 tasks across 25 applications, with nearly 60% involving both GUI and CLI interaction. The benchmark adopts deterministic rule-based evaluation and introduces fine-grained multi-checkpoint scoring with capability annotations for multi-application tasks. Experiments across three frameworks and 16 models show that the best configuration, Claude Opus 4.6 on OpenClaw, attains 73.7% Pass@1, while this advantage is primarily driven by the skill library rather than by framework design. Fine-grained metrics further reveal that models with similar Pass@1 can differ substantially in sub-goal completion. Our code and data are publicly available at https://github.com/JetAstra/MacAgentBench.

</details>


### [346] [Text2DSL: LLM-Based Code Generation for Domain-Specific Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22586)
*Alexander V. Kozachok,Alexander M. Nazimov,Shamil G. Magomedov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化定义了Text2DSL任务（从自然语言生成领域特定语言代码），构建了含4,204个验证对的PolkitBench数据集，并通过在两个MoE模型上的实验证明：向提示中加入结构化上下文（BNF语法、API规范、标识符词汇表）可将语法有效性提升至98.6-99.4%，结构有效性提升9.7-35.5个百分点，CodeBLEU提升60-95%，且效果跨模型规模一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DSL安全策略规则编写依赖高专业知识且易出错，而Text2DSL作为独立问题类别（区别于Text-to-SQL和通用代码生成）尚未被系统研究。

Method: 构建PolkitBench数据集（4,204个自然语言到Polkit规则的配对，经三级AST管道验证）；在GigaChat-10B-A1.8B和Nemotron-3-Nano-30B-A3B两个MoE模型上进行控制提示实验，对比有无结构化上下文（BNF语法、API规范、允许标识符词汇表）的效果。

Result: 提供结构化上下文后，语法有效性达98.6-99.4%，结构有效性提升+9.7至+35.5个百分点，CodeBLEU得分提升+60%至+95%。效果在两个不同规模和来源的模型上表现一致。

Conclusion: 对于Text2DSL类问题，在提示中注入形式化的目标语言规范是无需微调即可实现高质量生成的稳健促成因素。

Abstract: Domain-specific languages (DSLs) are widely used for managing operating system security policies, yet manually authoring rules in such languages demands high expertise and is error-prone. This paper formalises the task of automatic DSL code generation from natural language descriptions - Text2DSL - as a distinct problem class, separate from Text-to-SQL and general-purpose code generation. We introduce the PolkitBench dataset comprising 4,204 verified natural-language-to-Polkit-rule pairs, each validated through a three-level AST-based pipeline. Controlled prompt experiments on two MoE models of different scale and provenance - GigaChat-10B-A1.8B (1.8B active parameters) and Nemotron-3-Nano-30B-A3B (3B active) - demonstrate the critical role of structured context (BNF grammar, API specification, permitted identifier vocabulary) for LLM-based DSL code generation. Across both models, supplying context raises syntactic validity to 98.6-99.4%, structural validity by +9.7 to +35.5 pp, and the CodeBLEU score by +60% to +95%. The consistency of the effect across models of different scale and provenance indicates that, for the Text2DSL class of problems, injecting a formal target-language specification into the prompt context is a robust enabling factor for high-quality generation without model fine-tuning.

</details>


### [347] [PaperClaw: Harnessing Agents for Autonomous Research and Human-in-the-Loop Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22610)
*Weiwei Ye,Hangchen Liu,Dongyuan Li,Renhe Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PAPERCLAW是一个多智能体系统，能从研究领域自主完成从文献调研到论文写作的全流程，支持人机协作优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型已具备推理和工具使用能力，使得自动化研究过程成为现实目标。现有研究缺乏从零到完整论文的全流程自动化系统。

Method: 构建多智能体框架，包含文献策展、头脑风暴生成想法与预注册合同、基于"提出-测试-反思"循环的可停止假设图、论文写作模块，以及全生命周期记忆系统。核心研究助手既可自主驱动全流程，也支持人机协同。

Result: 通过LLM裁判评估，PAPERCLAW在完全自主模式和人类参与优化模式下均能产出高质量论文。

Conclusion: PAPERCLAW实现了从研究领域到完整论文的端到端自动化，提供了可暂停、可检查、可人工介入的灵活研究框架，且输出结果可验证、可溯源。

Abstract: Large language models have become capable reasoners and tool users that write and run code and search the literature, which makes automating the research process itself a realistic goal. We present PAPERCLAW, a harnessed multi-agent system that carries a project autonomously, from a field of study to a finished paper. PAPERCLAW curates a domain from a field's live literature, datasets, and code; brainstorms it into an idea with a pre-registered main-result contract; and drives a stoppable hypothesis map through an iterative propose, test, reflect loop that grows only from measured verdicts and halts once the evidence supports the idea, at which point it writes a venue-compliant paper. A full-lifecycle memory keeps each stage in a single living record, so a long run can be paused, inspected, and resumed without losing context. At the centre is an in-cycle research assistant with research tools and skills: it can drive the whole pipeline on its own, while the same interface lets a person step in at any stage, turning a first autonomous draft into a stronger paper through human-in-the-loop refinement. Throughout, PAPERCLAW keeps its output grounded and checkable, citing only references validated against open scholarly indexes and reporting results that genuinely ran. An evaluation with an LLM judge finds that PAPERCLAW produces strong papers both fully autonomously and with human-in-the-loop refinement.

</details>


### [348] [SkillAudit: From Fixed-Suite Benchmarking to Skill-Centered Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22613)
*Dexu Yu,Youhua Li,Zhaoyang Guan,Xianhao Lin,Jining Luan,Zihao Rao,Xuanqi Lan,Yang Ran,Bo Lan,Nai-Xin Zhai,Hanwen Du,Junchen Fu,Wenhao Deng,Yongxin Ni,Chunxiao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillAudit是一个端到端的技能中心评估框架，能够自动对任意智能体技能生成涵盖效用、效率/成本和安全的综合评估报告，通过对真实世界技能包的扫描发现超过7%的技能存在风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法依赖于固定任务套件，无法可靠判断技能是否值得部署，且容易混淆技能的边际贡献与骨干模型能力，也无法评估技能超出预期范围时的价值。

Method: SkillAudit以技能工件为中心，从技能包直接构建能力对齐的评估任务，在隔离沙箱环境中执行并收集证据，采用LLM自动检查生成可审计结果；提出基线比较原则衡量效用和效率/成本，引入静态语义分析结合动态运行时验证的两阶段检测范式评估安全风险。

Result: 扫描了涵盖23个职业类别的高排名真实世界技能包，发现超过7%的技能处于风险状态。

Conclusion: 固定任务套件的评估范式在技能市场扩张背景下已不再适用，SkillAudit提供了一种更可靠、多维度的技能评估方法，能够有效识别风险技能。

Abstract: Agent skills have become a practical way to extend large language model agents, but the growing skill ecosystem still lacks a reliable way to judge whether a skill is worth deploying. Existing evaluation methods remain largely anchored to fixed task suites, assessing skills through performance on predefined tasks and environments. As skill marketplaces expand, this paradigm becomes inadequate: fixed suites can conflate a skill's marginal contribution with backbone strength and miss its value when tasks fall outside the skill's intended scope. We introduce SkillAudit, an end-to-end framework for skill-centered assessment that takes an arbitrary agent skill as input and automatically generates a comprehensive, multi-dimensional evaluation report spanning utility, efficiency/cost, and safety. SkillAudit focuses on the skill artifact itself and constructs capability-aligned evaluation tasks directly from the skill package. The generated tasks are conducted in isolated sandbox environments to collect execution evidence, followed by automated checks with LLM-based judging to produce auditable results. To dissect the agent skills, we propose the baseline comparison principle to measure utility and efficiency/cost, and introduce a two-stage detection paradigm combining static semantic analysis with dynamic runtime verification to assess safety risks. After scanning top-ranked real-world skill packages spanning 23 occupational categories, we found that over 7% of skills are at risky status.

</details>


### [349] [Confident but Conflicted: Internal Uncertainty and Cognitive Dissonance Resolution in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22633)
*Weihong Qi,Kristina Lerman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型在面对与先前输出相矛盾的信息时（如用户反驳、检索文档或搜索结果）如何解决认知失调。通过沿信息来源权威性和证据质量两个维度设计说服实验，引入经济学启发的"信任弹性"（Trust Elasticity）指标衡量模型被说服的倾向。研究发现不同模型间信任弹性差异显著，而明显错误的陈述在所有模型中几乎不引发说服。在Qwen和Llama两个开放权重模型中，这种差异与互补的内部不确定性指标（Qwen的置信度校准偏差和Llama的内部不确定性变化）相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已从行为层面刻画了LLMs如何解决与自身先前输出相矛盾的认知失调，但其与模型内部不确定性的关联尚不清楚。本文旨在系统研究这一连接。

Method: 沿信息来源权威性和证据质量两个维度变化说服尝试，基于12个具有分层认知状态的健康科学声明进行实验。引入经济学启发的信任弹性（TE）指标，评估模型被冲突证据说服的难易程度，并分析其与内部不确定性指标（置信度校准偏差、内部不确定性变化）的关系。

Result: 四个LLM的信任弹性差异显著；明显错误的声明在所有模型中几乎不引发说服（TE接近零）；在Qwen和Llama中，信任弹性变化分别与置信度校准偏差和内部不确定性变化两种互补的内部不确定性指标相关。

Conclusion: 跨模型行为差异与可测量的内部属性相关联，提示未来可针对内部不确定性进行干预以影响模型的认知失调解决过程。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) frequently encounter inputs that disagree with their prior outputs, through user pushback, retrieved documents, or web search results. While the way they resolve such conflicts -- a process we frame as cognitive dissonance resolution -- has been characterized behaviorally, its connection to internal model uncertainty is not well understood. To study this systematically, we vary persuasion attempts along two dimensions, source authority and evidence quality, across 12 health-science claims of stratified epistemic status. Dissonance can be resolved through persuasion, backfire, or immunity. We introduce Trust Elasticity (TE), an econometrics-inspired measure of how readily a model is persuaded toward conflicting evidence. Across four LLMs, TE varies substantially, while clearly false claims elicit near-zero TE across all models. On two open-weight models, we further find that this variation is associated with two complementary internal uncertainty indicators, Confidence Miscalibration in Qwen and Internal Uncertainty Change in Llama. These results link cross-model behavioral variation to a measurable internal property and point to interventions targeting internal uncertainty as future work.

</details>


### [350] [AgentLens: Interpretable Safety Steering via Mechanistic Subspaces for Multi-Turn Coding Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22673)
*Weidi Luo,Qiming Zhang,Yihao Quan,Mingyu Jin,Jie Cai,Chaowei Xiao,Jingcheng Niu,Zhen Xiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentLens，一个基于机制可解释性的白盒防御框架，通过运行时监控步骤级隐藏状态和干预单层10维子空间，实现对多轮编码代理的安全检测与有害行为缓解，并在MAS基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM编码代理在多轮交互中存在安全与滥用风险，而外部护栏细粒度控制能力有限，机制可解释性方法多局限于单轮或越狱场景，无法捕捉多轮代理执行中的动态风险演化。

Method: 提出AgentLens白盒防御框架：1) 从步骤级隐藏状态中检测有害执行状态；2) 通过在单层内干预一个10维子空间来缓解不安全行为。同时构建MAS基准，包含194个任务的多轮执行轨迹及细粒度标注。

Result: AgentLens在安全检测任务上表现优异，展现了前瞻性风险预判能力，并显著减少了编码代理的有害行为。

Conclusion: AgentLens为将机制可解释性应用于动态LLM代理安全领域奠定了基础，证明了从模型内部进行运行时安全干预的有效性。

Abstract: Coding agents based on large language models (LLMs) demonstrate remarkable autonomous capabilities, but they also introduce significant safety and misuse risks during multi-turn interactions with external environments. Existing safety mechanisms mainly rely on external guardrails, which have a limited ability to perform fine-grained behavioral control during execution. Meanwhile, recent mechanistic interpretability methods for LLM safety are mostly confined to single-turn or jailbreak-style QA settings, limiting their ability to capture the evolving risk dynamics of multi-turn agent execution. In this paper, we investigate the safety of multi-turn coding agents from an internal perspective. We propose AgentLens (Mechanistic Subspace Intervention and Steering), a white-box defense framework that performs runtime safety detection and representation-level mitigation for coding agents. Unlike conventional agent guardrails, AgentLens detect harmful execution states from step-level hidden representations and mitigate unsafe behavior by intervening in a 10-dimensional subspace within a single layer. To support this research, we introduce the Mechanistic Agent Safety (MAS) benchmark, comprising comprehensively annotated multi-turn execution trajectories across 194 tasks using LLaMA-3.1-8B, Qwen-2.5-7B, and Gemma-2-9B.
